== @JER

[.book-title]
The Book of the Prophet Jeremiah

=== @1

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Call of Jeremiah

@ The words of Jeremiah the son of Hilkiah, of the priests that __were__ in Anathoth in the land of Benjamin:
@ To whom the word of the LORD came in the days of Josiah the son of Amon king of Judah, in the thirteenth year of his reign.
@ ##It came also##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:5–8>>] in the days of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah, unto the end of the eleventh year of Zedekiah the son of Josiah king of Judah, unto the carrying away of Jerusalem captive in the fifth month.

@ Then ##the word##note:x[<<Ezek 1:3; 3:16>>] of the LORD came unto me, saying,
@ ##Before I##note:x[<<Ps 71:5–6; Isa 49:1; Gal 1:15–16>>] formed thee in the belly I knew thee; and before thou camest forth out of the womb I sanctified thee, __and__ I ##ordained##note:tn[Heb. gave] thee a prophet unto the nations.
@ Then said I, ##Ah, Lord##note:x[<<Jer 32:17>>] GOD! behold, ##I cannot##note:x[<<Exod 4:10–16; 6:12>>] speak: for I __am__ a child.

@ ¶ But the LORD said unto me, Say not, I __am__ a child: ##for thou shalt##note:x[<<Num 22:20; Ezek 3:27; Mark 16:15–16>>] go to all that I shall send thee, and whatsoever I command thee thou shalt speak.
@ Be ##not afraid##note:x[<<Isa 51:7>>] of their faces: ##for I __am__##note:x[<<Josh 1:5, 9>>] with thee to deliver thee, saith the LORD.
@ Then the LORD put forth his hand, ##and touched##note:x[<<Exod 4:11–12; Luke 21:15>>] my mouth. And the LORD said unto me, ##Behold##note:x[<<Luke 12:12>>], I have put my words in thy mouth.
@ See, I have this day set thee over the nations and over the kingdoms, ##to root out##note:x[<<2 Cor 10:4–5>>], and to pull down, and to destroy, and to throw down, ##to build##note:x[<<Isa 44:26–28; Jer 31:28>>], and to plant.

@ ¶ Moreover the word of the LORD came unto me, saying, Jeremiah, ##what seest thou##note:x[<<Amos 7:8; 8:2; Zech 5:2>>]? And I said, I see a rod of an almond tree.
@ Then said the LORD unto me, Thou hast well seen: for ##I will##note:x[<<Ezek 12:22–23, 25, 28>>] hasten my word to perform it.
@ And the word of the LORD came unto me the second time, saying, What seest thou? And I said, I see a seething pot; and the face thereof __is__ toward the north.note:tn[__toward…__: Heb. from the face of the north]
@ Then the LORD said unto me, Out of the north an evil shall break forth upon all the inhabitants of the land.note:tn[__shall…__: Heb. shall be opened]
@ For, lo, ##I will call##note:x[<<Jer 25:9>>] all the families of the kingdoms of the north, saith the LORD; ##and they##note:x[<<Jer 43:10>>] shall come, and they shall set every one his throne at the entering of the gates of Jerusalem, ##and against##note:x[<<Jer 4:16>>] all the walls thereof round about, and against all the cities of Judah.
@ And I will utter my judgments against them touching all their wickedness, ##who have##note:x[<<Deut 28:20; Jer 19:4>>] forsaken me, ##and have##note:x[<<Jer 7:9>>] burned incense unto other gods, and worshipped the works of their own hands.

@ ¶ Thou therefore ##gird up##note:x[<<1 Kgs 18:46; Luke 12:35>>] thy loins, and arise, ##and speak##note:x[<<Jer 1:7>>] unto them all that I command thee: be not dismayed at their faces, lest I ##confound##note:tn[or, break to pieces] thee before them.
@ For, behold, ##I have##note:x[<<Isa 50:7; Jer 15:20; Ezek 3:8–9>>] made thee this day a defenced city, and an iron pillar, and brasen walls against the whole land, against the kings of Judah, against the princes thereof, against the priests thereof, and against the people of the land.
@ ##And they##note:x[<<Ps 129:2>>] shall fight against thee; but they shall not prevail against thee; ##for I __am__##note:x[<<Josh 1:9; Jer 1:8>>] with thee, saith the LORD, to deliver thee.

=== @2

[discrete.pericope]
==== Israel Has Forsaken God

@ Moreover ##the word##note:x[<<Jer 23:28>>] of the LORD came to me, saying,
@ Go and ##cry##note:x[<<Jer 11:6>>] in the ears of Jerusalem, saying, Thus saith the LORD; I remember ##thee##note:tn[or, for thy sake], ##the kindness##note:x[<<Ezek 16:8, 60>>] of thy youth, the love of thine espousals, when thou wentest after me in the wilderness, in a land __that was__ not sown.
@ note:x[<<Jas 1:18; Rev 14:4>>]Israel __was__ ##holiness##note:x[<<Deut 7:6>>] unto the LORD, __and__ the firstfruits of his increase: all that devour him shall offend; evil shall come upon them, saith the LORD.
@ Hear ye the word of the LORD, O house of Jacob, and all the families of the house of Israel:

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD, What iniquity have your fathers found in me, that they are gone far from me, and have ##walked##note:x[<<2 Kgs 17:15; Jer 10:14–15>>] after vanity, ##and are##note:x[<<Rom 1:21>>] become vain?
@ Neither said they, Where __is__ the LORD that ##brought us up##note:x[<<Isa 63:11–13>>] out of the land of Egypt, that ##led us##note:x[<<Deut 8:14–16; 32:10>>] through the wilderness, through a land of deserts and of pits, through a land of drought, and of the shadow of death, through a land that no man passed through, and where no man dwelt?
@ And I ##brought##note:x[<<Num 13:27; Deut 8:7–9>>] you into a plentiful country, to eat the fruit thereof and the goodness thereof; but when ye entered, ##ye defiled##note:x[<<Jer 16:18>>] my land, and made mine heritage an abomination.note:tn[__a plentiful…__: or, the land of Carmel]
@ The ##priests##note:x[<<Jer 5:31; Hos 4:6>>] said not, Where __is__ the LORD? and they that handle the law knew me not: the pastors also transgressed against me, and the prophets prophesied by Baal, and walked after __things that__ ##do not##note:x[<<Hab 2:18>>] profit.

@ ¶ Wherefore ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 2:35; Ezek 20:35–36>>] yet plead with you, saith the LORD, and ##with your##note:x[<<Exod 20:5>>] children’s children will I plead.
@ For pass ##over##note:tn[or, over to] ##the isles##note:x[<<Ps 120:5>>] of Chittim, and see; and send unto Kedar, and consider diligently, and see if there be such a thing.
@ Hath a nation changed __their__ gods, which __are__ yet ##no gods##note:x[<<Isa 37:19; Jer 16:20>>]? but my people have ##changed their glory##note:x[<<Rom 1:23>>] for __that which__ doth not profit.
@ ##Be astonished, O ye heavens, at this, and be horribly afraid, be ye very desolate, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Isa 1:2; Jer 6:19; 22:29>>]
@ For my people have committed two evils; they have forsaken me ##the fountain##note:x[<<Jer 17:13; John 4:14; 7:37>>] of living waters, __and__ hewed them out cisterns, broken cisterns, that can hold no water.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Consequence of Israel’s Sin

@ ¶ __Is__ ##Israel##note:x[<<Exod 4:22>>] a servant? __is__ he a homeborn __slave__? why is he ##spoiled##note:tn[Heb. become a spoil?]?
@ The ##young lions##note:x[<<Jer 50:17>>] roared upon him, __and__ ##yelled##note:tn[Heb. gave out their voice], and they made his land waste: ##his cities##note:x[<<Jer 4:7>>] are burned without inhabitant.
@ Also the children of ##Noph##note:x[<<Jer 46:14>>] and ##Tahapanes##note:x[<<Jer 43:7–9; 46:14>>] have broken the crown of thy head.note:tn[__have…__: or, feed on thy crown]
@ ##Hast thou##note:x[<<Jer 2:19; 4:18>>] not procured this unto thyself, ##in that##note:x[<<Jer 2:13>>] thou hast forsaken the LORD thy God, when he led thee by the way?
@ And now what hast thou to do in the way of Egypt, to drink the waters of ##Sihor##note:x[<<Josh 13:3>>]? or what hast thou to do in the way of Assyria, to drink the waters of the river?
@ ##Thine##note:x[<<Isa 3:9; Jer 2:17>>] own wickedness shall correct thee, and thy backslidings shall reprove thee: know therefore and see that __it is__ an evil __thing__ and ##bitter##note:x[<<Jer 4:18>>], that thou hast forsaken the LORD thy God, and that my fear __is__ not in thee, saith the Lord GOD of hosts.

@ ¶ ##For of##note:x[<<Lev 26:13>>] old time I have broken thy yoke, __and__ burst thy bands; and thou saidst, I will not ##transgress##note:tn[or, serve]; ##when upon##note:x[<<Deut 12:2>>] every high hill and under every green tree thou wanderest, ##playing##note:x[<<Deut 12:2>>] the harlot.
@ ##Yet I##note:x[<<Exod 15:17; John 15:1>>] had planted thee a noble vine, wholly a right seed: how then art thou turned ##into the degenerate##note:x[<<Isa 5:4>>] plant of a strange vine unto me?
@ For though thou wash thee with nitre, and take thee much soap, ##__yet__ thine iniquity##note:x[<<Job 14:17; Jer 17:1; Hos 13:12>>] is marked before me, saith the Lord GOD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Israel’s Unfaithfulness

[.parallel]
(<<Judges 2:10–15; Isaiah 43:22–28>>)

@ ##How canst##note:x[<<Prov 30:12>>] thou say, I am not polluted, I have not gone after Baalim? see thy way in the ##valley##note:x[<<Jer 7:31>>], know what thou hast done: __thou art__ a swift dromedary traversing her ways;note:tn[__thou art…__: or, O swift dromedary]
@ ##A wild ass##note:x[<<Jer 14:6>>] ##used##note:tn[Heb. taught] to the wilderness, __that__ snuffeth up the wind at her pleasure; in her occasion who can turn her away? all they that seek her will not weary themselves; in her month they shall find her.note:tn[__A wild…__: or, O wild ass, etc]note:tn[__her pleasure__: Heb. the desire of her heart]note:tn[__turn…__: or, reverse it?]
@ Withhold thy foot from being unshod, and thy throat from thirst: but thou saidst, ##There is no hope##note:x[<<Jer 18:12>>]: no; ##for I have##note:x[<<Jer 3:13>>] loved strangers, and ##after##note:x[<<Deut 32:16>>] them will I go.note:tn[__There…__: or, Is the case desperate?]
@ As the thief is ashamed when he is found, so is the house of Israel ashamed; they, their kings, their princes, and their priests, and their prophets,
@ Saying to a stock, Thou __art__ my father; and to a stone, Thou hast brought me forth: for they have turned __their__ back unto me, and not __their__ face: ##but in the time##note:x[<<Isa 26:16>>] of their trouble they will say, Arise, and save us.note:tn[__brought…__: or, begotten me]note:tn[__their back__: Heb. the hinder part of the neck]
@ ##But where##note:x[<<Deut 32:37; Judg 10:14>>] __are__ thy gods that thou hast made thee? let them arise, if they can save thee in the time of thy ##trouble##note:tn[Heb. evil]: for __according__ ##__to__ the number##note:x[<<Jer 11:13>>] of thy cities are thy gods, O Judah.

@ Wherefore will ye plead with me? ##ye all have##note:x[<<Jer 5:1; 6:13; Dan 9:11>>] transgressed against me, saith the LORD.
@ In vain have I smitten your children; they received no correction: ##your own sword##note:x[<<Neh 9:26; Acts 7:52; 1 Thess 2:15>>] hath devoured your prophets, like a destroying lion.

@ ¶ O generation, see ye the word of the LORD. Have I been a wilderness unto Israel? a land of darkness? wherefore say my people, We are lords; we will come no more unto thee?note:tn[__We are…__: Heb. We have dominion]
@ Can ##a maid##note:x[<<Jer 2:11>>] forget her ornaments, __or__ a bride her attire? ##yet my people##note:x[<<Ps 106:21; Jer 13:25>>] have forgotten me days without number.
@ Why trimmest thou thy way to seek love? therefore hast thou also taught the wicked ones thy ways.
@ ##Also##note:x[<<2 Kgs 21:16; 24:4; Jer 19:4>>] in thy skirts is found the blood of the souls of the poor innocents: I have not found it by secret search, but upon all these.note:tn[__secret…__: Heb. digging]
@ Yet thou sayest, ##Because##note:x[<<Prov 28:13>>] I am innocent, surely his anger shall turn from me. Behold, I will plead with thee, because thou sayest, I have not sinned.
@ Why ##gaddest##note:x[<<Jer 31:22; Hos 5:13>>] thou about so much to change thy way? thou also shalt be ashamed of Egypt, ##as thou wast##note:x[<<Hos 5:13>>] ashamed of Assyria.
@ Yea, thou shalt go forth from him, and ##thine hands##note:x[<<2 Sam 13:19>>] upon thine head: for the LORD hath rejected thy confidences, and thou shalt not prosper in them.

=== @3

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Wages of the Harlot

@ They say, ##If a man##note:x[<<Deut 24:1–4>>] put away his wife, and she go from him, and become another man’s, shall he return unto her again? shall not that land be greatly polluted? ##but thou hast##note:x[<<Jer 2:20; Ezek 16:26>>] played the harlot with many lovers; yet return again to me, saith the LORD.note:tn[__They say__: Heb. Saying]
@ ##Lift##note:x[<<Ezek 8:4–6>>] up thine eyes ##unto##note:x[<<Jer 2:20; Ezek 16:24–25>>] the high places, and see where thou hast not been lien with. In the ways hast thou sat for them, as the Arabian in the wilderness; and thou hast polluted the land with thy whoredoms and with thy wickedness.
@ Therefore ##the showers##note:x[<<Lev 26:19; Jer 14:4>>] have been withholden, and there hath been no ##latter rain##note:x[<<Jer 5:24>>]; and thou hadst a whore’s forehead, thou refusedst to be ashamed.
@ ##Wilt thou##note:x[<<Jer 3:19; 31:9>>] not from this time cry unto me, My father, thou __art__ ##the guide##note:x[<<Jer 2:2>>] of my youth?
@ Will he reserve __his anger__ for ever? will he keep __it__ to the end? Behold, thou hast spoken and done evil things as thou couldest.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judah Follows Israel’s Example

@ ¶ The LORD said also unto me in the days of Josiah the king, Hast thou seen __that__ which backsliding Israel hath done? she is gone up upon every high mountain and under every green tree, and there hath played the harlot.
@ And I said after she had done all these __things__, Turn thou unto me. But she returned not. And ##her treacherous##note:x[<<Ezek 16:46>>] sister Judah saw __it__.
@ And I saw, ##when for##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:9–11; Hos 2:2–3>>] all the causes whereby backsliding Israel committed adultery I had put her away, ##and given her##note:x[<<Deut 24:1>>] a bill of divorce; yet her treacherous sister Judah feared not, but went and played the harlot also.
@ And it came to pass through the ##lightness##note:tn[or, fame] of her whoredom, that ##she defiled##note:x[<<Jer 3:2>>] the land, and ##committed##note:x[<<Isa 57:6; Jer 2:27>>] adultery with stones and with stocks.
@ And yet for all this her treacherous sister ##Judah##note:x[<<Hos 7:14>>] hath not turned unto me with her whole heart, but ##feignedly##note:tn[Heb. in falsehood], saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Call to Repentance

[.parallel]
(<<Hosea 14:1–3; Zechariah 1:1–6>>)

@ And the LORD said unto me, ##The backsliding##note:x[<<Hos 11:7>>] Israel hath ##justified##note:x[<<Ezek 16:51–52; 23:11>>] herself more than treacherous Judah.

@ ¶ Go and proclaim these words toward the north, and say, ##Return##note:x[<<Ezek 33:11>>], thou backsliding Israel, saith the LORD; __and__ I will not cause mine anger to fall upon you: ##for I __am__##note:x[<<Deut 4:29–31; Ps 86:15>>] merciful, saith the LORD, __and__ I will not keep __anger__ for ever.
@ Only ##acknowledge##note:x[<<Deut 30:1–3; Jer 3:25>>] thine iniquity, that thou hast transgressed against the LORD thy God, ##and hast scattered##note:x[<<Jer 3:6>>] thy ways to the strangers under every green tree, and ye have not obeyed my voice, saith the LORD.
@ Turn, ##O backsliding##note:x[<<Jer 2:19>>] children, saith the LORD; ##for I am married##note:x[<<Jer 31:32; Hos 2:19–20>>] unto you: and I will take you one of a city, and two of a family, and I will bring you to Zion:
@ ##And I##note:x[<<Jer 23:4; Eph 4:11–12>>] will give you pastors according to mine heart, ##which shall##note:x[<<Acts 20:28>>] feed you with knowledge and understanding.
@ And it shall come to pass, ##when##note:x[<<Ezek 37:26; Amos 9:14–15>>] ye be multiplied and increased in the land, in those days, saith the LORD, they shall say no more, ##The ark##note:x[<<Isa 65:17>>] of the covenant of the LORD: neither shall it come to mind: neither shall they remember it; neither shall they visit __it;__ neither shall __that__ be done any more.note:tn[__come to mind__: Heb. come upon the heart]note:tn[__that…__: or, it be magnified]
@ At that time they shall call Jerusalem ##the throne##note:x[<<Jer 17:12; Ezek 43:7>>] of the LORD; ##and all the nations##note:x[<<Mic 4:1–5>>] shall be gathered unto it, to the name of the LORD, to Jerusalem: neither shall they walk any more after the ##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of their evil heart.
@ ##In##note:x[<<Jer 50:4; Hos 1:11>>] those days the house of Judah shall walk ##with##note:tn[or, to] the house of Israel, and they shall come together ##out##note:x[<<Jer 31:8>>] of the land of the north to the land that I have given for an inheritance unto your fathers.note:tn[__given…__: or, caused your fathers to possess]
@ But I said, How shall I ##put thee##note:x[<<Jer 3:4>>] among the children, and give thee a pleasant land, a goodly heritage of the hosts of nations? and I said, ##Thou shalt##note:x[<<Jer 3:4; Rom 8:15–17>>] call me, My father; and shalt not turn away from me.note:tn[__from me__: Heb. from after me]note:tn[__a goodly…__: Heb. an heritage of glory, or, beauty]note:tn[__pleasant…__: Heb. land of desire]

@ ¶ Surely __as__ a wife treacherously departeth from her ##husband##note:tn[Heb. friend], ##so have##note:x[<<Isa 48:8>>] ye dealt treacherously with me, O house of Israel, saith the LORD.
@ ##A voice##note:x[<<Jer 31:9; 2 Cor 7:10>>] was heard upon the high places, weeping __and__ supplications of the children of Israel: for they have perverted their way, ##__and__ they have##note:x[<<Jer 2:32>>] forgotten the LORD their God.
@ ##Return##note:x[<<Hos 14:1, 4>>], ye backsliding children, __and__ I will heal your backslidings. Behold, ##we##note:x[<<Zech 13:9>>] come unto thee; for thou __art__ the LORD our God.
@ Truly ##in vain##note:x[<<Ps 121:1–2>>] __is salvation hoped for__ from the hills, __and from__ the multitude of mountains: truly ##in the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 3:8; 121:1–2>>] our God __is__ the salvation of Israel.
@ ##For shame hath devoured the labour of our fathers from our youth; their flocks and their herds, their sons and their daughters.##note:x[<<Hos 9:10>>]
@ We ##lie down##note:x[<<Jer 2:26; Lam 5:16>>] in our shame, and our confusion covereth us: for we have sinned against the LORD our God, we and our fathers, from our youth even unto this day, ##and have not##note:x[<<Jer 22:21>>] obeyed the voice of the LORD our God.

=== @4

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Plea to Return

@ If thou note:x[<<Jer 3:12, 22>>]wilt ##return##note:x[<<Joel 2:12>>], O Israel, saith the LORD, return unto me: and if thou wilt put away thine abominations out of my sight, then shalt thou not remove.
@ And thou ##shalt swear##note:x[<<Isa 65:16>>], The LORD liveth, ##in truth##note:x[<<Jer 9:24>>], in judgment, and in righteousness; ##and the nations##note:x[<<Isa 65:16>>] shall bless themselves in him, and in him shall they glory.

@ ¶ For thus saith the LORD to the men of Judah and Jerusalem, ##Break##note:x[<<Hos 10:12; Luke 8:7, 14>>] up your fallow ground, and sow not among thorns.
@ Circumcise yourselves to the LORD, and ##take##note:x[<<Deut 10:16; 30:6; Rom 2:28–29>>] away the foreskins of your heart, ye men of Judah and inhabitants of Jerusalem: lest my fury come forth like fire, and burn that none can quench __it__, because of the evil of your doings.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Disaster from the North

@ Declare ye in Judah, and publish in Jerusalem; and say, ##Blow##note:x[<<Jer 6:1>>] ye the trumpet in the land: cry, gather together, and say, ##Assemble##note:x[<<Josh 10:20; Jer 8:14>>] yourselves, and let us go into the defenced cities.
@ Set up ##the standard##note:x[<<Jer 50:2; 51:27>>] toward Zion: ##retire##note:tn[or, strengthen], stay not: ##for I will##note:x[<<Jer 1:13–15>>] bring evil from the north, and a great ##destruction##note:tn[Heb. breaking].
@ The ##lion##note:x[<<Jer 5:6>>] is come up from his thicket, and the ##destroyer##note:x[<<Ezek 26:7–10>>] of the Gentiles is on his way; he is gone forth from his place ##to##note:x[<<Jer 2:15>>] make thy land desolate; __and__ thy cities shall be laid waste, without an inhabitant.
@ For this ##gird##note:x[<<Isa 22:12; Jer 6:26>>] you with sackcloth, lament and howl: for the fierce anger of the LORD is not turned back from us.
@ And it shall come to pass at that day, saith the LORD, __that__ the heart of the king shall perish, and the heart of the princes; and the priests shall be astonished, and the prophets shall wonder.
@ Then said I, Ah, Lord GOD! ##surely##note:x[<<2 Thess 2:9–12>>] thou hast greatly deceived this people and Jerusalem, saying, ##Ye shall have##note:x[<<Jer 5:12>>] peace; whereas the sword reacheth unto the soul.
@ At that time shall it be said to this people and to Jerusalem, A dry wind of the high places in the wilderness toward the daughter of my people, not to fan, nor to cleanse,
@ __Even__ ##a full wind from those##note:x[<<Jer 1:16>>] __places__ shall come unto me: now also will I give sentence against them.note:tn[__a full…__: or, a fuller wind than those]note:tn[__give…__: Heb. utter judgments]
@ ##Behold##note:x[<<Isa 19:1>>], he shall come up as clouds, and ##his chariots##note:x[<<Isa 5:28; 66:15>>] __shall be__ as a whirlwind: his horses are swifter than eagles. Woe unto us! for we are spoiled.
@ O Jerusalem, ##wash##note:x[<<Isa 55:7; Luke 11:39; Jas 4:8>>] thine heart from wickedness, that thou mayest be saved. How long shall thy vain thoughts lodge within thee?
@ For ##a voice##note:x[<<Jer 8:16>>] declareth from Dan, and publisheth affliction from mount Ephraim.
@ Make ye mention to the nations; behold, publish against Jerusalem, __that__ watchers come ##from##note:x[<<Jer 5:15>>] a far country, and ##give out##note:x[<<Ezek 21:22>>] their voice against the cities of Judah.
@ As keepers of a field, are they against her round about; ##because##note:x[<<Jer 5:23>>] she hath been rebellious against me, saith the LORD.
@ ##Thy way##note:x[<<Ps 107:17; Jer 2:17, 19>>] and thy doings have procured these __things__ unto thee; this __is__ thy wickedness, because it is bitter, because it reacheth unto thine heart.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Lamentation for Judah

@ ¶ ##My bowels##note:x[<<Isa 16:11; 22:4; Hab 3:16>>], my bowels! I am pained at my very heart; my heart maketh a noise in me; I cannot hold my peace, because thou hast heard, O my soul, the sound of the trumpet, the alarm of war.note:tn[__my very…__: Heb. the walls of my heart]
@ Destruction ##upon destruction##note:x[<<Ps 42:7; Ezek 7:25–26; Joel 1:15>>] is cried; for the whole land is spoiled: suddenly are my tents spoiled, __and__ my curtains in a moment.
@ How long shall I see the standard, __and__ hear the sound of the trumpet?
@ ##For my##note:x[<<Jer 5:21>>] people __is__ foolish, they have not known me; they __are__ sottish children, and they have none understanding: ##they __are__ wise##note:x[<<Rom 16:19; 1 Cor 14:20>>] to do evil, but to do good they have no knowledge.
@ I beheld the earth, and, lo, __it was__ without form, and void; and ##the heavens##note:x[<<Isa 13:10; Matt 24:29; Mark 13:24–25>>], and they __had__ no light.
@ I beheld the ##mountains##note:x[<<Ezek 38:20>>], and, lo, they trembled, and all the hills moved lightly.
@ I beheld, and, lo, ##__there was__ no man##note:x[<<Zeph 1:2–3>>], and all the birds of the heavens were fled.
@ I beheld, and, lo, ##the fruitful##note:x[<<Ps 107:34>>] place __was__ a wilderness, and all the cities thereof were broken down at the presence of the LORD, __and__ by his fierce anger.
@ For thus hath the LORD said, The whole land shall be desolate; ##yet##note:x[<<Jer 5:10, 18; 46:28>>] will I not make a full end.
@ For this shall ##the earth##note:x[<<Jer 12:4; Hos 4:3>>] mourn, and the heavens above be black: ##because##note:x[<<Num 23:19>>] I have spoken __it__, I have purposed __it__, and will not repent, neither will I turn back from it.
@ The whole city shall flee for the noise of the horsemen and bowmen; they shall go into thickets, and climb up upon the rocks: every city __shall be__ forsaken, and not a man dwell therein.
@ And __when__ thou __art__ spoiled, what wilt thou do? Though thou clothest thyself with crimson, though thou deckest thee with ornaments of gold, though thou rentest thy note:x[<<2 Kgs 9:30>>]##face##note:tn[Heb. eyes] with painting, ##in vain##note:x[<<Lam 1:2, 19>>] shalt thou make thyself fair; __thy__ lovers will despise thee, they will seek thy life.
@ For I have heard a voice as of a woman in travail, __and__ the anguish as of her that bringeth forth her first child, the voice of the daughter of Zion, __that__ bewaileth herself, __that__ ##spreadeth##note:x[<<Isa 1:15; Lam 1:17>>] her hands, __saying__, Woe __is__ me now! for my soul is wearied because of murderers.

=== @5

[discrete.pericope]
==== No One Is Just

@ ##Run ye##note:x[<<2 Chr 16:9>>] to and fro through the streets of Jerusalem, and see now, and know, and seek in the broad places thereof, ##if ye can##note:x[<<Ezek 22:30>>] find a man, ##if there##note:x[<<Gen 18:23–32>>] be __any__ that executeth judgment, that seeketh the truth; and I will pardon it.
@ And ##though##note:x[<<Jer 4:2; Titus 1:16>>] they say, The LORD liveth; surely they swear falsely.
@ O LORD, ##__are__ not thine##note:x[<<2 Chr 16:9>>] eyes upon the truth? ##thou hast stricken##note:x[<<Jer 2:30; 7:28>>] them, but they have not grieved; thou hast consumed them, __but__ they have refused to receive correction: they have made their faces harder than a rock; they have refused to return.
@ ##Therefore I said, Surely these __are__ poor; they are foolish: for they know not the way of the LORD, __nor__ the judgment of their God.##note:x[<<Jer 8:7; Hos 4:6>>]
@ I will get me unto the great men, and will speak unto them; for they have known the way of the LORD, __and__ the judgment of their God: but these have altogether broken the yoke, __and__ burst the bonds.
@ Wherefore ##a lion##note:x[<<Jer 4:7>>] out of the forest shall slay them, ##__and__ a wolf##note:x[<<Ezek 22:27; Hab 1:8>>] of the ##evenings##note:tn[or, deserts] shall spoil them, a leopard shall watch over their cities: every one that goeth out thence shall be torn in pieces: because their transgressions are many, __and__ their backslidings are increased.note:tn[__are increased__: Heb. are strong]

@ ¶ How shall I pardon thee for this? thy children have forsaken me, and ##sworn by##note:x[<<Josh 23:7>>] __them that are__ ##no gods##note:x[<<Jer 2:11; Gal 4:8>>]: when I had fed them to the full, they then committed adultery, and assembled themselves by troops in the harlots’ houses.
@ They were __as__ fed horses in the morning: ##every one##note:x[<<Jer 13:27>>] neighed after his neighbour’s wife.
@ Shall ##I not visit##note:x[<<Jer 5:29; 9:9>>] for these __things__? saith the LORD: and shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?

@ ¶ Go ye up upon her walls, and destroy; ##but make##note:x[<<Jer 4:27>>] not a full end: take away her battlements; for they __are__ not the LORD’s.
@ For ##the house##note:x[<<Jer 3:20>>] of Israel and the house of Judah have dealt very treacherously against me, saith the LORD.
@ They ##have belied##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:16>>] the LORD, and said, __It is__ not he; neither shall evil come upon us; neither shall we see sword nor famine:
@ And ##the prophets##note:x[<<Job 8:2; Jer 14:13, 15>>] shall become wind, and the word __is__ not in them: thus shall it be done unto them.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment Proclaimed

@ Wherefore thus saith the LORD God of hosts, Because ye speak this word, behold, ##I will make##note:x[<<Jer 1:9; 23:29; Hos 6:5>>] my words in thy mouth fire, and this people wood, and it shall devour them.
@ Lo, ##I will##note:x[<<Deut 28:49; Isa 5:26; Jer 4:16>>] bring a nation upon you from far, O house of Israel, saith the LORD: it __is__ a mighty nation, it __is__ an ancient nation, a nation whose language thou knowest not, neither understandest what they say.
@ ##Their quiver __is__ as an open sepulchre, they __are__ all mighty men.##note:x[<<Ps 5:9; Isa 5:28>>]
@ ##And they##note:x[<<Lev 26:16; Deut 28:33>>] shall eat up thine harvest, and thy bread, __which__ thy sons and thy daughters should eat: they shall eat up thy flocks and thine herds: they shall eat up thy vines and thy fig trees: they shall impoverish thy fenced cities, wherein thou trustedst, with the sword.
@ Nevertheless in those days, saith the LORD, ##I will not make##note:x[<<Jer 4:27; 5:10>>] a full end with you.

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass, when ye shall say, ##Wherefore##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:8–9; Jer 22:8–9>>] doeth the LORD our God all these __things__ unto us? then shalt thou answer them, ##Like as##note:x[<<Deut 28:47–48>>] ye have forsaken me, and served strange gods in your land, so shall ye serve strangers in a land __that is__ not yours.

@ Declare this in the house of Jacob, and publish it in Judah, saying,
@ Hear now this, ##O foolish##note:x[<<Isa 6:9–10; Ezek 12:2; Acts 28:26>>] people, and without ##understanding##note:tn[Heb. heart]; which have eyes, and see not; which have ears, and hear not:
@ Fear ye not me? saith the LORD: will ye not tremble at my presence, which have ##placed##note:x[<<Job 26:10; 38:10–11; Ps 104:9>>] the sand __for__ the bound of the sea by a perpetual decree, that it cannot pass it: and though the waves thereof toss themselves, yet can they not prevail; though they roar, yet can they not pass over it?
@ But this people hath ##a revolting##note:x[<<Jer 6:28>>] and a rebellious heart; they are revolted and gone.
@ Neither say they in their heart, Let us now fear the LORD our God, ##that giveth##note:x[<<Ps 147:8; Joel 2:23>>] rain, both the former and the latter, in his season: he reserveth unto us ##the appointed##note:x[<<Gen 8:22>>] weeks of the harvest.

@ ¶ ##Your iniquities have turned away these __things__, and your sins have withholden good __things__ from you.##note:x[<<Ps 107:17, 34; Isa 59:2>>]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ezek 22:2–12>>] among my people are found wicked __men:__ they ##lay wait##note:x[<<Ps 64:5; Prov 1:11>>], as he that setteth snares; they set a trap, they catch men.note:tn[__they lay…__: or, they pry as fowlers lie in wait]
@ As a ##cage##note:tn[or, coop] is full of birds, so __are__ their houses full of deceit: therefore they are become great, and waxen rich.
@ They are ##waxen##note:x[<<Deut 32:15; Ps 73:12>>] fat, they shine: yea, they overpass the deeds of the wicked: they ##judge##note:x[<<Isa 1:23>>] not the cause, the cause of the fatherless, yet they prosper; and the right of the needy do they not judge.
@ ##Shall I not visit for these __things__? saith the LORD: shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?##note:x[<<Jer 5:9; Mal 3:5>>]

@ ¶ ##A wonderful and horrible thing##note:x[<<Jer 23:14; Hos 6:10>>] is committed in the land;note:tn[__A wonderful…__: or, Astonishment and filthiness]
@ The ##prophets##note:x[<<Jer 14:14; Ezek 13:6>>] prophesy falsely, and the priests bear rule by their means; and ##my people##note:x[<<Mic 2:11>>] love __to have it__ so: and what will ye do in the end thereof?note:tn[__bear…__: or, take into their hands]

=== @6

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jerusalem’s Final Warning

@ note:x[<<Neh 3:14>>]O ye children of Benjamin, gather yourselves to flee out of the midst of Jerusalem, and blow the trumpet in Tekoa, and set up a sign of fire in Beth-haccerem: for evil appeareth out of the north, and great destruction.
@ I have likened the ##daughter##note:x[<<Jer 4:31>>] of Zion to a ##comely##note:tn[or, dwelling at home] and delicate __woman__.
@ The shepherds with their flocks shall come unto her; ##they shall##note:x[<<Luke 19:43>>] pitch __their__ tents against her round about; they shall feed every one in his place.
@ ##Prepare##note:x[<<Joel 3:9>>] ye war against her; arise, and let us go up ##at noon##note:x[<<Jer 15:8; Zeph 2:4>>]. Woe unto us! for the day goeth away, for the shadows of the evening are stretched out.
@ Arise, and let us go by night, and let us destroy her palaces.

@ ¶ For thus hath the LORD of hosts said, Hew ye down trees, and cast a mount against Jerusalem: this __is__ the city to be visited; she __is__ wholly oppression in the midst of her.note:tn[__cast…__: or, pour out the engine of shot]
@ As a fountain casteth out her waters, so she casteth out her wickedness: ##violence##note:x[<<Jer 20:8; Ezek 7:11, 23>>] and spoil is heard in her; before me continually __is__ grief and wounds.
@ ##Be thou##note:x[<<Jer 17:23>>] instructed, O Jerusalem, ##lest##note:x[<<Ezek 23:18; Hos 9:12>>] my soul ##depart##note:tn[Heb. be loosed, or, disjointed] from thee; lest I make thee desolate, a land not inhabited.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, They shall throughly glean the remnant of Israel as a vine: turn back thine hand as a grapegatherer into the baskets.
@ To whom shall I speak, and ##give##note:x[<<Heb 11:7>>] warning, that they may hear? behold, ##their ear##note:x[<<Jer 7:26; Acts 7:51>>] __is__ uncircumcised, and they cannot hearken: behold, the word of the LORD is unto them a reproach; they have no delight in it.
@ ##Therefore##note:x[<<Jer 20:9>>] I am full of the fury of the LORD; I am weary with holding in: ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 9:21>>] pour it out upon the children abroad, and upon the assembly of young men together: for even the husband with the wife shall be taken, the aged with __him that is__ full of days.
@ ##And their##note:x[<<Jer 8:10>>] houses shall be turned unto others, __with their__ fields and wives together: for I will stretch out my hand upon the inhabitants of the land, saith the LORD.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Jer 8:10; 23:11>>] from the least of them even unto the greatest of them every one __is__ given to covetousness; ##and##note:x[<<Jer 23:11>>] from the prophet even unto the priest every one dealeth falsely.
@ They have ##healed##note:x[<<Ezek 13:10>>] also the ##hurt##note:tn[Heb. bruise, or, breach] __of the daughter__ of my people slightly, saying, ##Peace##note:x[<<Jer 4:10; 23:17>>], peace; when __there is__ no peace.
@ ##Were##note:x[<<Jer 3:3; 8:12>>] they ashamed when they had committed abomination? nay, they were not at all ashamed, neither could they blush: ##therefore##note:x[<<Matt 15:14>>] they shall fall among them that fall: at the time __that__ I visit them they shall be cast down, saith the LORD.
@ Thus saith the LORD, ##Stand##note:x[<<Deut 32:7; Jer 18:15>>] ye in the ways, and see, and ask for the old paths, where __is__ the good way, ##and walk##note:x[<<Isa 30:21>>] therein, and ye shall find rest for your souls. But they said, We will not walk __therein__.
@ Also ##I##note:x[<<Isa 21:11; Jer 25:4>>] set watchmen over you, __saying__, ##Hearken##note:x[<<Isa 58:1>>] to the sound of the trumpet. But they said, We will not hearken.

@ ¶ Therefore hear, ye nations, and know, O congregation, what __is__ among them.
@ Hear, ##O earth##note:x[<<Isa 1:2>>]: behold, I will bring evil upon this people, ##__even__##note:x[<<Jer 17:10; Acts 8:22>>] the fruit of their thoughts, because they have not hearkened unto my words, nor to my law, but rejected it.
@ ##To what##note:x[<<Isa 1:11; Amos 5:21–22; Mic 6:6–8>>] purpose cometh there to me incense from Sheba, and the sweet cane from a far country? your burnt offerings __are__ not acceptable, nor your sacrifices sweet unto me.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD, Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 8:14; Jer 13:16; Ezek 3:20>>] lay stumblingblocks before this people, and the fathers and the sons together shall fall upon them; the neighbour and his friend shall perish.

[discrete.pericope]
==== An Invasion from the North

@ Thus saith the LORD, Behold, ##a people##note:x[<<Jer 10:22; 50:41–43>>] cometh from the north country, and a great nation shall be raised from the sides of the earth.
@ ##They shall##note:x[<<Isa 13:18>>] lay hold on bow and spear; they __are__ ##cruel##note:x[<<Isa 13:18>>], and have no mercy; their voice roareth like the sea; and they ride upon horses, set in array as men for war against thee, O daughter of Zion.
@ We have heard the fame thereof: our hands wax feeble: ##anguish##note:x[<<Jer 4:31; 49:24; 50:43>>] hath taken hold of us, __and__ pain, as of a woman in travail.
@ Go not forth into the field, nor walk by the way; for ##the sword##note:x[<<Jer 49:29>>] of the enemy __and__ fear __is__ on every side.

@ ¶ O daughter of my people, ##gird##note:x[<<Jer 4:8>>] __thee__ with sackcloth, and wallow thyself in ashes: ##make thee##note:x[<<Zech 12:10>>] mourning, __as for__ an only son, most bitter lamentation: for the spoiler shall suddenly come upon us.
@ ##I have set thee __for__ a tower __and__ a fortress among my people, that thou mayest know and try their way.##note:x[<<Jer 1:18; 15:20>>]
@ They __are__ ##all grievous##note:x[<<Jer 5:23>>] revolters, ##walking##note:x[<<Jer 9:4>>] with slanders: __they are__ brass and iron; they __are__ all corrupters.
@ The bellows are burned, the lead is consumed of the fire; the founder melteth in vain: for the wicked are not plucked away.
@ ##Reprobate silver##note:x[<<Ps 119:119; Isa 1:22>>] shall __men__ call them, because ##the LORD##note:x[<<Hos 9:17>>] hath rejected them.note:tn[__Reprobate…__: or, Refuse silver]

=== @7

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Message at the Temple Gate

@ The word that came to Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,
@ ##Stand##note:x[<<Jer 26:2>>] in the gate of the LORD’s house, and proclaim there this word, and say, ##Hear##note:x[<<Rev 3:22>>] the word of the LORD, all __ye of__ Judah, ##that enter##note:x[<<Rev 3:22>>] in at these gates to worship the LORD.
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, ##Amend##note:x[<<Prov 28:13; Jer 18:11; 26:13>>] your ways and your doings, and I will cause you to dwell in this place.
@ ##Trust##note:x[<<Matt 3:9–10>>] ye not in lying words, saying, ##The temple##note:x[<<Mic 3:11; Zeph 3:11>>] of the LORD, The temple of the LORD, The temple of the LORD, __are__ these.
@ For if ye throughly amend your ways and your doings; if ye throughly execute judgment between a man and his neighbour;
@ __If__ ye ##oppress##note:x[<<Exod 22:21–24; Zech 7:9–12>>] not the stranger, the fatherless, and the widow, and shed not innocent blood in this place, ##neither walk##note:x[<<Jer 13:10>>] after other gods to your hurt:
@ Then ##will I##note:x[<<Jer 18:7–8; 25:5>>] cause you to dwell in this place, ##in the land##note:x[<<Deut 4:40>>] that I gave to your fathers, for ever and ever.

@ ¶ Behold, ##ye trust##note:x[<<Isa 28:15>>] in lying words, that cannot profit.
@ Will ye ##steal##note:x[<<Jas 4:1–4>>], murder, and commit adultery, and swear falsely, and burn incense unto Baal, ##and walk##note:x[<<Exod 20:3; Jer 7:6>>] after other gods whom ye know not;
@ And ##come##note:x[<<Ezek 23:37, 39>>] and stand before me in this house, ##which is called##note:x[<<Jer 32:34>>] by my name, and say, We are delivered to do all these abominations?note:tn[__which…__: Heb. whereupon my name is called]
@ Is ##this##note:x[<<Isa 56:7; Matt 21:13; Luke 19:45–46>>] house, which is called by my name, become a den of robbers in your eyes? Behold, even I have seen __it__, saith the LORD.
@ But go ye now unto ##my place##note:x[<<Josh 18:1>>] which __was__ in Shiloh, where I set my name at the first, ##and see##note:x[<<1 Sam 4:10–11; Jer 26:6>>] what I did to it for the wickedness of my people Israel.
@ And now, because ye have done all these works, saith the LORD, ##and I spake##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:15–16; Jer 7:25>>] unto you, rising up early and speaking, but ye heard not; ##and I called##note:x[<<Isa 65:12>>] you, but ye answered not;
@ Therefore will I do unto __this__ house, which is called by my name, ##wherein##note:x[<<Jer 7:4>>] ye trust, and unto the place which I gave to you and to your fathers, ##as##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:9; Ps 78:60>>] I have done to Shiloh.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 15:1>>] cast you out of my sight, as I have cast out all your brethren, __even__ the whole seed of Ephraim.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judah’s Idolatry Persists

@ Therefore ##pray##note:x[<<Jer 11:14; 14:11–12; 15:1>>] not thou for this people, neither lift up cry nor prayer for them, neither make intercession to me: for I will not hear thee.

@ ¶ ##Seest thou not what they do in the cities of Judah and in the streets of Jerusalem?##note:x[<<Ezek 8:6–18; 14:23>>]
@ The ##children##note:x[<<Jer 44:17–19>>] gather wood, and the fathers kindle the fire, and the women knead __their__ dough, to make cakes to the queen of heaven, and ##to pour##note:x[<<Jer 19:13>>] out drink offerings unto other gods, that they may provoke me to anger.note:tn[__queen…__: or, frame, or, workmanship of heaven]
@ Do they provoke me to anger? saith the LORD: __do they__ not __provoke__ themselves to the confusion of their own faces?
@ ##Therefore thus saith the Lord GOD; Behold, mine anger and my fury shall be poured out upon this place, upon man, and upon beast, and upon the trees of the field, and upon the fruit of the ground; and it shall burn,##note:x[<<Isa 42:25; Jer 42:18; Lam 2:3–5>>] and shall not be quenched.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Put##note:x[<<Jer 6:20; Hos 8:13>>] your burnt offerings unto your sacrifices, and eat flesh.
@ For I spake not unto your fathers, ##nor##note:x[<<1 Sam 15:22; Ps 51:16–17; Hos 6:6>>] commanded them in the day that I brought them out of the land of Egypt, ##concerning##note:tn[Heb. concerning the matter of] burnt offerings or sacrifices:
@ But this thing commanded I them, saying, ##Obey##note:x[<<Exod 15:26; Deut 5:33>>] my voice, and I will be your God, and ye shall be my people: and walk ye in all the ways that I have commanded you, ##that it##note:x[<<Deut 5:33>>] may be well unto you.
@ But ##they##note:x[<<Ps 81:11–12; Jer 7:26>>] hearkened not, nor inclined their ear, but ##walked##note:x[<<Deut 29:19>>] in the counsels __and__ in the ##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of their evil heart, and ##went##note:tn[Heb. were] backward, and not forward.
@ Since ##the day##note:x[<<Neh 9:26>>] that your fathers came forth out of the land of Egypt unto this day I have even ##sent##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:15; Jer 25:4>>] unto you all my servants the prophets, daily rising up early and sending __them:__
@ Yet they hearkened not unto me, nor inclined their ear, ##but##note:x[<<2 Chr 30:8>>] hardened their neck: ##they did##note:x[<<Jer 16:12>>] worse than their fathers.
@ Therefore thou shalt speak all these words unto them; but they will not hearken to thee: thou shalt also call unto them; but they will not answer thee.
@ But thou shalt say unto them, This __is__ a nation that obeyeth not the voice of the LORD their God, nor receiveth ##correction##note:tn[or, instruction]: ##truth##note:x[<<Isa 59:14–15>>] is perished, and is cut off from their mouth.

@ ¶ ##Cut##note:x[<<Job 1:20; Mic 1:16>>] off thine hair, __O Jerusalem__, and cast __it__ away, and take up a lamentation on high places; ##for##note:x[<<Jer 6:30>>] the LORD hath rejected and forsaken the generation of his wrath.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Valley of Slaughter

@ For the children of Judah have done evil in my sight, saith the LORD: ##they##note:x[<<Jer 32:34; Ezek 7:20; Dan 9:27>>] have set their abominations in the house which is called by my name, to pollute it.
@ And they have built ##the high##note:x[<<Jer 32:35>>] places of Tophet, which __is__ in the valley of the son of Hinnom, ##to burn##note:x[<<2 Kgs 17:17>>] their sons and their daughters in the fire; ##which I##note:x[<<Lev 18:21>>] commanded __them__ not, neither came it into my heart.note:tn[__came…__: Heb. came it upon my heart]

@ ¶ Therefore, behold, ##the days##note:x[<<Jer 19:6>>] come, saith the LORD, that it shall no more be called Tophet, nor the valley of the son of Hinnom, but the valley of slaughter: ##for##note:x[<<Jer 19:11>>] they shall bury in Tophet, till there be no place.
@ ##And the carcases of this people shall be meat for the fowls of the heaven, and for the beasts of the earth; and none shall fray __them__ away.##note:x[<<Deut 28:26; Jer 12:9>>]
@ Then will I cause ##to cease##note:x[<<Ezek 26:13; Hos 2:11; Rev 18:23>>] from the cities of Judah, and from the streets of Jerusalem, the voice of mirth, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride: for the land shall be desolate.

=== @8

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judah’s Sin and Punishment

@ ##At that time, saith the LORD, they shall bring out the bones of the kings of Judah, and the bones of his princes, and the bones of the priests, and the bones of the prophets, and the bones of the inhabitants of Jerusalem, out of their graves:##note:x[<<Ezek 6:5>>]
@ And they shall spread them before the sun, and the moon, ##and all##note:x[<<Deut 4:19; 2 Kgs 23:5; Zeph 1:5>>] the host of heaven, whom they have loved, and whom they have served, and after whom they have walked, and whom they have sought, and whom they have worshipped: they shall not be gathered, nor be buried; they shall be for dung upon the face of the earth.
@ And ##death##note:x[<<Rev 9:6>>] shall be chosen rather than life by all the residue of them that remain of this evil family, which remain ##in all##note:x[<<Jer 23:3; 29:14>>] the places whither I have driven them, saith the LORD of hosts.

@ ¶ Moreover thou shalt say unto them, Thus saith the LORD; ##Shall they##note:x[<<Prov 24:16; Mic 7:8>>] fall, and not arise? shall he ##turn##note:x[<<Jer 3:1>>] away, and not return?
@ Why __then__ is this people of Jerusalem slidden back by a perpetual backsliding? ##they hold##note:x[<<Jer 9:6>>] fast deceit, ##they refuse##note:x[<<Jer 5:3; Zech 7:11>>] to return.
@ I ##hearkened##note:x[<<Ps 14:2; Isa 30:18>>] and heard, __but__ they spake not aright: no man repented him of his wickedness, ##saying##note:x[<<Ezek 18:28>>], What have I done? every one turned to his course, as the horse rusheth into the battle.
@ Yea, the ##stork##note:x[<<Isa 1:3>>] in the heaven knoweth her appointed times; and the turtle and the crane and the swallow observe the time of their coming; but my ##people##note:x[<<Isa 1:3; Jer 5:4>>] know not the judgment of the LORD.
@ How do ye say, ##We##note:x[<<Job 5:12–13; John 9:41>>] __are__ wise, and the law of the LORD __is__ with us? Lo, certainly ##in vain##note:x[<<Isa 10:1–2>>] made he __it;__ the pen of the scribes __is__ in vain.note:tn[__in vain made…__: or, the false pen of the scribes worketh for falsehood]
@ ##The wise __men__ are##note:x[<<Job 5:12; Jer 6:15>>] ashamed, they are dismayed and taken: ##lo##note:x[<<Ps 119:98–100>>], they have rejected the word of the LORD; and what wisdom __is__ in them?note:tn[__The wise…__: or, Have they been ashamed, etc]note:tn[__what…__: Heb. the wisdom of what thing]
@ Therefore ##will I##note:x[<<Jer 6:12>>] give their wives unto others, __and__ their fields to them that shall inherit __them:__ ##for##note:x[<<Ezek 33:31>>] every one from the least even unto the greatest is given to covetousness, from the prophet even unto the priest every one dealeth falsely.
@ For ##they##note:x[<<Jer 6:14>>] have healed the hurt of the daughter of my people slightly, saying, Peace, peace; when __there is__ no peace.
@ Were they ##ashamed when##note:x[<<Ps 52:7; Jer 3:3; 6:15>>] they had committed abomination? nay, they were not at all ashamed, neither could they blush: therefore shall they fall among them that fall: in the time of their visitation they shall be cast down, saith the LORD.note:tn[__I will…__: or, In gathering I will consume]

@ ¶ I will surely consume them, saith the LORD: ##__there__##note:x[<<Joel 1:7; Matt 21:19>>] __shall be__ no grapes on the vine, nor figs on the fig tree, and ##the leaf##note:x[<<Jer 17:8>>] shall fade; and __the things that__ I have given them shall pass away from them.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The People Respond

@ Why do we sit still? assemble yourselves, and let us ##enter##note:x[<<Jer 35:11>>] into the defenced cities, and let us be silent there: for the LORD our God hath put us to silence, and given us ##water##note:x[<<Jer 9:15; 23:15>>] of ##gall##note:tn[or, poison] to drink, because we have sinned against the LORD.
@ ##We looked for peace, but no good __came; and__ for a time of health, and behold trouble!##note:x[<<Jer 14:19>>]
@ The snorting of his horses ##was heard##note:x[<<Judg 18:29>>] from Dan: the whole land trembled ##at the##note:x[<<Judg 5:22>>] sound of the neighing of his strong ones; for they are come, and have devoured the land, and ##all that is in it##note:x[<<1 Cor 10:28>>]; the city, and those that dwell therein.note:tn[__all…__: Heb. the fulness thereof]
@ For, behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Deut 32:24>>] send serpents, cockatrices, among you, ##which##note:x[<<Ps 58:4–5; Eccl 10:11>>] __will__ not __be__ charmed, and they shall bite you, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Weeps for His People

@ ¶ __When__ I would comfort myself against sorrow, my heart __is__ faint ##in##note:tn[Heb. upon] me.
@ Behold ##the voice##note:x[<<Isa 39:3>>] of the cry of the daughter of my people because of them that dwell in a far country: __Is__ not the LORD in Zion? __is__ not her king in her? Why have they provoked me to anger with their graven images, __and__ with strange vanities?note:tn[__them…__: Heb. the country of them that are far off]
@ ##The harvest is past, the summer is ended, and we are not saved.##note:x[<<Prov 10:5; Matt 25:1–12; Luke 13:25>>]
@ For ##the hurt##note:x[<<Jer 14:17>>] of the daughter of my people am I hurt; ##I am##note:x[<<Joel 2:6; Nah 2:10>>] black; astonishment hath taken hold on me.
@ __Is there__ ##no balm##note:x[<<Gen 37:25; Jer 46:11; 51:8>>] in Gilead; __is there__ no physician there? why then is not the health of the daughter of my people ##recovered##note:tn[Heb. gone up?]?

=== @9

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Lament over Zion

@ note:x[<<Isa 22:4; Jer 13:17; Lam 2:11>>]Oh that my head were waters, and mine eyes a fountain of tears, that I might weep day and night for the slain of the daughter of my people!note:tn[__Oh…__: Heb. Who will give my head, etc]
@ Oh that I had in the wilderness a lodging place of wayfaring men; that I might leave my people, and go from them! ##for##note:x[<<Jer 5:7–8; 23:10; Hos 4:2>>] they __be__ all adulterers, an assembly of treacherous men.
@ And ##they bend##note:x[<<Isa 59:13–15; Jer 9:8>>] their tongues __like__ their bow __for__ lies: but they are not valiant for the truth upon the earth; for they proceed from evil to evil, and ##they know##note:x[<<Jer 4:22>>] not me, saith the LORD.
@ Take ##ye heed##note:x[<<Ps 12:2–3; Prov 26:24–25; Mic 7:5–6>>] every one of his ##neighbour##note:tn[or, friend], and trust ye not in any brother: for every brother will utterly supplant, and every neighbour will walk with slanders.
@ And ##they will##note:x[<<Isa 59:13–15; Mic 6:12>>] ##deceive##note:tn[or, mock] every one his neighbour, and will not speak the truth: they have ##taught##note:x[<<Job 15:5>>] their tongue to speak lies, __and__ weary themselves to commit iniquity.
@ Thine ##habitation##note:x[<<Jer 11:19>>] __is__ in the midst of deceit; through deceit they ##refuse##note:x[<<Jer 13:10; Hos 4:6>>] to know me, saith the LORD.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts, Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 1:25; 48:10; Mal 3:3>>] melt them, and try them; for how shall I do for the daughter of my people?
@ Their ##tongue##note:x[<<Ps 64:3–4>>] __is as__ an arrow shot out; it speaketh deceit: ##__one__##note:x[<<Ps 28:3; Prov 26:24–26>>] speaketh peaceably to his neighbour with his mouth, but in heart he layeth his wait.note:tn[__in heart__: Heb. in the midst of him]note:tn[__his wait__: or, wait for him]

@ ¶ ##Shall I not visit them for these __things__? saith the LORD: shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?##note:x[<<Jer 5:9, 29>>]
@ For the mountains will I take up a weeping and wailing, and for the ##habitations##note:tn[or, pastures] of the wilderness a lamentation, ##because##note:x[<<Jer 12:4>>] they are burned up, so that none can pass through __them;__ neither can __men__ hear the voice of the cattle; ##both##note:x[<<Hos 4:3>>] the fowl of the heavens and the beast are fled; they are gone.note:tn[__burned up__: or, desolate]note:tn[__both…__: Heb. from the fowl even to, etc]
@ And I will make ##Jerusalem##note:x[<<Isa 25:2; Jer 51:37>>] heaps, __and__ ##a den##note:x[<<Jer 10:22>>] of dragons; and I will make the cities of Judah ##desolate##note:tn[Heb. desolation], without an inhabitant.

@ ¶ Who __is__ ##the wise##note:x[<<Ps 107:43; Hos 14:9>>] man, that may understand this? and __who is he__ to whom the mouth of the LORD hath spoken, that he may declare it, for what the land perisheth __and__ is burned up like a wilderness, that none passeth through?
@ ##And the LORD saith, Because they have forsaken my law which I set before them, and have not obeyed my voice, neither walked therein;##note:x[<<2 Chr 7:19; Jer 22:9>>]
@ But have ##walked##note:x[<<Jer 3:17; 7:24; Rom 1:21–24>>] after the ##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of their own heart, and after Baalim, which their fathers taught them:
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 80:5; Jer 8:14; Lam 3:15>>] feed them, __even__ this people, with wormwood, and give them water of gall to drink.
@ I will ##scatter##note:x[<<Lev 26:33; Deut 28:64>>] them also among the heathen, whom neither they nor their fathers have known: ##and I##note:x[<<Ezek 5:2>>] will send a sword after them, till I have consumed them.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, Consider ye, and ##call##note:x[<<Eccl 12:5; Amos 5:16–17>>] for the mourning women, that they may come; and send for cunning __women__, that they may come:
@ And let them make haste, and take up a wailing for us, that ##our eyes##note:x[<<Isa 22:4; Jer 9:1; 14:17>>] may run down with tears, and our eyelids gush out with waters.
@ For a voice of wailing is heard out of Zion, How are we spoiled! ##we are##note:x[<<Jer 4:13>>] greatly confounded, because we have forsaken the land, because our dwellings have cast __us__ out.
@ Yet ##hear##note:x[<<Isa 32:9–13; Luke 23:27–30>>] the word of the LORD, O ye women, and let your ear ##receive##note:x[<<Job 22:22>>] the word of his mouth, and teach your daughters wailing, and every one her neighbour lamentation.
@ ##For death is come up into our windows, __and__ is entered into our palaces, to cut off the children from without, __and__ the young men from the streets.##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:17; Jer 6:11; 15:7>>]
@ Speak, Thus saith the LORD, Even the carcases of men shall ##fall##note:x[<<Jer 8:2; 25:33>>] as dung upon the open field, and as the handful after the harvestman, and none shall gather __them__.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD, Let not the ##wise##note:x[<<Eccl 9:11; Isa 5:21; 1 Cor 1:27–29>>] __man__ glory in his wisdom, neither let the mighty __man__ glory in his might, let not the rich __man__ glory in his riches:
@ But ##let him##note:x[<<1 Cor 1:31; 2 Cor 10:17; Gal 6:14>>] that glorieth glory in this, that he understandeth and knoweth me, that I __am__ the LORD which exercise lovingkindness, judgment, and righteousness, in the earth: for in these __things__ I delight, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ Behold, the days come, saith the LORD, ##that##note:x[<<Rom 2:8–9, 25–26; Gal 5:2–6>>] I will ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] all __them which are__ circumcised with the uncircumcised;
@ Egypt, and ##Judah##note:x[<<Isa 19:24–25>>], and Edom, and the children of Ammon, and Moab, and all __that are__ in the utmost corners, that dwell in the wilderness: for all __these__ nations __are__ uncircumcised, and all the house of Israel __are__ ##uncircumcised in##note:x[<<Lev 26:41; Jer 4:4>>] the heart.note:tn[__in the utmost…__: Heb. cut off into corners, or, having the corners of their hair polled]

=== @10

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Sovereignty of God

@ Hear ye the word which the LORD speaketh unto you, O house of Israel:
@ Thus saith the LORD, ##Learn##note:x[<<Lev 18:3; 20:23; Ezek 20:32>>] not the way of the heathen, and be not dismayed at the signs of heaven; for the heathen are dismayed at them.
@ For the ##customs##note:x[<<Lev 18:30; 1 Pet 1:18>>] of the people __are__ vain: for ##__one__##note:x[<<Isa 44:9–20>>] cutteth a tree out of the forest, the work of the hands of the workman, with the axe.note:tn[__customs…__: Heb. statutes, or, ordinances are vanity]
@ They ##deck##note:x[<<Isa 40:19–20>>] it with silver and with gold; they ##fasten##note:x[<<Isa 46:7>>] it with nails and with hammers, that it move not.
@ They __are__ upright as the palm tree, but ##speak##note:x[<<Ps 115:5–8; 1 Cor 12:2>>] not: they must needs ##be borne##note:x[<<Isa 46:7>>], because they cannot go. Be not afraid of them; for they cannot do evil, neither also __is it__ in them to do good.
@ Forasmuch as ##__there__##note:x[<<Exod 15:11>>] __is__ none like unto thee, O LORD; ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 48:1; 96:4>>] __art__ great, and thy name __is__ great in might.
@ Who ##would##note:x[<<Rev 15:4>>] not fear thee, ##O King##note:x[<<Ps 22:28>>] of nations? for to thee doth it appertain: forasmuch as among all the wise __men__ of the nations, and in all their kingdoms, __there is__ none like unto thee.note:tn[__to…__: or, it liketh thee]
@ But they are ##altogether##note:tn[Heb. in one, or, at once] ##brutish##note:x[<<Jer 10:14; Zech 10:2>>] and foolish: the stock __is__ a doctrine of vanities.
@ Silver spread into plates is brought from Tarshish, and gold from ##Uphaz##note:x[<<Dan 10:5>>], the work of the workman, and of the hands of the founder: blue and purple __is__ their clothing: they ##__are__ all##note:x[<<Ps 115:4>>] the work of cunning __men__.
@ But the LORD __is__ the ##true God##note:x[<<Deut 32:4>>], he __is__ the living God, and an ##everlasting king##note:x[<<Isa 57:15>>]: ##at##note:x[<<Nah 1:6>>] his wrath the earth shall tremble, and the nations shall not be able to abide his indignation.note:tn[__true…__: Heb. God of truth]note:tn[__living God__: Heb. living Gods]note:tn[__everlasting…__: Heb. king of eternity]
@ Thus shall ye say unto them, ##The gods##note:x[<<Ps 96:5>>] that have not made the heavens and the earth, __even__ ##they##note:x[<<Isa 2:18; Jer 10:15>>] shall perish from the earth, and from under these heavens.
@ He ##hath made##note:x[<<Gen 1:1>>] the earth by his power, he hath ##established##note:x[<<Prov 3:19>>] the world by his wisdom, and hath ##stretched##note:x[<<Job 9:8>>] out the heavens by his discretion.
@ When he uttereth his voice, __there is__ a ##multitude##note:tn[or, noise] of waters in the heavens, and ##he causeth##note:x[<<Ps 135:7>>] the vapours to ascend from the ends of the earth; he maketh lightnings ##with##note:tn[or, for] rain, and ##bringeth##note:x[<<Job 38:22>>] forth the wind out of his treasures.
@ Every ##man##note:x[<<Isa 46:7–8>>] is brutish in __his__ knowledge: every founder is confounded by the graven image: for his molten image __is__ falsehood, ##and##note:x[<<Ps 115:4–8; 135:16–18>>] __there is__ no breath in them.note:tn[__brutish in his knowledge__: or, more brutish than to know]
@ They __are__ ##vanity##note:x[<<Isa 41:24; Jer 14:22; 51:18>>], __and__ the work of errors: in the time of their visitation they shall perish.
@ The portion of Jacob __is__ not like them: for he __is__ the former of all __things;__ and ##Israel##note:x[<<Deut 32:9>>] __is__ the rod of his inheritance: ##The LORD##note:x[<<Jer 31:35; 32:18>>] of hosts __is__ his name.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Coming Captivity of Judah

@ ¶ Gather up ##thy wares##note:x[<<Jer 6:1; Ezek 12:3–12; Mic 2:10>>] out of the land, O ##inhabitant##note:tn[Heb. inhabitress] of the fortress.
@ For thus saith the LORD, Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<1 Sam 25:29>>] sling out the inhabitants of the land at this once, and will distress them, that they may find __it so__.

@ ¶ Woe is me for my hurt! my wound is grievous: but I said, ##Truly##note:x[<<Mic 7:9>>] this __is__ a grief, and I must bear it.
@ My ##tabernacle##note:x[<<Jer 4:20>>] is spoiled, and all my cords are broken: ##my children##note:x[<<Jer 31:15>>] are gone forth of me, and they __are__ not: ##__there__##note:x[<<Jer 4:20>>] __is__ none to stretch forth my tent any more, and to set up my curtains.
@ For ##the pastors##note:x[<<Jer 12:10; Ezek 34:2–10>>] are become brutish, and have not sought the LORD: therefore they shall not prosper, and all ##their##note:x[<<Jer 23:1>>] flocks shall be scattered.
@ Behold, the noise of the bruit is come, and a great commotion out of the north country, to make the cities of Judah desolate, __and__ ##a den##note:x[<<Jer 9:11>>] of dragons.

@ ¶ ##O LORD, I know that the way of man __is__ not in himself: __it is__ not in man that walketh to direct his steps.##note:x[<<Ps 37:23; Prov 16:1; 20:24>>]
@ O LORD, ##correct##note:x[<<Ps 6:1; 38:1; Jer 30:11>>] me, but with judgment; not in thine anger, lest thou bring me to nothing.note:tn[__bring…__: Heb. diminish me]
@ ##Pour##note:x[<<Ps 79:6–7>>] out thy fury upon the heathen ##that know##note:x[<<Job 18:21>>] thee not, and upon the families that call not on thy name: for they have ##eaten##note:x[<<Jer 8:16>>] up Jacob, and devoured him, and consumed him, and have made his habitation desolate.

=== @11

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Broken Covenant

@ The word that came to Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,
@ ##Hear ye the words of this covenant, and speak unto the men of Judah, and to the inhabitants of Jerusalem;##note:x[<<Jer 11:6>>]
@ ##And say thou unto them, Thus saith the LORD God of Israel; Cursed __be__ the man that obeyeth not the words of this covenant,##note:x[<<Deut 27:26; 28:15–68; 29:19–20>>]
@ Which I commanded your fathers in the day __that__ I brought them forth out of the land of Egypt, from the ##iron##note:x[<<Deut 4:20>>] furnace, saying, ##Obey##note:x[<<Lev 26:12>>] my voice, and do them, according to all which I command you: so shall ##ye be##note:x[<<Lev 26:12>>] my people, and I will be your God:
@ That I may ##perform##note:x[<<Ps 105:9–11>>] the oath which I have sworn unto your fathers, to give them ##a land##note:x[<<Deut 7:12–13>>] flowing with milk and honey, as __it is__ this day. Then answered I, and said, ##So be it##note:x[<<Jer 28:6>>], O LORD.note:tn[__So…__: Heb. Amen]
@ Then the LORD said unto me, Proclaim all these words in the cities of Judah, and in the streets of Jerusalem, saying, ##Hear##note:x[<<Rom 2:13; Jas 1:22>>] ye the words of this covenant, and do them.
@ For I earnestly protested unto your fathers in the day __that__ I brought them up out of the land of Egypt, __even__ unto this day, rising early and protesting, saying, Obey my voice.
@ Yet they ##obeyed##note:x[<<Jer 7:24, 26>>] not, nor inclined their ear, but walked every one in the note:x[<<Jer 7:24>>]##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of their evil heart: therefore I will bring upon them all the words of this covenant, which I commanded __them__ to do; but they did __them__ not.
@ ##And the LORD said unto me, A conspiracy is found among the men of Judah, and among the inhabitants of Jerusalem.##note:x[<<Hos 6:9>>]
@ They are ##turned##note:x[<<1 Sam 15:11>>] back to the iniquities of their forefathers, which refused to hear my words; and they went after other gods to serve them: ##the house of Israel##note:x[<<Deut 31:16; Ezek 16:59>>] and the house of Judah have broken my covenant which I made with their fathers.

@ ¶ Therefore thus saith the LORD, Behold, I will bring evil upon them, which they shall not be able to escape; and though they shall ##cry##note:x[<<Prov 1:28; Isa 1:15; Ezek 8:18>>] unto me, I will not hearken unto them.note:tn[__to escape__: Heb. to go forth of]
@ Then shall the cities of Judah and inhabitants of Jerusalem ##go##note:x[<<Deut 32:37; Jer 2:28>>], and cry unto the gods unto whom they offer incense: but they shall not save them at all in the time of their ##trouble##note:tn[Heb. evil].
@ ##For __according__##note:x[<<Jer 2:28>>] __to__ the number of thy cities were thy gods, O Judah; and __according to__ the number of the streets of Jerusalem have ye set up altars to __that__ shameful thing, __even__ altars to burn incense unto Baal.note:tn[__shameful…__: Heb. shame]
@ Therefore ##pray##note:x[<<Jer 7:16; 14:11>>] not thou ##for##note:x[<<Jer 11:11>>] this people, neither lift up a cry or prayer for them: for I will not hear __them__ in the time that they cry unto me for their ##trouble##note:tn[Heb. evil].
@ What hath my beloved to do in mine house, __seeing__ she hath wrought lewdness with many, and the holy flesh is passed from thee? when ##thou doest evil##note:x[<<Prov 2:14>>], then thou rejoicest.note:tn[__What…__: Heb. What is to my beloved in my house]note:tn[__when…__: or, when thy evil is]
@ The LORD called thy name, ##A green##note:x[<<Ps 52:8; Rom 11:17–24>>] olive tree, fair, __and__ of goodly fruit: ##with##note:x[<<Isa 27:11>>] the noise of a great tumult he hath kindled fire upon it, and the branches of it are broken.
@ For the LORD of hosts, ##that##note:x[<<Isa 5:2; Jer 2:21; 12:2>>] planted thee, hath pronounced evil against thee, for the evil of the house of Israel and of the house of Judah, which they have done against themselves to provoke me to anger in offering incense unto Baal.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Plot against Jeremiah

[.parallel]
(<<Jeremiah 18:18–23>>)

@ ¶ And ##the LORD##note:x[<<1 Sam 23:11–12; 2 Kgs 6:9–10>>] hath given me knowledge __of it__, and I know __it:__ then thou shewedst me their doings.
@ But ##I __was__##note:x[<<Isa 53:7>>] like a lamb __or__ an ox __that__ is brought to the slaughter; ##and I##note:x[<<Jer 20:10>>] knew not that they had devised devices against me, __saying__, Let us ##destroy##note:x[<<Ps 83:4>>] the tree with the fruit thereof, and let us cut him off from the land of the living, that his name may be no more remembered.note:tn[__the tree…__: Heb. the stalk with his bread]
@ But, O LORD of hosts, that judgest righteously, that ##triest##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:7; Jer 17:10; 20:12>>] the reins and the heart, let me see thy vengeance on them: for unto thee have I revealed my cause.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of the men of Anathoth, that seek thy life, saying, Prophesy not in the name of the LORD, that thou die not by our hand:
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts, Behold, I will ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] them: the ##young##note:x[<<Jer 18:21>>] men shall die by the sword; their sons and their daughters shall die by famine:
@ And there shall be no remnant of them: for I will bring evil upon the men of Anathoth, __even__ ##the year##note:x[<<Jer 23:12>>] of their visitation.

=== @12

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Prosperity of the Wicked

@ Righteous __art__ thou, O LORD, when I plead with thee: yet let me talk with thee of __thy__ judgments: ##Wherefore doth##note:x[<<Ps 37:1; 92:7; Mal 3:15>>] the way of the wicked prosper? __wherefore__ are all they happy that deal very treacherously?note:tn[__talk…__: or, reason the case with thee]
@ Thou hast planted them, yea, they have taken root: they grow, yea, they bring forth fruit: thou __art__ ##near##note:x[<<Isa 29:13; Matt 15:8; Titus 1:16>>] in their mouth, and far from their reins.note:tn[__they grow__: Heb. they go on]
@ But thou, O LORD, ##knowest##note:x[<<2 Kgs 20:3; Ps 139:1>>] me: thou hast seen me, and tried mine heart toward thee: ##pull##note:x[<<Jer 17:18>>] them out like sheep for the slaughter, and prepare them for the day of slaughter.note:tn[__toward…__: Heb. with thee]
@ How ##long##note:x[<<Jer 9:10>>] shall the land mourn, and ##the herbs##note:x[<<Ps 107:34>>] of every field wither, for the wickedness of them that dwell therein? ##the beasts##note:x[<<Jer 4:25>>] are consumed, and the birds; because they said, He shall not see our last end.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Answer to Jeremiah

@ ¶ If ##thou hast##note:x[<<Prov 24:10; Heb 12:3–4; 1 Pet 4:12>>] run with the footmen, and they have wearied thee, then how canst thou contend with horses? and __if__ in the land of peace, __wherein__ thou trustedst, __they wearied thee__, then how wilt thou do in the swelling of Jordan?
@ For even ##thy brethren##note:x[<<Jer 9:4>>], and the house of thy father, even they have dealt treacherously with thee; yea, they have called a multitude after thee: believe them not, ##though##note:x[<<Ps 12:2; Prov 26:25>>] they speak fair words unto thee.note:tn[__they have called…__: or, they cried after thee fully]note:tn[__fair…__: Heb. good things]

@ ¶ I ##have forsaken##note:x[<<Ps 78:59–60; Isa 2:6; Jer 11:15>>] mine house, I have left mine heritage; I have given the dearly beloved of my soul into the hand of her enemies.note:tn[__the dearly…__: Heb. the love]
@ Mine heritage is unto me as a lion in the forest; it crieth out against me: ##therefore##note:x[<<Hos 9:15; Amos 6:8>>] have I hated it.note:tn[__crieth…__: or, yelleth: Heb. giveth out his voice]
@ Mine heritage __is__ unto me __as__ a ##speckled##note:tn[or, taloned] bird, ##the birds##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:2>>] round about __are__ against her; ##come ye##note:x[<<Isa 56:9; Jer 7:33>>], assemble all the beasts of the field, ##come to##note:tn[or, cause them to come to] devour.
@ Many pastors have destroyed ##my vineyard##note:x[<<Isa 5:1–7>>], they have ##trodden##note:x[<<Isa 63:18>>] my portion under foot, they have made my pleasant portion a desolate wilderness.note:tn[__pleasant…__: Heb. portion of desire]
@ They have made it desolate, __and being__ desolate ##it mourneth##note:x[<<Jer 23:10>>] unto me; the whole land is made desolate, because no man ##layeth##note:x[<<Isa 42:25>>] __it__ to heart.
@ The spoilers are come upon all high places through the wilderness: for ##the sword##note:x[<<Jer 47:6; Amos 9:4>>] of the LORD shall devour from the __one__ end of the land even to the __other__ end of the land: no flesh shall have peace.
@ They have ##sown##note:x[<<Deut 28:38; Mic 6:15; Hag 1:6>>] wheat, but shall reap thorns: they have put themselves to pain, __but__ shall not profit: and ##they shall##note:tn[or, ye shall] be ashamed of your revenues because of the fierce anger of the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Message for Israel’s Neighbors

[.parallel]
(<<Amos 1:1–15>>)

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD ##against##note:x[<<Zeph 2:8–10>>] all mine evil neighbours, ##that##note:x[<<Zech 2:8>>] touch the inheritance which I have caused my people Israel to inherit; Behold, I will pluck them out of their land, ##and pluck##note:x[<<Jer 3:18>>] out the house of Judah from among them.
@ And it shall come to pass, ##after##note:x[<<Jer 48:47; 49:6>>] that I have plucked them out I will return, and have compassion on them, and will bring them again, every man to his ##heritage##note:x[<<Jer 49:6>>], and every man to his land.
@ And it shall come to pass, if they will diligently learn the ways of my people, to swear by ##my name##note:x[<<Jer 4:2>>], The LORD liveth; ##as they##note:x[<<Josh 23:7; Zeph 1:5>>] taught my people to swear by Baal; then shall they be built in the midst of my people.
@ But ##if##note:x[<<Isa 60:12>>] they will not obey, I will utterly pluck up and destroy that nation, saith the LORD.

=== @13

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Linen Loincloth

@ Thus saith the LORD unto me, Go and get thee a linen girdle, and put it upon thy loins, and put it not in water.
@ So I got a girdle ##according##note:x[<<Ezek 2:8>>] to the word of the LORD, and put __it__ on my loins.
@ And the word of the LORD came unto me the second time, saying,
@ Take the girdle that thou hast got, which __is__ upon thy loins, and arise, go to Euphrates, and hide it there in a hole of the rock.
@ So I went, and hid it by Euphrates, ##as##note:x[<<Exod 40:16>>] the LORD commanded me.
@ And it came to pass after many days, that the LORD said unto me, Arise, go to Euphrates, and take the girdle from thence, which I commanded thee to hide there.
@ Then I went to Euphrates, and digged, and took the girdle from the place where I had hid it: and, behold, the girdle was marred, it was profitable for nothing.
@ Then the word of the LORD came unto me, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD, After this manner will I mar ##the pride##note:x[<<Lev 26:19>>] of Judah, and the great pride of Jerusalem.
@ This ##evil##note:x[<<Num 14:11>>] people, which refuse to hear my words, which ##walk##note:x[<<Jer 9:14; 16:12>>] in the ##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of their heart, and walk after other gods, to serve them, and to worship them, shall even be as this girdle, which is good for nothing.
@ For as the girdle cleaveth to the loins of a man, so have ##I caused##note:x[<<Exod 19:5–6>>] to cleave unto me the whole house of Israel and the whole house of Judah, saith the LORD; that they might be unto me for a people, and ##for a name##note:x[<<Isa 43:21; Jer 33:9>>], and for a praise, and for a glory: but they would not hear.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Wineskins

@ ¶ Therefore thou shalt speak unto them this word; Thus saith the LORD God of Israel, Every bottle shall be filled with wine: and they shall say unto thee, Do we not certainly know that every bottle shall be filled with wine?
@ Then shalt thou say unto them, Thus saith the LORD, Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 60:3; Isa 51:17; 63:6>>] fill all the inhabitants of this land, even the kings that sit upon David’s throne, and the priests, and the prophets, and all the inhabitants of Jerusalem, with drunkenness.
@ And ##I will dash##note:x[<<Ps 2:9>>] them one against another, even the fathers and the sons together, saith the LORD: ##I will not##note:x[<<Deut 29:20>>] pity, nor spare, nor have mercy, but destroy them.note:tn[__one…__: Heb. a man against his brother]note:tn[__but…__: Heb. from destroying them]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Captivity Threatened

@ ¶ Hear ye, ##and##note:x[<<Rev 2:29>>] give ear; ##be##note:x[<<Isa 28:14–22>>] not proud: for the LORD hath spoken.
@ Give glory to the LORD your God, ##before##note:x[<<Isa 8:22; 59:9>>] he cause darkness, and before your feet stumble upon the dark mountains, and, ##while##note:x[<<Isa 59:9>>] ye look for light, he turn it into the shadow of death, __and__ make __it__ gross darkness.
@ But ##if##note:x[<<Mal 2:2>>] ye will not hear it, ##my soul##note:x[<<Jer 9:1; 14:17>>] shall weep in secret places for __your__ pride; and mine eye shall weep sore, and run down with tears, because the LORD’s flock is carried away captive.
@ Say ##unto##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:12, 15; Jer 22:26>>] the king and to the queen, Humble yourselves, sit down: for your ##principalities##note:tn[or, head tires] shall come down, __even__ the crown of your glory.
@ The cities of the south shall be shut up, and none shall open __them:__ ##Judah##note:x[<<Jer 52:27>>] shall be carried away captive all of it, it shall be wholly carried away captive.
@ Lift up your eyes, ##and##note:x[<<Jer 1:14; 6:22; Hab 1:6>>] behold them that come from the north: where __is__ the flock __that__ was given thee, thy beautiful flock?
@ What wilt thou say when he shall ##punish##note:tn[Heb visit upon] thee? for thou hast taught them __to be__ captains, __and__ as chief over thee: ##shall not##note:x[<<Isa 13:8; Jer 4:31>>] sorrows take thee, as a woman in travail?

@ ¶ And if thou say in thine heart, ##Wherefore##note:x[<<Jer 5:19; 16:10–11>>] come these things upon me? For the greatness of thine iniquity are thy ##skirts##note:x[<<Nah 3:5>>] discovered, __and__ thy heels made bare.note:tn[__made…__: or, shall be violently taken away]
@ Can the ##Ethiopian##note:x[<<Jer 2:22; Matt 19:24–28>>] change his skin, or the leopard his spots? __then__ may ye also do good, that are note:x[<<Jer 9:5>>]##accustomed##note:tn[Heb. taught] to do evil.
@ Therefore ##will##note:x[<<Lev 26:33>>] I scatter them ##as##note:x[<<Ps 1:4>>] the stubble that passeth away by the wind of the wilderness.
@ This __is__ ##thy lot##note:x[<<Job 20:29; Ps 11:6>>], the portion of thy measures from me, saith the LORD; ##because##note:x[<<Ps 9:17>>] thou hast forgotten me, and trusted in falsehood.
@ ##Therefore will I discover thy skirts upon thy face, that thy shame may appear.##note:x[<<Lam 1:8; Ezek 16:37; Hos 2:10>>]
@ I have seen thine adulteries, and thy neighings, the lewdness of thy whoredom, __and__ thine ##abominations##note:x[<<Isa 65:7; Ezek 6:13>>] on the hills in the fields. Woe unto thee, O Jerusalem! ##wilt##note:x[<<Ezek 24:13>>] thou not be made clean? when __shall it__ once __be__?note:tn[__when…__: Heb. after when yet?]

=== @14

[discrete.pericope]
==== Drought, Famine, Sword, and Plague

@ The word of the LORD that came to Jeremiah concerning ##the dearth##note:x[<<Jer 17:8>>].note:tn[__the dearth__: Heb. the words of the dearths, or, restraints]
@ Judah ##mourneth##note:x[<<Isa 3:26>>], and the gates thereof languish; ##they##note:x[<<Jer 8:21>>] are black unto the ground; and ##the cry##note:x[<<1 Sam 5:12>>] of Jerusalem is gone up.
@ And their nobles have sent their little ones to the waters: they came to the ##pits##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:31; Jer 2:13>>], __and__ found no water; they returned with their vessels empty; they were ashamed and confounded, and ##covered##note:x[<<2 Sam 15:30>>] their heads.
@ Because ##the ground##note:x[<<Joel 1:19–20>>] is chapt, for there was no rain in the earth, ##the plowmen##note:x[<<Joel 1:11>>] were ashamed, they covered their heads.
@ ##Yea, the hind also calved in the field, and forsook __it__, because there was no grass.##note:x[<<Ps 29:9>>]
@ And ##the wild##note:x[<<Job 39:5–6; Jer 2:24>>] asses did stand in the high places, they snuffed up the wind like dragons; their eyes did fail, because __there was__ no grass.

@ ¶ O LORD, ##though##note:x[<<Hos 5:5>>] our iniquities testify against us, ##do##note:x[<<Ps 25:11; Jer 14:20–21>>] thou __it__ for thy name’s sake: for our backslidings are many; we have sinned against thee.
@ O ##the hope##note:x[<<Jer 17:13; 50:7>>] of Israel, the ##saviour##note:x[<<Isa 43:3>>] thereof in time of trouble, why shouldest thou be as a stranger in the land, and as a wayfaring man __that__ turneth aside to tarry for a night?
@ Why shouldest thou be as a man astonied, as a mighty man __that__ ##cannot##note:x[<<Num 11:23; Isa 59:1>>] save? yet thou, O LORD, __art__ in the midst of us, and ##we are called by thy name##note:x[<<Isa 63:19>>]; leave us not.note:tn[__we…__: Heb. thy name is called upon us]

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD unto this people, Thus have they loved to wander, they have not ##refrained##note:x[<<Ps 119:101>>] their feet, therefore the LORD doth not accept them; ##he will##note:x[<<Hos 8:13; 9:9>>] now remember their iniquity, and visit their sins.
@ ##Then said the LORD unto me, Pray not for this people for __their__ good.##note:x[<<Jer 7:16; 11:14>>]
@ When ##they fast##note:x[<<Prov 1:28; Jer 11:11; Ezek 8:18>>], I will not hear their cry; and when they offer burnt offering and an oblation, I will not accept them: but I will consume them by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence.

@ ¶ Then said I, ##Ah##note:x[<<Jer 4:10>>], Lord GOD! ##behold##note:x[<<Jer 6:14; 23:17>>], the prophets say unto them, Ye shall not see the sword, neither shall ye have famine; but I will give you assured peace in this place.note:tn[__assured…__: Heb. peace of truth]
@ Then the LORD said unto me, The prophets prophesy lies in my name: ##I sent##note:x[<<Jer 29:8–9>>] them not, neither have I commanded them, neither spake unto them: they prophesy unto you a false vision and ##divination##note:x[<<Jer 29:8–9; Ezek 13:6–7>>], and a thing of nought, and the deceit of their heart.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD concerning the prophets that prophesy in my name, and I sent them not, yet they say, ##Sword and famine shall not##note:x[<<Jer 5:12–13>>] be in this land; By sword and famine shall those prophets be consumed.
