@ And the people to whom they prophesy shall ##be cast##note:x[<<Ps 79:2–3; Jer 7:33; 15:2–3>>] out in the streets of Jerusalem because of the famine and the sword; and they shall have none to bury them, them, their wives, nor their sons, nor their daughters: for I will pour their wickedness upon them.

@ ¶ Therefore thou shalt say this word unto them; ##Let mine##note:x[<<Jer 8:21; 9:1; 13:17>>] eyes run down with tears night and day, and let them not cease: for the virgin daughter of my people is broken with a great breach, with a very grievous blow.
@ If I ##go forth##note:x[<<Lam 1:20; Ezek 7:15>>] into the field, then behold the slain with the sword! and if I enter into the city, then behold them that are sick with famine! ##yea##note:x[<<Jer 8:10>>], both the prophet and the priest go about into a land that they know not.note:tn[__go about…__: or, make merchandise against a land, and men acknowledge it not]

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Prayer for Mercy

[.parallel]
(<<Isaiah 63:15–19>>)

@ Hast thou ##utterly##note:x[<<Jer 6:30; Lam 5:22>>] rejected Judah? hath thy soul lothed Zion? why hast thou smitten us, and __there is__ no healing for us? ##we##note:x[<<Jer 8:15>>] looked for peace, and __there is__ no good; and for the time of healing, and behold trouble!
@ ##We acknowledge##note:x[<<Ps 32:5; Jer 3:25; 1 John 1:7–9>>], O LORD, our wickedness, __and__ the iniquity of our fathers: for we have sinned against thee.
@ Do not abhor __us__, ##for##note:x[<<Jer 14:7>>] thy name’s sake, do not ##disgrace##note:x[<<Ps 106:45>>] the throne of thy glory: ##remember##note:x[<<Ps 106:45>>], break not thy covenant with us.
@ Are there __any__ among the vanities of the Gentiles that can cause rain? or can the heavens give showers? ##__art__##note:x[<<Jer 5:24; Joel 2:23>>] not thou he, O LORD our God? therefore we will ##wait##note:x[<<Ps 135:7>>] upon thee: for thou hast made all these __things__.

=== @15

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment to Continue

@ Then said the LORD unto me, ##Though##note:x[<<Jer 11:14; 14:11; Ezek 14:14>>] Moses and Samuel stood before me, __yet__ my mind __could__ not __be__ toward this people: cast __them__ out of my sight, and let them go forth.
@ And it shall come to pass, if they say unto thee, Whither shall we go forth? then thou shalt tell them, Thus saith the LORD; Such as __are__ ##for death##note:x[<<Jer 43:11; Ezek 5:2; Zech 11:9>>], to death; and such as __are__ for the sword, to the sword; and such as __are__ for the famine, to the famine; and such as __are__ for the captivity, to the captivity.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Lev 26:16, 22; Deut 28:26>>] appoint over them four ##kinds##note:tn[Heb. families], saith the LORD: the sword to slay, and the dogs to tear, and the fowls of the heaven, and the beasts of the earth, to devour and destroy.
@ And I will ##cause them to be removed##note:x[<<Jer 24:9>>] into all kingdoms of the earth, ##because##note:x[<<2 Kgs 21:11–13; 23:26–27>>] of Manasseh the son of Hezekiah king of Judah, for __that__ which he did in Jerusalem.note:tn[__cause…__: Heb. give them for a removing]
@ ##For who##note:x[<<Isa 51:19; Jer 21:7; Nah 3:7>>] shall have pity upon thee, O Jerusalem? or who shall bemoan thee? or who shall go aside to ask how thou doest?note:tn[__how…__: Heb. of thy peace?]
@ Thou hast forsaken me, saith the LORD, ##thou art##note:x[<<Isa 1:4; Jer 7:24>>] gone backward: therefore will I ##stretch##note:x[<<Zeph 1:4>>] out my hand against thee, and destroy thee; I am weary with repenting.
@ And ##I will fan##note:x[<<Isa 41:16; Jer 51:2; Matt 3:12>>] them with a fan in the gates of the land; I will bereave __them__ of ##children##note:tn[or, whatsoever is dear], I will destroy my people, __since__ they return not from their ways.
@ Their widows are increased to me above the sand of the seas: I have brought upon them against the mother of the young men a spoiler at noonday: I have caused __him__ to fall upon it suddenly, and terrors upon the city.note:tn[__the mother…__: or, the mother city a young man spoiling, etc, or, the mother and the young men]
@ ##She that hath##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:5; Isa 47:9>>] borne seven languisheth: she hath given up the ghost; her sun is gone down while __it was__ yet day: she hath been ashamed and confounded: and the residue of them will I deliver to the sword before their enemies, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Woe

@ ¶ Woe is me, my mother, that thou hast borne me a man of strife and a man of contention to the whole earth! I have neither lent on usury, nor men have lent to me on usury; __yet__ every one of them doth ##curse##note:x[<<Luke 6:22>>] me.
@ The LORD said, Verily it shall be well with thy remnant; ##verily I##note:x[<<Ps 106:46; Jer 29:11–14>>] will ##cause the enemy to entreat thee##note:x[<<Prov 16:7>>] __well__ in the time of evil and in the time of affliction.note:tn[__cause…__: or, intreat the enemy for thee]
@ Shall iron break the northern iron and the steel?
@ Thy ##substance##note:x[<<Jer 17:3>>] and thy treasures will I give to the spoil ##without##note:x[<<Ps 44:12; Isa 52:3>>] price, and __that__ for all thy sins, even in all thy borders.
@ And I will make __thee__ to ##pass##note:x[<<Jer 16:13; 17:4>>] with thine enemies into a land __which__ thou knowest not: for ##a fire##note:x[<<Deut 32:22>>] is kindled in mine anger, __which__ shall burn upon you.

@ ¶ O LORD, ##thou##note:x[<<Jer 12:3>>] knowest: remember me, and visit me, and revenge me of my persecutors; take me not away in thy longsuffering: ##know##note:x[<<Ps 69:7–9; Luke 21:17>>] that for thy sake I have suffered rebuke.
@ Thy words were found, and ##I did##note:x[<<Ezek 3:1–3; Rev 10:9>>] eat them; and ##thy word##note:x[<<Ps 119:101–103>>] was unto me the joy and rejoicing of mine heart: for I am called by thy name, O LORD God of hosts.note:tn[__I am…__: Heb. thy name is called upon me]
@ I ##sat not##note:x[<<Ps 1:1; 26:4–5>>] in the assembly of the mockers, nor rejoiced; I ##sat alone##note:x[<<Lam 3:28>>] because of thy hand: for thou hast filled me with indignation.
@ Why is my pain perpetual, and ##my wound##note:x[<<Job 34:6; Jer 30:15; Mic 1:9>>] incurable, __which__ refuseth to be healed? wilt thou be altogether unto me as a liar, __and as__ waters __that__ ##fail##note:tn[Heb. be not sure?]?

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD’s Promise

@ ¶ Therefore thus saith the LORD, If thou return, then will I bring thee again, __and__ thou shalt ##stand##note:x[<<Zech 3:7>>] before me: and if thou ##take##note:x[<<Lev 10:10>>] forth the precious from the vile, thou shalt be ##as my##note:x[<<Luke 21:15>>] mouth: let them return unto thee; but return not thou unto them.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 1:18–19; Ezek 3:9>>] make thee unto this people a fenced brasen wall: and they shall fight against thee, ##but##note:x[<<Ps 124:1–3>>] they shall not prevail against thee: for I __am__ with thee to save thee and to deliver thee, saith the LORD.
@ And I will ##deliver##note:x[<<Isa 49:24–25; 54:17; 2 Cor 1:10>>] thee out of the hand of the wicked, and I will redeem thee out of the hand of the terrible.

=== @16

[discrete.pericope]
==== Disaster Predicted

@ The word of the LORD came also unto me, saying,
@ Thou shalt not take thee a wife, neither shalt thou have sons or daughters in this place.
@ For thus saith the LORD concerning the sons and concerning the daughters that are born in this place, and concerning their mothers that bare them, and concerning their fathers that begat them in this land;
@ They shall die of grievous deaths; they shall ##not##note:x[<<Jer 25:33>>] be lamented; neither shall they be buried; __but__ they shall be ##as dung##note:x[<<Ps 83:10; Jer 25:33>>] upon the face of the earth: and they shall be consumed by the sword, and by famine; and their carcases shall be meat for the fowls of heaven, and for the beasts of the earth.
@ For thus saith the LORD, ##Enter##note:x[<<Ezek 24:16–23>>] not into the house of ##mourning##note:tn[or, mourning feast], neither go to lament nor bemoan them: for ##I have##note:x[<<Isa 27:11; Jer 15:1–4>>] taken away my peace from this people, saith the LORD, __even__ lovingkindness and mercies.
@ Both the great and the small shall die in this land: they shall not be buried, neither shall __men__ lament for them, ##nor cut##note:x[<<Deut 14:1; Jer 41:5; 47:5>>] themselves, nor make themselves bald for them:
@ Neither shall __men__ ##tear __themselves__##note:x[<<Deut 26:14; Ezek 24:17; Hos 9:4>>] for them in mourning, to comfort them for the dead; neither shall __men__ give them the cup of consolation to drink for their father or for their mother.note:tn[__tear…__: or, break bread for them]
@ ##Thou shalt not also go into the house of feasting, to sit with them to eat and to drink.##note:x[<<Eccl 7:2–4; Isa 22:12–14; Jer 15:17>>]
@ For thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 7:34; 25:10; Ezek 26:13>>] cause to cease out of this place in your eyes, and in your days, the voice of mirth, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride.

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass, when thou shalt shew this people all these words, and they shall say unto thee, ##Wherefore##note:x[<<Jer 5:19>>] hath the LORD pronounced all this great evil against us? or what __is__ our iniquity? or what __is__ our sin that we have committed against the LORD our God?
@ Then shalt thou say unto them, ##Because##note:x[<<Ps 106:35–41>>] your fathers have forsaken me, saith the LORD, and have walked after other gods, and have served them, and have worshipped them, and have forsaken me, and have not kept my law;
@ And ye have done ##worse##note:x[<<Jer 7:26; 13:10>>] than your fathers; for, behold, ye walk every one after the note:x[<<Jer 13:10>>]##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of his evil heart, that they may not hearken unto me:
@ Therefore ##will I##note:x[<<Deut 4:26–28; 28:36>>] cast you out of this land into a land that ye know not, __neither__ ye nor your fathers; ##and##note:x[<<Deut 28:36>>] there shall ye serve other gods day and night; where I will not shew you favour.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God Will Restore Israel

@ ¶ Therefore, ##behold##note:x[<<Jer 23:7–8>>], the days come, saith the LORD, that it shall no more be said, The LORD liveth, ##that brought##note:x[<<Exod 20:2; Deut 15:15>>] up the children of Israel out of the land of Egypt;
@ But, The LORD liveth, ##that brought##note:x[<<Jer 3:18; 24:6; 32:37>>] up the children of Israel from the land of the north, and from all the lands whither he had driven them: and I will bring them again into their land that I gave unto their fathers.

@ ¶ Behold, ##I will send##note:x[<<Amos 4:2; Hab 1:14–15>>] for many fishers, saith the LORD, and they shall fish them; and after will I send for many ##hunters##note:x[<<Mic 7:2>>], and they shall hunt them from every mountain, and from every hill, and out of the holes of the rocks.
@ ##For mine eyes __are__ upon all their ways: they are not hid from my face, neither is their iniquity hid from mine eyes.##note:x[<<Prov 5:21; 15:3; Jer 23:24>>]
@ And ##first##note:x[<<Isa 40:2; Jer 17:18>>] I will recompense their iniquity and their sin double; because ##they have defiled##note:x[<<Num 35:33–34>>] my land, they have filled mine inheritance with the carcases of their detestable and abominable things.
@ O LORD, ##my strength##note:x[<<Isa 25:4; Jer 17:17; Nah 1:7>>], and my fortress, and my refuge in the day of affliction, the Gentiles shall come unto thee from the ends of the earth, and shall say, Surely our fathers have inherited lies, vanity, and __things__ wherein __there is__ no profit.
@ ##Shall a man make gods unto himself, and they __are__ no gods?##note:x[<<Ps 115:4–8; Isa 37:19; Gal 4:8>>]
@ Therefore, behold, ##I will this##note:x[<<Ps 9:16>>] once cause them to know, I will cause them to know mine hand and my might; ##and they##note:x[<<Ps 83:18; Jer 33:2>>] shall know that my name __is__ The LORD.note:tn[__The LORD__: or, JEHOVAH]

=== @17

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Sin and Punishment of Judah

@ The sin of Judah __is__ written with a pen of iron, __and__ with the ##point##note:tn[Heb. nail] of a diamond: __it is__ ##graven##note:x[<<Prov 3:3; 7:3; 2 Cor 3:3>>] upon the table of their heart, and upon the horns of your altars;
@ Whilst their children remember ##their altars##note:x[<<2 Chr 24:18; Jer 2:20>>] and their groves by the green trees upon the high hills.
@ O ##my##note:x[<<Jer 26:18>>] mountain in the field, ##I will##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:13; Jer 15:13>>] give thy substance __and__ all thy treasures to the spoil, __and__ thy high places for sin, throughout all thy borders.
@ And thou, even ##thyself##note:tn[Heb. in thyself], shalt discontinue from thine heritage that I gave thee; and I will cause thee to serve thine enemies in the land which thou knowest not: ##for##note:x[<<Isa 5:25; Jer 7:20; 15:14>>] ye have kindled a fire in mine anger, __which__ shall burn for ever.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD; ##Cursed##note:x[<<Ps 118:8–9; 146:3–4; Isa 2:22>>] __be__ the man that trusteth in man, and maketh flesh his arm, and whose heart departeth from the LORD.
@ For he shall be ##like##note:x[<<Ps 1:4>>] the heath in the desert, ##and shall##note:x[<<Job 20:17>>] not see when good cometh; but shall inhabit the parched places in the wilderness, __in__ ##a salt##note:x[<<Deut 29:23>>] land and not inhabited.
@ ##Blessed __is__ the man that trusteth in the LORD, and whose hope the LORD is.##note:x[<<Ps 34:8; 40:4; Isa 26:3–4>>]
@ For ##he shall##note:x[<<Ps 1:3; Isa 58:11; Ezek 47:12>>] be as a tree planted by the waters, and __that__ spreadeth out her roots by the river, and shall not see when heat cometh, but her leaf shall be green; and shall not be careful in the year of ##drought##note:tn[or, restraint], neither shall cease from yielding fruit.

@ ¶ ##The heart __is__ deceitful above all __things__, and desperately wicked: who can know it?##note:x[<<Eccl 9:3; Matt 15:19; Mark 7:21–22>>]
@ I ##the LORD##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:7; Jer 11:20; Rom 8:27>>] search the heart, __I__ try the reins, even to give every man according to his ways, __and__ according to the fruit of his doings.
@ __As__ the partridge sitteth __on eggs__, and hatcheth __them__ not; __so__ ##he that##note:x[<<Prov 21:6; 28:20; Jer 22:17>>] getteth riches, and not by right, shall leave them in the midst of his days, and at his end shall be a fool.note:tn[__sitteth…__: or, gathereth young which she hath not brought forth]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Prayer for Deliverance

@ ¶ ##A glorious high throne from the beginning __is__ the place of our sanctuary.##note:x[<<Ps 96:6; Jer 3:17; Ezek 43:7>>]
@ O LORD, the hope of Israel, all that forsake thee shall be ashamed, __and__ they that depart from me shall be written in the earth, because they have ##forsaken##note:x[<<John 4:14; 7:37–38; Rev 7:17>>] the LORD, the fountain of living waters.
@ ##Heal##note:x[<<Deut 32:39; Ps 6:2>>] me, O LORD, and I shall be healed; ##save##note:x[<<Ps 106:47>>] me, and I shall be saved: for thou __art__ my praise.

@ ¶ ##Behold, they say unto me, Where __is__ the word of the LORD? let it come now.##note:x[<<Isa 5:19; Ezek 12:22>>]
@ As for me, I have not hastened from __being__ a pastor to follow thee: neither have I desired the woeful day; thou knowest: that which came out of my lips was __right__ before thee.note:tn[__to…__: Heb. after thee]
@ Be not a terror unto me: ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 59:16; Jer 16:19; Nah 1:7>>] __art__ my hope in the day of evil.
@ Let them be ##confounded##note:x[<<Ps 35:4; 40:14; 83:17–18>>] that persecute me, but let not me be confounded: let them be dismayed, but let not me be dismayed: bring upon them the day of evil, and destroy them with double destruction.note:tn[__destroy…__: Heb. break them with a double breach]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Restoring the Sabbath

[.parallel]
(<<Nehemiah 13:15–22>>)

@ ¶ ##Thus said the LORD unto me; Go and stand in the gate of the children of the people, whereby the kings of Judah come in, and by the which they go out, and in all the gates of Jerusalem;##note:x[<<Jer 7:2; 26:2>>]
@ ##And say unto them, Hear ye the word of the LORD, ye kings of Judah, and all Judah, and all the inhabitants of Jerusalem, that enter in by these gates:##note:x[<<Ps 49:1–2; Jer 19:3; 22:2>>]
@ Thus saith the LORD; Take heed to yourselves, and ##bear##note:x[<<Num 15:32–36; Neh 13:15–21; John 5:9–12>>] no burden on the sabbath day, nor bring __it__ in by the gates of Jerusalem;
@ Neither carry forth a burden out of your houses on the sabbath day, ##neither do##note:x[<<Exod 20:8–10; Isa 56:2–6; Ezek 20:12>>] ye any work, but hallow ye the sabbath day, as I commanded your fathers.
@ But ##they obeyed##note:x[<<Jer 7:24–26; Acts 7:51>>] not, neither inclined their ear, but ##made##note:x[<<Prov 29:1>>] their neck stiff, that they might not hear, nor receive instruction.
@ And it shall come to pass, ##if##note:x[<<Deut 11:13; 2 Pet 1:5–10>>] ye diligently hearken unto me, saith the LORD, ##to bring##note:x[<<Jer 17:21–22>>] in no burden through the gates of this city on the sabbath day, but hallow the sabbath day, to do no work therein;
@ Then ##shall there##note:x[<<Jer 22:4>>] enter into the gates of this city kings and princes ##sitting##note:x[<<Isa 9:7>>] upon the throne of David, riding in chariots and on horses, they, and their princes, the men of Judah, and the inhabitants of Jerusalem: and this city shall remain for ever.
@ And they shall come ##from the cities##note:x[<<Jer 32:44; 33:13>>] of Judah, and from the places about Jerusalem, and from the land of Benjamin, and from ##the plain##note:x[<<Zech 7:7>>], and from the mountains, and from the south, bringing burnt offerings, and sacrifices, and meat offerings, and incense, and bringing sacrifices of praise, unto the house of the LORD.
@ But if ye will not hearken unto me to hallow the sabbath day, and not to bear a burden, even entering in at the gates of Jerusalem on the sabbath day; ##then##note:x[<<Jer 21:14>>] will I kindle a fire in the gates thereof, and it ##shall devour##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:9>>] the palaces of Jerusalem, and it ##shall not##note:x[<<Jer 7:20>>] be quenched.

=== @18

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Potter and the Clay

@ The word which came to Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,
@ Arise, ##and go##note:x[<<Heb 1:1>>] down to the potter’s house, and there I will ##cause##note:x[<<Acts 9:6>>] thee to hear my words.
@ Then I went down to the potter’s house, and, behold, he wrought a work on the ##wheels##note:tn[or, frames, or, seats].
@ And the vessel that he made of clay was marred in the hand of the potter: so he made it again another vessel, ##as##note:x[<<Isa 45:9; Jer 18:6; Rom 9:20–23>>] seemed good to the potter to make __it__.note:tn[__of clay…__: or, was marred, as clay in the hand of the potter]note:tn[__made it…__: Heb. returned and made, etc]
@ Then the word of the LORD came to me, saying,
@ note:x[<<Isa 64:8; Jer 18:4; Matt 20:15>>]O house of Israel, cannot I do with you as this potter? saith the LORD. Behold, as the clay __is__ in the potter’s hand, so __are__ ye in mine hand, O house of Israel.
@ __At what__ instant I shall speak concerning a nation, and concerning a kingdom, ##to pluck##note:x[<<Jer 1:10>>] up, and to pull down, and to destroy __it;__
@ If ##that nation##note:x[<<Ezek 18:21>>], against whom I have pronounced, turn from their evil, ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 26:13; Hos 11:8>>] repent of the evil that I thought to do unto them.
@ And __at what__ instant I shall speak concerning a nation, and concerning a kingdom, ##to build##note:x[<<Jer 1:10; 31:28; Amos 9:11–15>>] and to plant __it;__
@ If it ##do##note:x[<<Ezek 18:24; 33:18>>] evil in my sight, that it obey not my voice, ##then##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:30>>] I will repent of the good, wherewith I said I would benefit them.

@ ¶ Now therefore go to, speak to the men of Judah, and to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, saying, Thus saith the LORD; Behold, I frame evil against you, and devise a device against you: ##return##note:x[<<2 Kgs 17:13; Isa 55:6–7; Jer 7:3>>] ye now every one from his evil way, and make your ways and your doings good.
@ And they said, ##There##note:x[<<Jer 2:25>>] is no hope: but ##we will walk##note:x[<<Jer 7:24; 16:12>>] after our own devices, and we will every one do the imagination of his evil heart.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD; ##Ask##note:x[<<Jer 2:10–13>>] ye now among the heathen, ##who##note:x[<<Isa 66:8>>] hath heard such things: the virgin of Israel hath done ##a very##note:x[<<Jer 5:30>>] horrible thing.
@ Will __a man__ leave the snow of Lebanon __which cometh__ from the rock of the field? __or__ shall the cold flowing waters that come from another place be forsaken?note:tn[__the snow…__: or, my fields for a rock, or for the snow of Lebanon? shall the running waters be forsaken for the strange cold waters?]
@ Because ##my people##note:x[<<Jer 2:32>>] hath forgotten me, they have ##burned##note:x[<<Hos 11:2>>] incense to vanity, and they have caused them to stumble in their ways __from__ ##the ancient##note:x[<<Jer 6:16>>] paths, to walk in paths, __in__ a way not cast up;
@ To ##make##note:x[<<Jer 19:8; 25:9>>] their land desolate, __and__ a perpetual hissing; every one that passeth thereby ##shall be##note:x[<<Ps 22:7>>] astonished, and wag his head.
@ I will ##scatter##note:x[<<Job 27:21; Jer 13:24>>] them as with an east wind before the enemy; I will ##shew##note:x[<<Jer 2:27>>] them the back, and not the face, in the day of their calamity.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Another Plot against Jeremiah

[.parallel]
(<<Jeremiah 11:18–23>>)

@ ¶ note:x[<<Ps 52:2>>]Then said they, ##Come##note:x[<<Jer 11:19>>], and let us devise devices against Jeremiah; ##for the##note:x[<<Mal 2:7>>] law shall not perish from the priest, nor counsel from the wise, nor the word from the prophet. Come, and let us smite him with the tongue, and let us not give heed to any of his words.note:tn[__with…__: or, for the tongue]
@ Give heed to me, O LORD, and ##hearken##note:x[<<Neh 6:9>>] to the voice of them that contend with me.
@ Shall evil be recompensed for good? for they have ##digged##note:x[<<Ps 35:7; 57:6>>] a pit for my soul. ##Remember##note:x[<<Ps 106:23>>] that I stood before thee to speak good for them, __and__ to turn away thy wrath from them.
@ Therefore ##deliver##note:x[<<Ps 109:9–20>>] up their children to the famine, and pour out their __blood__ by the force of the sword; and let their wives be bereaved of their children, and __be__ widows; and let their men be put to death; __let__ their young men __be__ slain by the sword in battle.note:tn[__pour…__: Heb. pour them out]
@ Let ##a cry##note:x[<<Jer 6:26; 25:34–36>>] be heard from their houses, when thou shalt bring a troop suddenly upon them: for they have digged a pit to take me, ##and hid##note:x[<<Ps 140:5>>] snares for my feet.
@ Yet, LORD, thou knowest all their counsel against me to slay __me:__ ##forgive##note:x[<<Ps 35:4; 69:22–28>>] not their iniquity, neither blot out their sin from thy sight, but let them be overthrown before thee; deal __thus__ with them in the time of thine anger.note:tn[__to slay…__: Heb. for death]

=== @19

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Broken Jar

@ Thus saith the LORD, Go and get a potter’s earthen bottle, and __take__ of ##the ancients of the people##note:x[<<Ezek 8:11–12>>], and of the ancients of the priests;
@ And go forth unto ##the valley##note:x[<<Josh 15:8; 2 Kgs 23:10>>] of the son of Hinnom, which __is__ by the entry of the east gate, and proclaim there the words that I shall tell thee,note:tn[__the east…__: Heb. the sun gate]
@ And say, ##Hear##note:x[<<Jer 17:20>>] ye the word of the LORD, O kings of Judah, and inhabitants of Jerusalem; Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, I will bring evil upon this place, the which whosoever heareth, ##his ears##note:x[<<1 Sam 3:11>>] shall tingle.
@ Because ##they have##note:x[<<Deut 28:20; Jer 2:34>>] forsaken me, and have estranged this place, and have burned incense in it unto other gods, whom neither they nor their fathers have known, nor the kings of Judah, and have ##filled##note:x[<<Jer 2:34>>] this place with the blood of innocents;
@ They have built also the high places of Baal, ##to burn##note:x[<<Deut 12:31; Ps 106:37–38>>] their sons with fire __for__ burnt offerings unto Baal, ##which##note:x[<<Jer 32:35>>] I commanded not, nor spake __it__, neither came __it__ into my mind:
@ Therefore, behold, the days come, saith the LORD, that ##this##note:x[<<Josh 15:8>>] place shall no more be called Tophet, nor The valley of the son of Hinnom, but The valley of slaughter.
@ And I will make void the counsel of Judah and Jerusalem in this place; and ##I will cause##note:x[<<Lev 26:17>>] them to fall by the sword before their enemies, and by the hands of them that seek their lives: ##and their##note:x[<<Jer 16:4>>] carcases will I give to be meat for the fowls of the heaven, and for the beasts of the earth.
@ ##And I will make this city desolate, and an hissing; every one that passeth thereby shall be astonished and hiss because of all the plagues thereof.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:8; Jer 18:16; 49:13>>]
@ And I will cause them to ##eat the##note:x[<<Lev 26:29; Isa 9:20; Lam 4:10>>] flesh of their sons and the flesh of their daughters, and they shall eat every one the flesh of his friend in the siege and straitness, wherewith their enemies, and they that seek their lives, shall straiten them.

@ Then shalt thou break the bottle in the sight of the men that go with thee,
@ And shalt say unto them, Thus saith the LORD of hosts; ##Even##note:x[<<Ps 2:9>>] so will I break this people and this city, as __one__ breaketh a potter’s vessel, that cannot be made whole again: and they shall bury __them__ in Tophet, till __there be__ no place to bury.note:tn[__be made…__: Heb. be healed]
@ Thus will I do unto this place, saith the LORD, and to the inhabitants thereof, and __even__ make this city as Tophet:
@ And the houses of Jerusalem, and the houses of the kings of Judah, shall be ##defiled##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:12>>] as the place of Tophet, because of all the houses ##upon##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:12; Jer 32:29>>] whose roofs they have burned incense unto all the host of heaven, and have poured out drink offerings unto other gods.
@ Then came Jeremiah from Tophet, whither the LORD had sent him to prophesy; and ##he stood##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:5; Jer 26:2>>] in the court of the LORD’s house; and said to all the people,
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, I will bring upon this city and upon all her towns all the evil that I have pronounced against it, ##because##note:x[<<Neh 9:29; Jer 7:26; 17:23>>] they have hardened their necks, that they might not hear my words.

=== @20

[discrete.pericope]
==== Pashhur Persecutes Jeremiah

@ Now Pashur the son of ##Immer##note:x[<<1 Chr 24:14; Ezra 2:37–38>>] the priest, who __was__ also ##chief##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:18>>] governor in the house of the LORD, heard that Jeremiah prophesied these things.
@ Then Pashur ##smote##note:x[<<2 Chr 16:10; Jer 1:19>>] Jeremiah the prophet, and put him in the stocks that __were__ ##in the high##note:x[<<Jer 37:13>>] gate of Benjamin, which __was__ by the house of the LORD.
@ And it came to pass on the morrow, that Pashur brought forth Jeremiah out of the stocks. Then said Jeremiah unto him, The LORD hath not called thy name Pashur, but note:x[<<Ps 31:13; Jer 20:10; 46:5>>]##Magor-missabib##note:tn[that is, Fear round about].
@ For thus saith the LORD, Behold, I will make thee a terror to thyself, and to all thy friends: and they shall fall by the sword of their enemies, and ##thine##note:x[<<Jer 29:21; 39:6–7>>] eyes shall behold __it:__ and ##I will give##note:x[<<Jer 25:9>>] all Judah into the hand of the king of Babylon, and he shall carry them captive into Babylon, and shall slay them with the sword.
@ Moreover ##I will deliver##note:x[<<Jer 15:13; Ezek 22:25>>] all the strength of this city, and all the labours thereof, and all the precious things thereof, and all the treasures of the kings of Judah will I give into the hand of their enemies, which shall spoil them, and take them, and carry them to Babylon.
@ And thou, Pashur, and all that dwell in thine house shall go into captivity: and thou shalt come to Babylon, and there thou shalt die, and shalt be buried there, thou, and all ##thy friends##note:x[<<Jer 14:14–15; Lam 2:14>>], to whom thou hast prophesied lies.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Complaint

@ ¶ O LORD, thou hast deceived me, and I was deceived: ##thou art##note:x[<<Jer 20:9>>] stronger than I, and hast prevailed: ##I am##note:x[<<Ps 22:6–7; Lam 3:14>>] in derision daily, every one mocketh me.note:tn[__was deceived__: or, was enticed]
@ For since I spake, I cried out, I cried violence and spoil; because ##the word##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:16; Jer 6:10>>] of the LORD was made a reproach unto me, and a derision, daily.
@ Then I said, I will not make mention of him, nor speak any more in his name. But __his word__ ##was in##note:x[<<Job 32:18–20; Ps 39:3; Acts 4:20>>] mine heart as a burning fire shut up in my bones, and I was weary with forbearing, and I could not __stay__.

@ ¶ For ##I heard##note:x[<<Ps 31:13>>] the defaming of many, fear on every side. ##Report##note:x[<<Isa 29:21>>], __say they__, and we will report it. ##All my familiars##note:x[<<Ps 41:9>>] watched for my halting, __saying__, Peradventure he will be enticed, and we shall prevail against him, and we shall take our revenge on him.note:tn[__All…__: Heb. Every man of my peace]
@ But ##the LORD##note:x[<<Jer 1:8, 19>>] __is__ with me as a mighty terrible one: therefore my persecutors shall stumble, ##and they##note:x[<<Jer 1:19>>] shall not prevail: they shall be greatly ashamed; for they shall not prosper: __their__ everlasting confusion shall never be forgotten.
@ But, O LORD of hosts, that triest the righteous, __and__ seest the reins and the heart, ##let me##note:x[<<Ps 54:7; Jer 11:20>>] see thy vengeance on them: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 62:8>>] unto thee have I opened my cause.
@ Sing unto the LORD, praise ye the LORD: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 35:9–11; 69:33; Isa 25:4>>] he hath delivered the soul of the poor from the hand of evildoers.

@ ¶ ##Cursed __be__ the day wherein I was born: let not the day wherein my mother bare me be blessed.##note:x[<<Job 3:3–16; Jer 15:10>>]
@ Cursed __be__ the man who brought tidings to my father, saying, A man child is born unto thee; making him very glad.
@ And let that man be as the cities which the LORD overthrew, and repented not: and ##let him##note:x[<<Jer 18:22>>] hear the cry in the morning, and the shouting at noontide;
@ Because ##he slew##note:x[<<Job 3:10–11; 10:18–19>>] me not from the womb; or that my mother might have been my grave, and her womb __to be__ always great __with me__.
@ Wherefore ##came##note:x[<<Job 3:20; 14:1; Lam 3:1>>] I forth out of the womb to see labour and sorrow, that my days should be consumed with shame?

=== @21

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jerusalem Will Fall to Babylon

@ The word which came unto Jeremiah from the LORD, when king Zedekiah sent unto him ##Pashur##note:x[<<Jer 38:1>>] the son of Melchiah, and ##Zephaniah##note:x[<<Jer 29:25; 37:3>>] the son of Maaseiah the priest, saying,
@ ##Enquire##note:x[<<Jer 37:3, 7; 42:4–6>>], I pray thee, of the LORD for us; for Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon maketh war against us; if so be that the LORD will deal with us according to all his wondrous works, that he may go up from us.

@ ¶ Then said Jeremiah unto them, Thus shall ye say to Zedekiah:
@ Thus saith the LORD God of Israel; ##Behold##note:x[<<Jer 32:5; 33:5; 37:8–10>>], I will turn back the weapons of war that __are__ in your hands, wherewith ye fight against the king of Babylon, and __against__ the Chaldeans, which besiege you without the walls, and I will assemble them into the midst of this city.
@ And ##I##note:x[<<Isa 63:10>>] myself will fight against you ##with an##note:x[<<Exod 6:6; Jer 32:17>>] outstretched hand and with a strong arm, even in anger, and in fury, and in great wrath.
@ And I will smite the inhabitants of this city, both man and beast: they shall die of a great pestilence.
@ And afterward, saith the LORD, ##I will##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:5–7; Jer 37:17>>] deliver Zedekiah king of Judah, and his servants, and the people, and such as are left in this city from the pestilence, from the sword, and from the famine, into the hand of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon, and into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of those that seek their life: and ##he shall##note:x[<<Hab 1:6–10>>] smite them with the edge of the sword; he shall not spare them, neither have pity, nor have mercy.

@ ¶ And unto this people thou shalt say, Thus saith the LORD; Behold, ##I set##note:x[<<Deut 11:26; 30:15, 19>>] before you the way of life, and the way of death.
@ He ##that abideth##note:x[<<Jer 38:2>>] in this city shall die by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence: but he that goeth out, and falleth to the Chaldeans that besiege you, he shall live, ##and his##note:x[<<Jer 38:2; 39:18>>] life shall be unto him for a prey.
@ For ##I have##note:x[<<Jer 44:11, 27; Amos 9:4>>] set my face against this city for evil, and not for good, saith the LORD: it shall be given into the hand of the king of Babylon, and he shall burn it with fire.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Message to the House of David

@ ¶ ##And touching the house of the king of Judah, __say__, Hear ye the word of the LORD;##note:x[<<Jer 17:20; Mic 3:1>>]
@ O house of David, thus saith the LORD; note:x[<<Isa 1:17>>]##Execute##note:tn[Heb. Judge] judgment in the morning, and ##deliver##note:x[<<Prov 31:8–9; Isa 1:17>>] __him that is__ spoiled out of the hand of the oppressor, lest my fury go out like fire, and burn that none can quench __it__, because of the evil of your doings.
@ Behold, ##I __am__##note:x[<<Jer 23:30–32>>] against thee, O ##inhabitant##note:tn[Heb. inhabitress] of the valley, __and__ rock of the plain, saith the LORD; which say, ##Who##note:x[<<Lam 4:12; Obad 1:3–4>>] shall come down against us? or who shall enter into our habitations?
@ But I will ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] you ##according##note:x[<<Prov 1:31; Isa 3:10–11; Jer 32:19>>] to the fruit of your doings, saith the LORD: and I will kindle a fire in the forest thereof, and it shall devour all things round about it.

=== @22

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning to Judah’s Kings

@ Thus saith the LORD; Go down to the house of the king of Judah, and speak there this word,
@ And say, Hear the word of the LORD, O king of Judah, ##that sittest##note:x[<<Luke 1:32>>] upon the throne of David, thou, and thy servants, and thy people that enter in by these gates:
@ Thus saith the LORD; ##Execute##note:x[<<Jer 21:12>>] ye judgment and righteousness, and deliver the spoiled out of the hand of the oppressor: and do no wrong, ##do no violence##note:x[<<Jas 1:27>>] to the stranger, the fatherless, nor the widow, ##neither##note:x[<<Jer 22:17>>] shed innocent blood in this place.
@ For if ye do this thing indeed, ##then##note:x[<<Jer 17:25>>] shall there enter in by the gates of this house kings sitting upon the throne of David, riding in chariots and on horses, he, and his servants, and his people.note:tn[__upon…__: Heb. for David upon his throne]
@ But ##if##note:x[<<Jer 17:27>>] ye will not hear these words, ##I##note:x[<<Gen 22:16; Amos 6:8>>] swear by myself, saith the LORD, that this house shall become a desolation.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning about the Palace

@ For thus saith the LORD unto the king’s house of Judah; Thou __art__ Gilead unto me, __and__ the head of Lebanon: __yet__ ##surely##note:x[<<Ps 107:34; Isa 6:11; Jer 9:11>>] I will make thee a wilderness, __and__ cities __which__ are not inhabited.
@ And ##I##note:x[<<Jer 4:6–7>>] will prepare destroyers against thee, every one with his weapons: and they shall ##cut##note:x[<<Isa 10:33–34; 37:24>>] down thy choice cedars, and cast __them__ into the fire.
@ ##And many nations shall pass by this city, and they shall say every man to his neighbour, Wherefore hath the LORD done thus unto this great city?##note:x[<<Deut 29:23–25; 1 Kgs 9:8–9; 2 Chr 7:20–22>>]
@ ##Then they shall answer, Because they have forsaken the covenant of the LORD their God, and worshipped other gods, and served them.##note:x[<<2 Kgs 22:17; 2 Chr 34:25>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning about Shallum

@ ¶ ##Weep ye##note:x[<<Eccl 4:2; Isa 57:1>>] not for the dead, neither bemoan him: __but__ ##weep sore##note:x[<<Jer 22:11>>] for him that goeth away: for he shall return no more, nor see his native country.
@ For thus saith the LORD touching ##Shallum##note:x[<<1 Chr 3:15>>] the son of Josiah king of Judah, which reigned instead of Josiah his father, which went forth out of this place; He shall not return thither any more:
@ ##But he shall die in the place whither they have led him captive, and shall see this land no more.##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:34>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning about Jehoiakim

@ ¶ Woe unto him that ##buildeth##note:x[<<Lev 19:13; Mic 3:10; Jas 5:4>>] his house by unrighteousness, and his chambers by wrong; __that__ useth his neighbour’s service without wages, and giveth him not for his work;
@ That saith, I will build me a wide house and note:x[<<2 Sam 7:2>>]##large##note:tn[Heb. through-aired] chambers, and cutteth him out ##windows##note:tn[or, my windows]; and __it is__ cieled with cedar, and painted with vermilion.
@ Shalt thou reign, because thou closest __thyself__ in cedar? did not ##thy##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:25>>] father eat and drink, ##and do##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:25>>] judgment and justice, __and__ ##then##note:x[<<Isa 3:10>>] __it was__ well with him?
@ He ##judged##note:x[<<Isa 1:17>>] the cause of the poor and needy; then __it was__ well __with him:__ ##__was__ not##note:x[<<Jer 9:24; 1 John 2:3–4>>] this to know me? saith the LORD.
@ But ##thine eyes##note:x[<<Ezek 19:6; 2 Pet 2:14>>] and thine heart __are__ not but for thy ##covetousness##note:x[<<2 Pet 2:14>>], and for to shed innocent blood, and for oppression, and for ##violence##note:tn[or, incursion], to do __it__.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD concerning Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah; ##They##note:x[<<Jer 16:6>>] shall not lament for him, __saying__, ##Ah my brother##note:x[<<1 Kgs 13:30>>]! or, Ah sister! they shall not lament for him, __saying__, Ah lord! or, Ah his glory!
@ ##He shall be buried with the burial of an ass, drawn and cast forth beyond the gates of Jerusalem.##note:x[<<Jer 36:30>>]

@ ¶ Go up to Lebanon, and cry; and lift up thy voice in Bashan, and cry from the passages: for all thy lovers are destroyed.
@ I spake unto thee in thy ##prosperity##note:tn[Heb. prosperities]; __but__ thou saidst, I will not hear. ##This##note:x[<<Jer 3:25; 7:22–28; 32:30>>] __hath been__ thy manner from thy youth, that thou obeyedst not my voice.
@ The ##wind##note:x[<<Hos 13:15>>] shall eat up all ##thy pastors##note:x[<<Jer 5:30–31; 10:21>>], and thy lovers shall go into captivity: surely then shalt thou be ashamed and confounded for all thy wickedness.
@ O ##inhabitant##note:tn[Heb. inhabitress] of Lebanon, that makest thy nest in the cedars, ##how##note:x[<<Jer 6:24>>] gracious shalt thou be when pangs come upon thee, the pain as of a woman in travail!

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning to Coniah

@ note:x[<<1 Chr 3:16>>]__As__ I live, saith the LORD, though ##Coniah##note:x[<<Jer 37:1>>] the son of Jehoiakim king of Judah were ##the signet##note:x[<<Song 8:6>>] upon my right hand, yet would I pluck thee thence;
@ And ##I##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:15–16; Jer 21:7; 34:20–21>>] will give thee into the hand of them that seek thy life, and into the hand __of them__ whose face thou fearest, even into the hand of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon, and into the hand of the Chaldeans.
@ ##And I will cast thee out, and thy mother that bare thee, into another country, where ye were not born; and there shall ye die.##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:15>>]
@ But to the land whereunto they ##desire##note:tn[Heb. lift up their mind] to return, thither shall they not return.
@ __Is__ this man Coniah ##a despised##note:x[<<Ps 31:12; Jer 48:38; Hos 8:8>>] broken idol? __is he__ a vessel wherein __is__ no pleasure? wherefore are they cast out, he and his seed, and are cast into a land which they know not?
@ ##O earth, earth, earth, hear the word of the LORD.##note:x[<<Deut 32:1; Jer 6:19; Mic 1:2>>]
@ Thus saith the LORD, Write ye this man childless, a man __that__ shall not prosper in his days: for no man of his seed shall prosper, ##sitting##note:x[<<Ps 94:20; Jer 36:30>>] upon the throne of David, and ruling any more in Judah.

=== @23

[discrete.pericope]
==== David’s Righteous Branch

@ ##Woe##note:x[<<Ezek 34:2>>] be unto the ##pastors##note:x[<<Jer 10:21; Zech 11:15–17>>] that destroy and scatter the sheep of my pasture! saith the LORD.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD God of Israel against the pastors that feed my people; Ye have scattered my flock, and driven them away, and have not visited them: behold, ##I##note:x[<<Mic 7:4>>] will visit upon you the evil of your doings, saith the LORD.
@ ##And I will gather the remnant of my flock out of all countries whither I have driven them, and will bring them again to their folds; and they shall be fruitful and increase.##note:x[<<Isa 43:5–6; Jer 32:37; Amos 9:14–15>>]
@ And ##I##note:x[<<Jer 3:14–15; Acts 20:28–29>>] will set up shepherds over them which shall feed them: and they shall fear no more, nor be dismayed, ##neither##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:5>>] shall they be lacking, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ Behold, the days come, saith the LORD, that I will raise unto David a righteous ##Branch##note:x[<<Isa 4:2>>], and a King shall ##reign##note:x[<<Isa 52:13; Luke 1:32–33>>] and prosper, and shall execute judgment and justice in the earth.
@ In his days Judah shall be saved, and Israel shall dwell safely: and this __is__ his name whereby he shall be called, ##THE LORD OUR RIGHTEOUSNESS##note:x[<<Jer 33:16; 1 Cor 1:30; Phil 3:9>>].note:tn[__THE LORD…__: Heb. Jehovah-tsidkenu]
@ ##Therefore, behold, the days come, saith the LORD, that they shall no more say, The LORD liveth, which brought up the children of Israel out of the land of Egypt;##note:x[<<Isa 43:18–19; Jer 16:14–15; 23:3>>]
@ ##But, The LORD liveth, which brought up and which led the seed of the house of Israel out of the north country, and from all countries whither I had driven them; and they shall dwell in their own land.##note:x[<<Isa 43:5–6; Ezek 34:13; Amos 9:14–15>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Lying Prophets

@ ¶ Mine ##heart##note:x[<<Hab 3:16>>] within me is broken because of the prophets; all my bones shake; I am like a drunken man, and like a man whom wine hath overcome, because of the LORD, and because of the words of his holiness.
@ For the land is ##full##note:x[<<Jer 5:7–8; Hos 4:2–3>>] of adulterers; for because of ##swearing##note:tn[or, cursing] the land mourneth; ##the pleasant##note:x[<<Ps 107:34>>] places of the wilderness are dried up, and their ##course##note:tn[or, violence] is evil, and their force __is__ not right.
@ For ##both##note:x[<<Jer 6:13; Zeph 3:4>>] prophet and priest are profane; yea, ##in##note:x[<<Matt 21:12–13>>] my house have I found their wickedness, saith the LORD.
@ Wherefore their way shall be unto them ##as##note:x[<<Ps 35:6; Prov 4:19; Jer 13:16>>] slippery __ways__ in the darkness: they shall be driven on, and fall therein: for I will bring evil upon them, __even__ the year of their visitation, saith the LORD.
@ ##And##note:x[<<Isa 9:16>>] I have seen ##folly##note:tn[or, an absurd thing: Heb. unsavoury] in the ##prophets##note:x[<<Hos 9:7–8>>] of Samaria; they ##prophesied##note:x[<<Jer 2:8>>] in Baal, and caused my people Israel to err.
@ I have seen also ##in the##note:x[<<Ezek 22:25>>] prophets of Jerusalem an horrible thing: ##they commit##note:x[<<Jer 29:23>>] adultery, and walk in lies: they strengthen also the hands of evildoers, that none doth return from his wickedness: they are all of them unto me as ##Sodom##note:x[<<Isa 1:9–10>>], and the inhabitants thereof as Gomorrah.note:tn[__an…__: or, filthiness]
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts concerning the prophets; Behold, I ##will##note:x[<<Jer 8:14; 9:15>>] feed them with wormwood, and make them drink the water of gall: for from the prophets of Jerusalem is ##profaneness##note:tn[or, hypocrisy] gone forth into all the land.
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, ##Hearken##note:x[<<Jer 27:9–10>>] not unto the words of the prophets that prophesy unto you: they make you vain: they speak ##a vision##note:x[<<Jer 14:14; Ezek 13:6>>] of their own heart, __and__ not out of the mouth of the LORD.
@ They say still unto them that despise me, The LORD hath said, ##Ye##note:x[<<Mic 3:11>>] shall have peace; and they say unto every one that walketh after the note:x[<<Jer 13:10>>]##imagination##note:tn[or, stubbornness] of his own heart, ##No##note:x[<<Mic 3:11>>] evil shall come upon you.
@ For ##who##note:x[<<Isa 40:13–14; Jer 23:22; 1 Cor 2:16>>] hath stood in the ##counsel##note:tn[or, secret] of the LORD, and hath perceived and heard his word? who hath marked his word, and heard __it__?
@ ##Behold, a whirlwind of the LORD is gone forth in fury, even a grievous whirlwind: it shall fall grievously upon the head of the wicked.##note:x[<<Jer 25:32; 30:23; Amos 1:14>>]
@ The anger of the LORD shall not return, ##until##note:x[<<Jer 30:24>>] he have executed, and till he have performed the thoughts of his heart: in the latter days ye shall consider it perfectly.
@ ##I have not sent these prophets, yet they ran: I have not spoken to them, yet they prophesied.##note:x[<<Jer 14:14; 23:32; 27:15>>]
@ But ##if##note:x[<<Jer 23:18>>] they had stood in my counsel, and had caused my people to hear my words, ##then##note:x[<<Jer 25:5; Zech 1:4>>] they should have turned them from their evil way, and from the evil of their doings.
@ ##__Am__ I a God at hand, saith the LORD, and not a God afar off?##note:x[<<Ps 139:1–10>>]
@ Can any ##hide##note:x[<<Ps 90:8; 139:7; Prov 15:3>>] himself in secret places that I shall not see him? saith the LORD. Do not I fill heaven and earth? saith the LORD.
@ I have heard what the prophets said, that prophesy lies in my name, saying, I have ##dreamed##note:x[<<Jer 23:28, 32; 29:8>>], I have dreamed.
@ How long shall __this__ be in the heart of the prophets that prophesy lies? yea, __they are__ ##prophets of##note:x[<<Jer 14:14; 1 Tim 4:1–2>>] the deceit of their own heart;
@ Which think to cause my people to forget my name by their dreams which they tell every man to his neighbour, ##as##note:x[<<Judg 3:7; 8:33–34>>] their fathers have forgotten my name for Baal.
@ The prophet that hath a dream, let him tell a dream; and he that hath my word, let him ##speak##note:x[<<Prov 14:5; 2 Cor 2:17; 1 Tim 1:12>>] my word faithfully. What __is__ the chaff to the wheat? saith the LORD.note:tn[__that hath a dream__: Heb. with whom is, etc]
@ __Is__ not my word ##like as##note:x[<<Jer 5:14; Luke 24:32; Heb 4:12>>] a fire? saith the LORD; and like a hammer __that__ breaketh the rock in pieces?
@ ##Therefore, behold, I __am__ against the prophets, saith the LORD, that steal my words every one from his neighbour.##note:x[<<Deut 18:20; Jer 14:14–15; Ezek 13:8>>]
@ Behold, I __am__ against the prophets, saith the LORD, that use their tongues, and say, ##He##note:x[<<Jer 23:17>>] saith.note:tn[__that…__: or, that smooth their tongues]
@ Behold, I __am__ against them that prophesy false dreams, saith the LORD, and do tell them, and cause my people ##to err##note:x[<<Ezek 13:7–18>>] by their lies, ##and by##note:x[<<Zeph 3:4>>] their lightness; yet I sent them not, nor commanded them: ##therefore##note:x[<<Lam 2:14>>] they shall not profit this people at all, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== False Prophecies

@ ¶ And when this people, or the prophet, or a priest, shall ask thee, saying, ##What##note:x[<<Mal 1:1>>] __is__ the burden of the LORD? thou shalt then say unto them, What burden? I will even forsake you, saith the LORD.
@ And __as for__ the prophet, and the priest, and the people, that shall say, The burden of the LORD, I will even ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] that man and his house.
@ Thus shall ye say every one to his neighbour, and every one to his brother, What hath the LORD answered? and, What hath the LORD spoken?
@ And the burden ##of the##note:x[<<Jer 10:10>>] LORD shall ye mention no more: ##for every##note:x[<<Isa 3:8>>] man’s word shall be his burden; ##for ye##note:x[<<2 Pet 3:16>>] have perverted the words of the living God, of the LORD of hosts our God.
@ Thus shalt thou say to the prophet, What hath the LORD answered thee? and, What hath the LORD spoken?
@ But since ye say, The burden of the LORD; therefore thus saith the LORD; Because ye say this word, The burden of the LORD, and I have sent unto you, saying, Ye shall not say, The burden of the LORD;
@ Therefore, behold, I, even I, will utterly forget you, ##and I##note:x[<<Jer 23:33>>] will forsake you, and the city that I gave you and your fathers, __and cast you__ out of my presence:
@ ##And I will bring an everlasting reproach upon you, and a perpetual shame, which shall not be forgotten.##note:x[<<Jer 20:11; Ezek 5:14–15>>]

=== @24

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Good and Bad Figs

@ The LORD shewed me, and, behold, two baskets of figs __were__ set before the temple of the LORD, ##after##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:10; Jer 29:2>>] that Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon had carried away captive Jeconiah the son of Jehoiakim king of Judah, and the princes of Judah, with the carpenters and smiths, from Jerusalem, and had brought them to Babylon.
@ One basket __had__ very good figs, __even__ like the figs __that are__ first ripe: and the other basket __had__ very ##naughty##note:x[<<Isa 5:4, 7>>] figs, which could not be eaten, they were so bad.note:tn[__they…__: Heb. for badness]
@ Then said the LORD unto me, ##What##note:x[<<Amos 8:2>>] seest thou, Jeremiah? And I said, Figs; the good figs, very good; and the evil, very evil, that cannot be eaten, they are so evil.

@ ¶ Again the word of the LORD came unto me, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel; Like these good figs, so will ##I acknowledge##note:x[<<Zech 13:9>>] them that are carried away captive of Judah, whom I have sent out of this place into the land of the Chaldeans for __their__ good.note:tn[__them…__: Heb. the captivity]
@ For I will set mine eyes upon them for good, ##and I will bring##note:x[<<Jer 29:10>>] them again to this land: and ##I will build##note:x[<<Jer 33:7; 42:10>>] them, and not pull __them__ down; and I will plant them, and not pluck __them__ up.
@ And I will give them an heart to know me, that I __am__ the LORD: ##and they##note:x[<<Jer 30:22; Ezek 37:23; Heb 8:10>>] shall be my people, and I will be their God: for they shall return unto me with their whole heart.

@ ¶ And as the evil figs, which cannot be eaten, they are so evil; surely thus saith the LORD, So will I give Zedekiah the king of Judah, and his princes, and the residue of Jerusalem, that remain in this land, ##and them##note:x[<<Jer 43:1–13>>] that dwell in the land of Egypt:
@ And I will deliver them ##to be removed##note:x[<<Deut 28:37; Jer 15:4>>] into all the kingdoms of the earth for __their__ hurt, __to be__ a reproach and a proverb, a taunt and ##a curse##note:x[<<Jer 29:18>>], in all places whither I shall drive them.note:tn[__to be removed__: Heb. for removing, or, vexation]
@ ##And I will send the sword, the famine, and the pestilence, among them, till they be consumed from off the land that I gave unto them and to their fathers.##note:x[<<Isa 51:19>>]

=== @25

[discrete.pericope]
==== Seventy Years of Captivity

@ The word that came to Jeremiah concerning all the people of Judah ##in the##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:1–2; Jer 36:1>>] fourth year of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah, that __was__ the first year of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon;
@ ##The which Jeremiah the prophet spake unto all the people of Judah, and to all the inhabitants of Jerusalem, saying,##note:x[<<Jer 18:11>>]
@ From the ##thirteenth##note:x[<<Jer 1:2>>] year of Josiah the son of Amon king of Judah, even unto this day, that __is__ the three and twentieth year, the word of the LORD hath come unto me, and I have spoken unto you, ##rising##note:x[<<Jer 7:13; 11:7>>] early and speaking; but ye have not hearkened.
@ And the LORD hath sent unto you all his servants the prophets, rising early and sending __them;__ but ##ye##note:x[<<Jer 25:3>>] have not hearkened, nor inclined your ear to hear.
@ They said, ##Turn##note:x[<<Isa 55:6–7>>] ye again now every one from his evil way, and from the evil of your doings, and dwell in the land that the LORD hath given unto you and to your fathers ##for##note:x[<<Jer 7:7; 17:25>>] ever and ever:
@ ##And go not after other gods to serve them, and to worship them, and provoke me not to anger with the works of your hands; and I will do you no hurt.##note:x[<<Deut 8:19>>]
@ Yet ye have not hearkened unto me, saith the LORD; ##that ye##note:x[<<2 Kgs 21:15; Prov 8:36>>] might provoke me to anger with the works of your hands to your own hurt.

@ ¶ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts; Because ye have not heard my words,
@ Behold, ##I##note:x[<<Jer 1:15>>] will send and take all the families of the north, saith the LORD, and ##Nebuchadrezzar##note:x[<<Isa 13:3>>] the king of Babylon, my servant, and will bring them against this land, and against the inhabitants thereof, and against all these nations round about, and will utterly destroy them, and make them ##an astonishment##note:x[<<Jer 18:16>>], and an hissing, and perpetual desolations.
@ Moreover I will take from them the ##voice of mirth##note:x[<<Jer 7:34; 16:9; Ezek 26:13>>], and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride, the sound of the millstones, and the light of the candle.note:tn[__I will…__: Heb. I will cause to perish from them]
@ And this whole land shall be a desolation, __and__ an astonishment; and these nations shall serve the king of Babylon ##seventy##note:x[<<Jer 25:12; Dan 9:2; Zech 1:12>>] years.

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass, ##when##note:x[<<Jer 29:10; Dan 9:2>>] seventy years are accomplished, __that__ I will ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] the king of Babylon, and that nation, saith the LORD, for their iniquity, and the land of the Chaldeans, and will make it ##perpetual##note:x[<<Isa 13:19>>] desolations.
@ And I will bring upon that land all my words which I have pronounced against it, __even__ all that is written in this book, which Jeremiah hath prophesied against all the nations.
@ For ##many##note:x[<<Jer 27:7; 51:6>>] nations and great kings shall serve themselves of them also: and ##I##note:x[<<Jer 51:6>>] will recompense them according to their deeds, and according to the works of their own hands.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Cup of God’s Wrath

@ ¶ For thus saith the LORD God of Israel unto me; ##Take##note:x[<<Job 21:20; Ps 75:8; Isa 51:17>>] the wine cup of this fury at my hand, and cause all the nations, to whom I send thee, to drink it.
@ ##And they shall drink, and be moved, and be mad, because of the sword that I will send among them.##note:x[<<Jer 51:7; Nah 3:11>>]
@ Then took I the cup at the LORD’s hand, ##and made##note:x[<<Jer 1:10; 25:28>>] all the nations to drink, unto whom the LORD had sent me:
@ __To wit__, Jerusalem, and the cities of Judah, and the kings thereof, and the princes thereof, ##to make##note:x[<<Jer 24:9>>] them a desolation, an astonishment, an hissing, and a curse; ##as __it__##note:x[<<Jer 44:22>>] __is__ this day;
@ Pharaoh king of Egypt, and his servants, and his princes, and all his people;
@ And all ##the mingled##note:x[<<Jer 50:37; Ezek 30:5>>] people, and all the kings of the land of ##Uz##note:x[<<Job 1:1>>], and all the kings of the land of the Philistines, and Ashkelon, and Azzah, and Ekron, and the remnant of Ashdod,
@ Edom, and ##Moab##note:x[<<Jer 48:1–47>>], and the children of Ammon,
@ And all the kings of ##Tyrus##note:x[<<Jer 47:4>>], and all the kings of Zidon, and the kings of the ##isles##note:tn[or, region by the sea side] which __are__ beyond the sea,
@ ##Dedan##note:x[<<Jer 49:8>>], and Tema, and Buz, and all __that are__ ##in the utmost corners##note:x[<<Jer 9:26; 49:32>>],note:tn[__that…__: Heb. cut off into corners, or, having the corners of the hair polled]
@ And all the kings of ##Arabia##note:x[<<2 Chr 9:14>>], and all the kings of ##the mingled##note:x[<<Jer 25:20>>] people that dwell in the desert,
@ note:x[<<Gen 10:22; Isa 11:11>>]And all the kings of Zimri, and all the kings of Elam, and all the kings of the Medes,
@ And all the kings of the north, far and near, one with another, and all the kingdoms of the world, which __are__ upon the face of the earth: ##and the##note:x[<<Jer 51:41>>] king of Sheshach shall drink after them.
@ Therefore thou shalt say unto them, Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Drink##note:x[<<Hab 2:16>>] ye, and be drunken, and spue, and fall, and rise no more, ##because##note:x[<<Jer 25:16>>] of the sword which I will send among you.
@ And it shall be, if they refuse to take the cup at thine hand to drink, then shalt thou say unto them, Thus saith the LORD of hosts; Ye shall certainly drink.
@ For, lo, ##I begin##note:x[<<Prov 11:31; Ezek 38:21; 1 Pet 4:17>>] to bring evil on the city which is called by my name, and should ye be utterly unpunished? Ye shall not be unpunished: for I will call for a sword upon all the inhabitants of the earth, saith the LORD of hosts.note:tn[__which…__: Heb. upon which my name is called]

@ Therefore prophesy thou against them all these words, and say unto them, The LORD shall ##roar##note:x[<<Isa 42:13; Joel 3:16; Amos 1:2>>] from on high, and utter his voice from his holy habitation; he shall mightily roar upon his habitation; he shall give a shout, as they that tread __the grapes__, against all the inhabitants of the earth.
@ ##A noise##note:x[<<Hos 4:1; Mic 6:2>>] shall come __even__ to the ends of the earth; for the LORD hath a controversy with the nations, he will ##plead##note:x[<<Joel 3:2>>] with all flesh; he will give them __that are__ wicked to the sword, saith the LORD.
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, Behold, ##evil##note:x[<<2 Chr 15:6; Isa 34:2>>] shall go forth from nation to nation, ##and a##note:x[<<Jer 23:19>>] great whirlwind shall be raised up from the coasts of the earth.
@ And ##the slain##note:x[<<Isa 66:16>>] of the LORD shall be at that day from __one__ end of the earth even unto the __other__ end of the earth: ##they shall not##note:x[<<Ps 79:3; Jer 8:2>>] be lamented, neither gathered, nor buried; they shall be dung upon the ground.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Cry of the Shepherds

@ ¶ Howl, ye shepherds, and cry; and ##wallow##note:x[<<Jer 6:26>>] yourselves __in the ashes__, ye principal of the flock: for the days of your slaughter and of your dispersions are accomplished; and ye shall fall like a pleasant vessel.note:tn[__the days…__: Heb. your days for slaughter]note:tn[__a pleasant…__: Heb. a vessel of desire]
@ And ##the shepherds##note:x[<<Job 11:20>>] shall have no way to flee, nor the principal of the flock to escape.note:tn[__the shepherds…__: Heb. flight shall perish from the shepherds, and escaping from, etc]
@ A voice of the cry of the shepherds, and an howling of the principal of the flock, __shall be heard:__ for the LORD hath spoiled their pasture.
@ And the peaceable habitations are cut down because of the fierce anger of the LORD.
@ He ##hath##note:x[<<Jer 4:7>>] forsaken his covert, as the lion: for their land is ##desolate##note:tn[Heb. a desolation] because of the fierceness of the oppressor, and because of his fierce anger.

=== @26

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning to the Cities of Judah

@ ##In the beginning of the reign of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah came this word from the LORD, saying,##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:4–5>>]
@ Thus saith the LORD; Stand in the court of the LORD’s house, and speak unto all the cities of Judah, which come to worship in the LORD’s house, ##all the words##note:x[<<Matt 28:20; Acts 20:27>>] that I command thee to speak unto them; ##diminish##note:x[<<Deut 4:2>>] not a word:
@ If ##so##note:x[<<Isa 1:16–19; Jer 18:7–10; 36:3>>] be they will hearken, and turn every man from his evil way, that I may repent me of the evil, which I purpose to do unto them because of the evil of their doings.
@ And thou shalt say unto them, Thus saith the LORD; ##If##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:6>>] ye will not hearken to me, to walk in my law, which I have set before you,
@ To hearken to the words of ##my##note:x[<<Jer 7:13, 25; 11:7>>] servants the prophets, whom I sent unto you, both rising up early, and sending __them__, but ye have not hearkened;
@ Then will I make this house like Shiloh, and will make this city ##a curse##note:x[<<2 Kgs 22:19; Jer 24:9; 25:18>>] to all the nations of the earth.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Threatened with Death

@ So the priests and the prophets and all the people heard Jeremiah speaking these words in the house of the LORD.

@ ¶ Now it came to pass, when Jeremiah had made an end of speaking all that the LORD had commanded __him__ to speak unto all the people, that the priests and the prophets and all the people took him, saying, Thou shalt surely die.
@ Why hast thou prophesied in the name of the LORD, saying, This house shall be like Shiloh, and this city shall be desolate without an inhabitant? And all the people were gathered against Jeremiah in the house of the LORD.

@ ¶ When the princes of Judah heard these things, then they came up from the king’s house unto the house of the LORD, and sat down in the entry of ##the new##note:x[<<Jer 36:10>>] gate of the LORD’s __house__.note:tn[__in the…__: or, at the door]
@ Then spake the priests and the prophets unto the princes and to all the people, ##saying##note:x[<<Deut 18:20; Matt 26:66>>], This man __is__ worthy to die; ##for he##note:x[<<Jer 38:4>>] hath prophesied against this city, as ye have heard with your ears.note:tn[__This man…__: Heb. The judgment of death is for this man]

@ ¶ Then spake Jeremiah unto all the princes and to all the people, saying, ##The LORD##note:x[<<Jer 26:15; Acts 4:19; 5:29>>] sent me to prophesy against this house and against this city all the words that ye have heard.
@ Therefore now ##amend##note:x[<<Isa 1:19>>] your ways and your doings, and obey the voice of the LORD your God; and the LORD will ##repent##note:x[<<Jonah 3:9; 4:2>>] him of the evil that he hath pronounced against you.
@ ##As for##note:x[<<Josh 9:25; Jer 38:5>>] me, behold, I __am__ in your hand: do with me ##as seemeth good and meet unto you##note:x[<<2 Sam 15:26>>].note:tn[__as…__: Heb. as it is good and right in your eyes]
@ But know ye for certain, that if ye put me to death, ##ye shall##note:x[<<Num 35:33; Prov 6:17>>] surely bring innocent blood upon yourselves, and upon this city, and upon the inhabitants thereof: for of a truth the LORD hath sent me unto you to speak all these words in your ears.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Spared from Death

@ ¶ ##Then said the princes and all the people unto the priests and to the prophets; This man __is__ not worthy to die: for he hath spoken to us in the name of the LORD our God.##note:x[<<Acts 5:34–39; 23:9, 29>>]
@ ##Then rose##note:x[<<Acts 5:34>>] up certain of the elders of the land, and spake to all the assembly of the people, saying,
@ ##Micah##note:x[<<Mic 1:1>>] the Morasthite prophesied in the days of Hezekiah king of Judah, and spake to all the people of Judah, saying, Thus saith the LORD of hosts; Zion shall be plowed __like__ a field, and ##Jerusalem##note:x[<<Neh 4:2>>] shall become heaps, and ##the mountain##note:x[<<Zech 8:3>>] of the house as the high places of a forest.
@ Did Hezekiah king of Judah and all Judah put him at all to death? ##did he##note:x[<<2 Chr 29:6–11>>] not fear the LORD, and ##besought the LORD##note:x[<<Exod 32:14; 2 Sam 24:16>>], and the LORD repented him of the evil which he had pronounced against them? Thus might we procure great evil against our souls.note:tn[__the LORD, and the__: Heb. the face of the LORD, etc]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Prophet Uriah

@ And there was also a man that prophesied in the name of the LORD, Urijah the son of Shemaiah of ##Kirjath-jearim##note:x[<<1 Sam 7:2>>], who prophesied against this city and against this land according to all the words of Jeremiah:
@ And when Jehoiakim the king, with all his mighty men, and all the princes, heard his words, the king sought to put him to death: but when Urijah heard it, ##he was##note:x[<<Matt 10:23>>] afraid, and fled, and went into Egypt;
@ And Jehoiakim the king sent men into Egypt, __namely__, Elnathan the son of Achbor, and __certain__ men with him into Egypt.
@ And they fetched forth Urijah out of Egypt, and brought him unto Jehoiakim the king; ##who##note:x[<<Jer 2:30>>] slew him with the sword, and cast his dead body into the graves of the common people.note:tn[__common…__: Heb. sons of the people]
@ Nevertheless the hand of Ahikam the son of Shaphan was with Jeremiah, that they should not give him into the hand of the people to put him to death.

=== @27

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Yoke of Nebuchadnezzar

@ In the beginning of the reign of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah came this word unto Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD to me; Make thee bonds and yokes, and put them upon thy neck,note:tn[__saith…__: or, hath the LORD said]
@ And send them to the king of Edom, and to the king of Moab, and to the king of the Ammonites, and to the king of Tyrus, and to the king of Zidon, by the hand of the messengers which come to Jerusalem unto Zedekiah king of Judah;
@ And command them to say unto their masters, Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Thus shall ye say unto your masters;note:tn[__to say…__: or, concerning their masters, saying]
@ I have ##made##note:x[<<Jer 32:17; 51:15>>] the earth, the man and the beast that __are__ upon the ground, by my great power and by my outstretched arm, ##and have##note:x[<<Ps 115:15–16>>] given it unto whom it seemed meet unto me.
@ ##And##note:x[<<Jer 28:14>>] now have ##I given all##note:x[<<Jer 28:14>>] these lands into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar the king of Babylon, ##my##note:x[<<Jer 25:9>>] servant; and the beasts of the field have I given him also to serve him.
@ And ##all##note:x[<<Jer 52:31>>] nations shall serve him, and his son, and his son’s son, until the very time of his land come: and then many nations and great kings shall serve themselves of him.
@ And it shall come to pass, __that__ the nation and kingdom which will not serve the same Nebuchadnezzar the king of Babylon, and that will not put their neck under the yoke of the king of Babylon, that nation will I punish, saith the LORD, with the sword, and with the famine, and with the pestilence, until I have consumed them by his hand.
@ Therefore ##hearken##note:x[<<Isa 8:19; Jer 14:14; 29:8>>] not ye to your prophets, nor to your diviners, nor to your ##dreamers##note:tn[Heb. dreams], nor to your enchanters, nor to your sorcerers, which speak unto you, saying, Ye shall not serve the king of Babylon:
@ For they prophesy a lie unto you, to remove you far from your land; and that I should drive you out, and ye should perish.
@ note:x[<<Jer 27:2, 8>>]But the nations that bring their neck under the yoke of the king of Babylon, and serve him, ##those##note:x[<<Jer 21:9>>] will I let remain still in their own land, saith the LORD; and they shall till it, and dwell therein.

@ ¶ I spake also to ##Zedekiah##note:x[<<Jer 28:1; 38:17>>] king of Judah according to all these words, saying, Bring your necks under the yoke of the king of Babylon, and serve him and his people, and live.
@ ##Why##note:x[<<Ezek 18:31>>] will ye die, thou and thy people, ##by the sword##note:x[<<Jer 27:8>>], by the famine, and by the pestilence, as the LORD hath spoken against the nation that will not serve the king of Babylon?
@ Therefore hearken not unto the words of the prophets that speak unto you, saying, Ye shall not serve the king of Babylon: for ##they##note:x[<<Jer 14:14>>] prophesy a lie unto you.
@ For I have not sent them, saith the LORD, yet they prophesy a lie in my name; ##that I##note:x[<<Jer 27:10>>] might drive you out, and that ye might perish, ye, and the prophets that prophesy unto you.note:tn[__a lie__: Heb. in a lie, or, lyingly]
@ Also I spake to the priests and to all this people, saying, Thus saith the LORD; Hearken not to the words of your prophets that prophesy unto you, saying, ##Behold##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:13; Jer 28:3; Dan 1:2>>], the vessels of the LORD’s house shall now shortly be brought again from Babylon: for they prophesy a lie unto you.
@ Hearken not unto them; serve the king of Babylon, and live: wherefore should this city be laid waste?
@ But if they __be__ prophets, and if the word of the LORD be with them, ##let them##note:x[<<1 Sam 7:8>>] now make intercession to the LORD of hosts, that the vessels which are left in the house of the LORD, and __in__ the house of the king of Judah, and at Jerusalem, go not to Babylon.

@ ¶ For thus saith the LORD of hosts concerning ##the pillars##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:13; Jer 52:17–23>>], and concerning the sea, and concerning the bases, and concerning the residue of the vessels that remain in this city,
@ note:x[<<2 Chr 36:10>>]Which Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon took not, when he carried away captive Je##coniah##note:x[<<Jer 24:1>>] the son of Jehoiakim king of Judah from Jerusalem to Babylon, and all the nobles of Judah and Jerusalem;
@ Yea, thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, concerning the vessels that remain __in__ the house of the LORD, and __in__ the house of the king of Judah and of Jerusalem;
@ They shall be ##carried##note:x[<<Jer 29:10>>] to Babylon, and there shall they be ##until##note:x[<<Jer 29:10; 32:5>>] the day that I visit them, saith the LORD; then will I bring them up, and restore them to this place.

=== @28

[discrete.pericope]
==== Hananiah’s False Prophecy

@ And it came to pass ##the same##note:x[<<Jer 27:1>>] year, in the beginning of the reign of Zedekiah king of Judah, in the fourth year, __and__ in the fifth month, __that__ Hananiah the son of Azur the prophet, which __was__ of Gibeon, spake unto me in the house of the LORD, in the presence of the priests and of all the people, saying,
@ Thus speaketh the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, saying, I have broken the yoke of the king of Babylon.
@ Within two full years will I bring again into this place all the vessels of the LORD’s house, ##that##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:13>>] Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon took away from this place, and carried them to Babylon&#58;note:tn[__two…__: Heb. two years of days]
@ And I will bring again to this place ##Jeconiah##note:x[<<Jer 22:24>>] the son of Jehoiakim king of Judah, with all the ##captives##note:tn[Heb. captivity] of Judah, that went into Babylon, saith the LORD: for I will break the yoke of the king of Babylon.

@ ¶ Then the prophet Jeremiah said unto the prophet Hananiah in the presence of the priests, and in the presence of all the people that stood in the house of the LORD,
@ Even the prophet Jeremiah said, ##Amen##note:x[<<1 Kgs 1:36; Ps 41:13>>]: the LORD do so: the LORD perform thy words which thou hast prophesied, to bring again the vessels of the LORD’s house, and all that is carried away captive, from Babylon into this place.
@ Nevertheless hear thou now this word that I speak in thine ears, and in the ears of all the people;
@ The prophets that have been before me and before thee of old ##prophesied##note:x[<<Deut 4:26–27; 1 Kgs 17:1; 22:8>>] both against many countries, and against great kingdoms, of war, and of evil, and of pestilence.
@ The prophet ##which##note:x[<<Jer 6:14>>] prophesieth of peace, when the word of the prophet shall come to pass, ##__then__##note:x[<<Deut 18:22>>] shall the prophet be known, that the LORD hath truly sent him.

@ ¶ Then Hananiah the prophet ##took##note:x[<<Jer 27:2>>] the yoke from off the prophet Jeremiah’s neck, and brake it.
@ And Hananiah spake in the presence of all the people, saying, Thus saith the LORD; Even so will I break the yoke of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon from the neck of all nations within the space of two full years. And the prophet Jeremiah went his way.

@ ¶ Then the word of the LORD came unto Jeremiah __the prophet__, after that Hananiah the prophet had broken the yoke from off the neck of the prophet Jeremiah, saying,
@ Go and tell Hananiah, saying, Thus saith the LORD; Thou hast broken the yokes of wood; but thou shalt make for them yokes of iron.
@ For thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##I have put##note:x[<<Deut 28:48>>] a yoke of iron upon the neck of all these nations, that they may serve Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon; and they shall serve him: ##and I##note:x[<<Jer 27:6–7>>] have given him the beasts of the field also.

@ ¶ Then said the prophet Jeremiah unto Hananiah the prophet, Hear now, Hananiah; ##The LORD##note:x[<<Jer 14:14–15; 23:21; 27:15>>] hath not sent thee; but thou makest this people to trust in a lie.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD; Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Gen 7:4>>] cast thee from off the face of the earth: this year thou shalt die, because thou hast taught note:x[<<Jer 29:32>>]##rebellion##note:tn[Heb. revolt] against the LORD.
@ So Hananiah the prophet died the same year in the seventh month.

=== @29

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Letter to the Exiles

@ Now these __are__ the words ##of the letter##note:x[<<Esth 9:20; Jer 29:25–29; Acts 15:23>>] that Jeremiah the prophet sent from Jerusalem unto the residue of the elders which were carried away captives, and to the priests, and to the prophets, and to all the people whom Nebuchadnezzar had carried away captive from Jerusalem to Babylon;
@ ( After that ##Jeconiah##note:x[<<Jer 22:24–28>>] the king, and the queen, and the ##eunuchs##note:tn[or, chamberlains], the princes of Judah and Jerusalem, and the carpenters, and the smiths, were departed from Jerusalem;)
@ By the hand of Elasah the son of Shaphan, and Gemariah the son of Hilkiah, (whom Zedekiah king of Judah sent unto Babylon to Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon) saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, unto all that are carried away captives, ##whom##note:x[<<Jer 24:5>>] I have caused to be carried away from Jerusalem unto Babylon;
@ ##Build ye houses, and dwell __in them;__ and plant gardens, and eat the fruit of them;##note:x[<<Jer 29:10, 28>>]
@ Take ye wives, and beget sons and daughters; and ##take wives##note:x[<<Gen 21:21; 24:3–4, 51>>] for your sons, and give your daughters to husbands, that they may bear sons and daughters; that ye may be increased there, and not diminished.
@ And ##seek##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:13–17>>] the peace of the city whither I have caused you to be carried away captives, and ##pray##note:x[<<Ezra 6:10; 1 Tim 2:1–2>>] unto the LORD for it: for in the peace thereof shall ye have peace.

@ ¶ For thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Let##note:x[<<Eph 5:6; 2 Thess 2:9–11>>] not your prophets and your diviners, that __be__ in the midst of you, deceive you, neither hearken to ##your dreams##note:x[<<Jer 5:31>>] which ye cause to be dreamed.
@ For they prophesy note:x[<<Jer 27:15; 29:31>>]##falsely##note:tn[Heb. in a lie] unto you in my name: I have not sent them, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ For thus saith the LORD, That ##after##note:x[<<Dan 9:2; Zech 7:5>>] seventy years be accomplished at Babylon ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 24:6–7>>] visit you, and perform my good word toward you, in causing you to return to this place.
@ For ##I know##note:x[<<Ps 33:11; Isa 55:8–12; Mic 4:12>>] the thoughts that I think toward you, saith the LORD, thoughts of peace, and not of evil, to give you an expected end.note:tn[__expected…__: Heb. end and expectation]
@ ##Then shall ye call upon me, and ye shall go and pray unto me, and I will hearken unto you.##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; Jer 33:3; Matt 7:7–8>>]
@ And ##ye shall##note:x[<<Deut 4:29–31>>] seek me, and find __me__, when ye shall search for me ##with##note:x[<<Ps 119:2, 10>>] all your heart.
@ And ##I will be##note:x[<<1 Chr 28:9>>] found of you, saith the LORD: ##and I will turn##note:x[<<Jer 30:3; Ezek 36:1–38>>] away your captivity, and I will gather you from all the nations, and from all the places whither I have driven you, saith the LORD; and I will bring you again into the place whence I caused you to be carried away captive.

@ ¶ Because ye have said, The LORD hath raised us up prophets in Babylon;
@ __Know__ that thus saith the LORD of the king that sitteth upon the throne of David, and of all the people that dwelleth in this city, __and__ of your brethren that are not gone forth with you into captivity;
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts; ##Behold##note:x[<<Jer 24:8–10>>], I will send upon them the sword, the famine, and the pestilence, and will make ##them like##note:x[<<Jer 24:8>>] vile figs, that cannot be eaten, they are so evil.
@ And I will persecute them with the sword, with the famine, and with the pestilence, and ##will deliver##note:x[<<2 Chr 29:8; Jer 15:4>>] them to be removed to all the kingdoms of the earth, ##to be a curse##note:x[<<Jer 42:18>>], and an astonishment, and an hissing, and a reproach, among all the nations whither I have driven them&#58;note:tn[__to be a curse__: Heb. for a curse]
@ ##Because they have not hearkened to my words, saith the LORD, which I sent unto them by my servants the prophets, rising up early and sending __them;__ but ye would not hear, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Jer 6:19; 7:13; 26:5>>]

@ ¶ Hear ye therefore the word of the LORD, all ye of the captivity, ##whom##note:x[<<Jer 24:5>>] I have sent from Jerusalem to Babylon:
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, of Ahab the son of Kolaiah, and of Zedekiah the son of Maaseiah, which prophesy a lie unto you in my name; Behold, I will deliver them into the hand of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon; and he shall slay them before your eyes;
@ And of them ##shall be##note:x[<<Gen 48:20; Isa 65:15>>] taken up a curse by all the captivity of Judah which __are__ in Babylon, saying, The LORD make thee like Zedekiah and like Ahab, whom the king of Babylon ##roasted##note:x[<<Dan 3:6>>] in the fire;
@ Because they have committed villany in Israel, ##and have##note:x[<<Jer 23:14>>] committed adultery with their neighbours’ wives, and have spoken lying words in my name, which I have not commanded them; ##even I##note:x[<<Mal 3:5; Heb 4:13>>] know, and __am__ a witness, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Message to Shemaiah

@ ¶ __Thus__ shalt thou also speak to ##Shemaiah##note:x[<<Jer 29:31–32>>] the ##Nehelamite##note:tn[or, dreamer], saying,
@ Thus speaketh the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, saying, Because thou hast sent letters in thy name unto all the people that __are__ at Jerusalem, and to Zephaniah the son of Maaseiah the priest, and to all the priests, saying,
@ The LORD hath made thee priest in the stead of Jehoiada the priest, that ye should be ##officers##note:x[<<Jer 20:1–2>>] in the house of the LORD, ##for every##note:x[<<2 Kgs 9:11>>] man __that is__ mad, and maketh himself a prophet, ##that thou##note:x[<<Jer 20:1–2>>] shouldest put him in prison, and in the stocks.
@ Now therefore why hast thou not reproved Jeremiah of Anathoth, which maketh himself a prophet to you?
