@ For therefore he sent unto us __in__ Babylon, saying, This __captivity is__ long: build ye houses, and dwell __in them;__ and plant gardens, and eat the fruit of them.
@ And Zephaniah the priest read this letter in the ears of Jeremiah the prophet.

@ ¶ Then came the word of the LORD unto Jeremiah, saying,
@ Send to all them of the captivity, saying, Thus saith the LORD concerning Shemaiah the Nehelamite; ##Because##note:x[<<Jer 29:9>>] that Shemaiah hath prophesied unto you, and I sent him not, and he caused you to trust in a lie:
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD; ##Behold##note:x[<<2 Kgs 7:19–20; Jer 17:6>>], I will punish Shemaiah the Nehelamite, and his seed: he shall not have a man to dwell among this people; neither shall he behold the good that I will do for my people, saith the LORD; because he hath taught note:x[<<Jer 28:16>>]##rebellion##note:tn[Heb. revolt] against the LORD.

=== @30

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Restoration of Israel and Judah

[.parallel]
(<<Ezekiel 28:25–26>>)

@ The word that came to Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,
@ ##Thus speaketh the LORD God of Israel, saying, Write thee all the words that I have spoken unto thee in a book.##note:x[<<Isa 30:8; Jer 36:32>>]
@ For, lo, the days come, saith the LORD, ##that I##note:x[<<Jer 29:14; 30:18>>] will bring again the captivity of my people Israel and Judah, saith the LORD: ##and I##note:x[<<Jer 16:15>>] will cause them to return to the land that I gave to their fathers, and they shall possess it.

@ ¶ And these __are__ the words that the LORD spake concerning Israel and concerning Judah.
@ For thus saith the LORD; We have heard a voice of trembling, of fear, and not of peace.note:tn[__of fear…__: or, there is fear, and not peace]
@ Ask ye now, and see whether a man doth travail with child? wherefore do I see ##every##note:x[<<Jer 4:31>>] man with his hands on his loins, as a woman in travail, and all faces are turned into ##paleness##note:x[<<Joel 2:6; Nah 2:10>>]?note:tn[__a man__: Heb. a male]
@ Alas! ##for##note:x[<<Zech 14:1–2; Mal 4:1>>] that day __is__ great, ##so##note:x[<<Dan 12:1>>] that none __is__ like it: it __is__ even the time of Jacob’s trouble; but he shall be saved out of it.
@ For it shall come to pass in that day, saith the LORD of hosts, __that__ ##I##note:x[<<Isa 9:4; Ezek 34:27; Nah 1:13>>] will break his yoke from off thy neck, and will burst thy bonds, and strangers shall no more serve themselves of him:
@ ##But they shall serve the LORD their God, and David their king, whom I will raise up unto them.##note:x[<<Isa 55:3–5; Hos 3:5; Luke 1:69>>]

@ ¶ Therefore ##fear##note:x[<<Isa 43:5; Jer 46:27–28>>] thou not, O my servant Jacob, saith the LORD; neither be dismayed, O Israel: for, lo, ##I##note:x[<<Jer 46:27>>] will save thee from afar, and thy seed from the land of their captivity; and Jacob shall return, and shall be in rest, and be quiet, and none shall make __him__ afraid.
@ For ##I __am__##note:x[<<Jer 1:8, 19>>] with thee, saith the LORD, to save thee: though I make a full end of all nations whither I have scattered thee, yet will I not make a full end of thee: ##but I##note:x[<<Jer 10:24>>] will correct thee in measure, and will not leave thee altogether unpunished.
@ ##For thus saith the LORD, Thy bruise __is__ incurable, __and__ thy wound __is__ grievous.##note:x[<<Jer 15:18; 30:15>>]
@ __There is__ none to plead thy cause, that thou mayest be bound up: thou ##hast##note:x[<<Jer 8:22>>] no healing medicines.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. for binding up, or, pressing]
@ All thy ##lovers##note:x[<<Lam 1:2>>] have forgotten thee; they seek thee not; for ##I##note:x[<<Job 30:21>>] have wounded thee with the wound of an enemy, with the chastisement of a cruel one, for the multitude of thine iniquity; ##__because__##note:x[<<Jer 5:6>>] thy sins were increased.
@ Why criest thou for thine affliction? thy sorrow __is__ incurable ##for the##note:x[<<Jer 30:14>>] multitude of thine iniquity: __because__ thy sins were increased, I have done these things unto thee.
@ ##Therefore all they that devour thee shall be devoured; and all thine adversaries, every one of them, shall go into captivity; and they that spoil thee shall be a spoil, and all that prey upon thee will I give for a prey.##note:x[<<Exod 23:22; Isa 33:1; Jer 10:25>>]
@ ##For I##note:x[<<Ps 107:20; Jer 33:6; 1 Pet 2:24>>] will restore health unto thee, and I will heal thee of thy wounds, saith the LORD; because they called thee an Outcast, __saying__, This __is__ Zion, whom no man seeketh after.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD; ##Behold##note:x[<<Ps 102:13; Jer 30:3>>], I will bring again the captivity of Jacob’s tents, and have mercy on his dwellingplaces; and the city shall be builded upon her own ##heap##note:tn[or, little hill], and ##the palace##note:x[<<Hag 2:7–9>>] shall remain after the manner thereof.
@ And ##out##note:x[<<Isa 51:11>>] of them shall proceed thanksgiving and the voice of them that make merry: ##and I##note:x[<<Isa 60:22; Jer 33:22>>] will multiply them, and they shall not be few; I will also glorify them, and they shall not be small.
@ Their ##children##note:x[<<Jer 32:39>>] also shall be as aforetime, and their congregation shall be established before me, ##and I##note:x[<<Jer 2:3; 50:33–34>>] will punish all that oppress them.
@ And their nobles shall be of themselves, and their ##governor##note:x[<<Ezek 34:23–24; 37:24>>] shall proceed from the midst of them; ##and I##note:x[<<Num 16:5>>] will cause him to draw near, and he shall approach unto me: for who __is__ this that engaged his heart to approach unto me? saith the LORD.
@ ##And ye shall be my people, and I will be your God.##note:x[<<Jer 24:7; Ezek 36:28; Rev 21:3>>]
@ Behold, ##the whirlwind##note:x[<<Jer 23:19–20; 25:32; Zech 9:14>>] of the LORD goeth forth with fury, a ##continuing##note:tn[Heb. cutting] whirlwind: it shall fall with pain upon the head of the wicked.note:tn[__fall…__: or, remain]
@ The fierce anger of the LORD shall not return, until he have done __it__, and until he have performed the intents of his heart: ##in##note:x[<<Jer 23:20; Hos 3:5; Mic 4:1>>] the latter days ye shall consider it.

=== @31

[discrete.pericope]
==== Mourning Turned to Joy

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 2:16–18>>)

@ At the ##same##note:x[<<Jer 30:24>>] time, saith the LORD, ##will##note:x[<<Isa 41:10; Jer 30:22>>] I be the God of all the families of Israel, and they shall be my people.
@ Thus saith the LORD, The people __which were__ left of the sword ##found##note:x[<<Deut 1:33>>] grace in the wilderness; __even__ Israel, ##when##note:x[<<Num 10:33; Ps 95:11>>] I went to cause him to rest.
@ note:x[<<Hos 11:4>>]The LORD hath appeared of old unto me, __saying__, Yea, I have loved thee with ##an##note:x[<<Ps 103:17; Isa 54:8–9>>] everlasting love: therefore with lovingkindness have I drawn thee.note:tn[__of…__: Heb. from afar]note:tn[__with lovingkindness…__: have I extended lovingkindness unto thee]
@ ##Again##note:x[<<Jer 31:13>>] I will ##build##note:x[<<Jer 33:7>>] thee, and thou shalt be built, ##O##note:x[<<Amos 5:2>>] virgin of Israel: thou shalt again be adorned with thy ##tabrets##note:tn[or, timbrels], and shalt go forth in the dances of them that make merry.
@ Thou shalt ##yet##note:x[<<Amos 9:14>>] plant vines upon the mountains of Samaria: the planters shall plant, and shall eat __them__ as common things.note:tn[__eat…__: Heb. profane them]
@ For there shall be ##a day##note:x[<<Isa 52:7–8>>], __that__ the watchmen ##upon##note:x[<<Jer 50:19>>] the mount Ephraim shall cry, ##Arise##note:x[<<Jer 50:4–5>>] ye, and let us go up to Zion unto the LORD our God.
@ For thus saith the LORD; Sing with gladness for Jacob, and shout among the chief of the nations: publish ye, praise ye, and say, ##O##note:x[<<Ps 14:7; 28:9>>] LORD, save thy people, the ##remnant##note:x[<<Isa 37:31>>] of Israel.
@ Behold, ##I##note:x[<<Jer 23:8>>] will bring them from the north country, and gather them from the coasts of the earth, __and__ with ##them the##note:x[<<Isa 42:16; Ezek 34:16>>] blind and the lame, the woman with child and her that travaileth with child together: a great company shall return thither.
@ They shall ##come##note:x[<<Jer 3:4>>] with weeping, and with ##supplications##note:tn[or, favours] will I lead them: I will cause them to walk by the rivers of waters in a straight way, wherein they shall not stumble: ##for I##note:x[<<Exod 4:22; Jer 3:4>>] am a father to Israel, and Ephraim __is__ my firstborn.

@ ¶ Hear the word of the LORD, O ye nations, and declare __it__ in the isles afar off, and say, ##He##note:x[<<Isa 40:11>>] that scattered Israel will gather him, ##and keep##note:x[<<Isa 40:11; Ezek 34:12>>] him, as a shepherd __doth__ his flock.
@ For the LORD hath ##redeemed##note:x[<<Isa 44:23; 48:20; Titus 2:14>>] Jacob, and ransomed him from the hand of __him that was__ stronger than he.
@ ##Therefore##note:x[<<Isa 35:10>>] they shall come and sing in the height of Zion, and shall flow together to the goodness of the LORD, for ##wheat##note:x[<<Joel 3:18>>], and for wine, and for oil, and for the young of the flock and of the herd: and their soul shall be as a watered garden; ##and they##note:x[<<Isa 35:10>>] shall not sorrow any more at all.
@ Then ##shall##note:x[<<Ps 30:11>>] the virgin rejoice in the dance, both young men and old together: ##for##note:x[<<Isa 51:11; 61:3>>] I will turn their mourning into joy, and will comfort them, and make them rejoice from their sorrow.
@ And I will ##satiate##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:9>>] the soul of the priests with fatness, and ##my people##note:x[<<Jer 31:25; 33:9>>] shall be satisfied with my goodness, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD; A voice was heard in ##Ramah##note:x[<<Jer 40:1>>], lamentation, __and__ bitter weeping; Rahel weeping for her children ##refused##note:x[<<Gen 37:35; Ps 77:2>>] to be comforted for her children, because they __were__ not.
@ Thus saith the LORD; Refrain thy voice from weeping, and thine eyes from tears: ##for##note:x[<<Ruth 2:12; Heb 6:10>>] thy work shall be rewarded, saith the LORD; and ##they##note:x[<<Jer 30:3>>] shall come again from the land of the enemy.
@ ##And there is hope in thine end, saith the LORD, that thy children shall come again to their own border.##note:x[<<Lam 3:21, 26; Hos 3:5>>]

@ ¶ I have surely heard ##Ephraim##note:x[<<Jer 3:21–22; 31:9>>] bemoaning himself __thus;__ Thou hast chastised me, and I was chastised, as a bullock unaccustomed __to the yoke:__ ##turn##note:x[<<Ps 80:3>>] thou me, and I shall be turned; for thou __art__ the LORD my God.
@ ##Surely after##note:x[<<Ezek 36:31>>] that I was turned, I repented; and after that I was instructed, I smote upon __my__ thigh: ##I was ashamed##note:x[<<Jer 3:25; Ezek 36:31>>], yea, even confounded, because I did bear the reproach of my youth.
@ ##__Is__ Ephraim##note:x[<<Ps 103:13>>] my dear son? __is he__ a pleasant child? for since I spake against him, I do earnestly remember him still: therefore my bowels are troubled for him; ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 55:7; Mic 7:18–19>>] surely have mercy upon him, saith the LORD.note:tn[__are…__: Heb. sound]
@ Set thee up waymarks, make thee high heaps: ##set thine##note:x[<<Jer 50:5>>] heart toward the highway, __even__ the way __which__ thou wentest: turn again, O virgin of Israel, turn again to these thy cities.

@ ¶ How long wilt thou go about, O thou ##backsliding##note:x[<<Jer 7:24; 49:4>>] daughter? for the LORD hath created a new thing in the earth, ##A woman##note:x[<<Matt 1:21>>] shall compass a man.
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##As##note:x[<<Isa 1:26; Zech 8:3>>] yet they shall use this speech in the land of Judah and in the cities thereof, when I shall bring again their captivity; The LORD bless thee, O habitation of justice, ##__and__ mountain##note:x[<<Zech 8:3>>] of holiness.
@ ##And there shall dwell in Judah itself, and in all the cities thereof together, husbandmen, and they __that__ go forth with flocks.##note:x[<<Zech 8:4–8>>]
@ ##For I have satiated the weary soul, and I have replenished every sorrowful soul.##note:x[<<Ps 107:9; Matt 5:6; 11:28>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The New Covenant

[.parallel]
(<<Hebrews 8:6–13>>)

@ Upon this I awaked, and beheld; and my sleep was sweet unto me.

@ ¶ Behold, the days come, saith the LORD, ##that I##note:x[<<Ezek 36:9; Hos 2:23; Zech 10:9>>] will sow the house of Israel and the house of Judah with the seed of man, and with the seed of beast.
@ And it shall come to pass, ##__that__ like##note:x[<<Jer 44:27>>] as I have watched over them, ##to pluck##note:x[<<Jer 1:10>>] up, and to break down, and to throw down, and to destroy, and to afflict; ##so##note:x[<<Jer 24:6>>] will I watch over them, to build, and to plant, saith the LORD.
@ note:x[<<Jer 31:30; Lam 5:7; Ezek 18:2–3>>]In those days they shall say no more, The fathers have eaten a sour grape, and the children’s teeth are set on edge.
@ ##But every one shall die for his own iniquity: every man that eateth the sour grape, his teeth shall be set on edge.##note:x[<<Deut 24:16; Isa 3:11; Ezek 18:20>>]

@ ¶ Behold, the days come, saith the LORD, that ##I##note:x[<<Jer 32:40; Ezek 37:26; Heb 10:16–17>>] will make a new covenant with the house of Israel, and with the house of Judah:
@ ##Not##note:x[<<Exod 19:5; 24:6–8; Deut 5:3>>] according to the covenant that I made with their fathers in the day __that__ I took them by the hand to bring them out of the land of Egypt; which my covenant they brake, although I was an husband unto them, saith the LORD&#58;note:tn[__although…__: or, should I have continued an husband unto them?]
@ But this __shall be__ the covenant that ##I will##note:x[<<2 Cor 3:3; Heb 8:10; 10:16>>] make with the house of Israel; After those days, saith the LORD, I will put my law in their inward parts, and write it in their hearts; and will be their God, and they shall be my people.
@ And they shall teach no more every man his neighbour, and every man his brother, saying, Know the LORD: for they shall all know me, from the least of them unto the greatest of them, saith the LORD: ##for I##note:x[<<Isa 43:25; Jer 33:8; Heb 8:12>>] will forgive their iniquity, and I will remember their sin no more.

@ ¶ Thus saith ##the LORD##note:x[<<Jer 10:16>>], which giveth the sun for a light by day, __and__ the ordinances of the moon and of the stars for a light by night, ##which divideth##note:x[<<Isa 51:15>>] the sea ##when##note:x[<<Isa 51:15>>] the waves thereof roar; The LORD of hosts __is__ his name:
@ If ##those##note:x[<<Ps 89:36–37; Isa 54:9–10; Jer 33:20–26>>] ordinances depart from before me, saith the LORD, __then__ the seed of Israel also shall cease from being a nation before me for ever.
@ Thus saith the LORD; ##If##note:x[<<Isa 40:12; Jer 33:22>>] heaven above can be measured, and the foundations of the earth searched out beneath, ##I##note:x[<<Jer 33:24–26>>] will also cast off all the seed of Israel for all that they have done, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ Behold, the days come, saith the LORD, ##that##note:x[<<Jer 30:18>>] the city shall be built to the LORD from ##the tower##note:x[<<Zech 14:10>>] of Hananeel unto ##the gate##note:x[<<2 Kgs 14:13>>] of the corner.
@ ##And the measuring line shall yet go forth over against it upon the hill Gareb, and shall compass about to Goath.##note:x[<<Zech 2:1–2>>]
@ And the whole valley of the dead bodies, and of the ashes, and all the fields unto ##the brook##note:x[<<2 Sam 15:23; John 18:1>>] of Kidron, unto the corner of the horse gate toward the east, ##__shall be__##note:x[<<Joel 3:17>>] holy unto the LORD; it shall not be plucked up, nor thrown down any more for ever.

=== @32

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Buys Hanamel’s Field

@ The word that came to Jeremiah from the LORD ##in the##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:1–2; Jer 39:1–2>>] tenth year of Zedekiah king of Judah, which __was__ the eighteenth year of Nebuchadrezzar.
@ For then the king of Babylon’s army besieged Jerusalem: and ##Jeremiah##note:x[<<Jer 33:1; 37:21>>] the prophet was shut up ##in the##note:x[<<Neh 3:25>>] court of the prison, which __was__ in the king of Judah’s house.
@ For Zedekiah king of Judah had shut him up, saying, ##Wherefore##note:x[<<Jer 26:8–9>>] dost thou prophesy, and say, Thus saith the LORD, ##Behold##note:x[<<Jer 21:4–7; 34:2–3>>], I will give this city into the hand of the king of Babylon, and he shall take it;
@ ##And Zedekiah king of Judah shall not escape out of the hand of the Chaldeans, but shall surely be delivered into the hand of the king of Babylon, and shall speak with him mouth to mouth, and his eyes shall behold his eyes;##note:x[<<Jer 37:17; 38:18, 23>>]
@ And he shall lead Zedekiah to Babylon, and there shall he be until I visit him, saith the LORD: though ye fight with the Chaldeans, ye shall not prosper.

@ ¶ And Jeremiah said, The word of the LORD came unto me, saying,
@ Behold, Hanameel the son of Shallum thine uncle shall come unto thee, saying, Buy thee my field that __is__ in ##Anathoth##note:x[<<Jer 1:1>>]: for the right of redemption __is__ thine to buy __it__.
@ So Hanameel mine uncle’s son came to me in the ##court##note:x[<<Jer 32:2>>] of the prison according to the word of the LORD, and said unto me, Buy my field, I pray thee, that __is__ in ##Anathoth##note:x[<<Jer 32:7>>], which __is__ in the country of Benjamin: for the right of inheritance __is__ thine, and the redemption __is__ thine; buy __it__ for thyself. Then I knew that this __was__ the word of the LORD.
@ And I bought the field of Hanameel my uncle’s son, that __was__ in Anathoth, and weighed him the money, __even__ seventeen shekels of silver.note:tn[__seventeen…__: or, seven shekels and ten pieces of silver]
@ And ##I##note:x[<<Jer 32:12, 44>>] subscribed the evidence, and sealed __it__, ##and took##note:x[<<Jer 32:44>>] witnesses, and weighed __him__ the money in the balances.note:tn[__subscribed…__: Heb. wrote in the book]
@ So I took the evidence of the purchase, __both__ that which was sealed __according__ to the law and custom, and that which was open:
@ And I gave the evidence of the purchase unto ##Baruch##note:x[<<Jer 32:16>>] the son of ##Neriah##note:x[<<Jer 51:59>>], the son of Maaseiah, in the sight of Hanameel mine uncle’s __son__, and in the presence of the witnesses that subscribed the book of the purchase, before all the Jews that sat in the court of the prison.

@ ¶ And I charged Baruch before them, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Take these evidences, this evidence of the purchase, both which is sealed, and this evidence which is open; and put them in an earthen vessel, that they may continue many days.
@ For thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Houses##note:x[<<Jer 30:18; Amos 9:14–15; Zech 3:10>>] and fields and vineyards shall be possessed again in this land.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Prays for Understanding

@ ¶ Now when I had delivered the evidence of the purchase unto Baruch the son of Neriah, I prayed unto the LORD, saying,
@ Ah Lord GOD! behold, thou hast made the heaven and the earth by thy great power and stretched out arm, __and__ ##there##note:x[<<Gen 18:14; Jer 32:27; Luke 1:37>>] is nothing too hard for thee&#58;note:tn[__too…__: or, hid from thee]
@ Thou ##shewest##note:x[<<Exod 34:7; Deut 5:9–10>>] lovingkindness unto thousands, and recompensest the iniquity of the fathers into the bosom of their children after them: the Great, the Mighty God, ##the LORD##note:x[<<Jer 10:16>>] of hosts, __is__ his name,
@ ##Great##note:x[<<Isa 28:29>>] in counsel, and mighty in ##work##note:tn[Heb. doing]: for thine eyes __are__ open upon all the ways of the sons of men: ##to give##note:x[<<Jer 17:10; Matt 16:27>>] every one according to his ways, and according to the fruit of his doings:
@ Which ##hast set##note:x[<<Neh 9:10>>] signs and wonders in the land of Egypt, __even__ unto this day, and in Israel, and among __other__ men; ##and hast##note:x[<<Neh 9:10>>] made thee a name, as at this day;
@ And hast ##brought##note:x[<<Exod 6:6>>] forth thy people Israel out of the land of Egypt with signs, and with wonders, and ##with a strong##note:x[<<Deut 26:8>>] hand, and with a stretched out arm, and with great terror;
@ And hast given them this land, ##which##note:x[<<Exod 13:5>>] thou didst swear to their fathers to give them, ##a land##note:x[<<Exod 3:8; 13:5>>] flowing with milk and honey;
@ And they came in, and ##possessed##note:x[<<Ps 78:54–55>>] it; ##but##note:x[<<Ezra 9:7>>] they obeyed not thy voice, neither walked in thy law; they have done nothing of all that thou commandedst them to do: ##therefore##note:x[<<Ezra 9:7>>] thou hast caused all this evil to come upon them:
@ Behold the note:x[<<Jer 33:4>>]##mounts##note:tn[or, engines of shot], they are come unto the city to take it; and ##the city is##note:x[<<Jer 32:36>>] given into the hand of the Chaldeans, that fight against it, because of the sword, and of the famine, and of the pestilence: and ##what##note:x[<<Deut 4:26>>] thou hast spoken is come to pass; and, behold, thou seest __it__.
@ And thou hast said unto me, O Lord GOD, Buy thee the field for money, and take witnesses; ##for the##note:tn[or, though the] city is given into the hand of the Chaldeans.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Answers Jeremiah

@ ¶ Then came the word of the LORD unto Jeremiah, saying,
@ Behold, I __am__ the LORD, the ##God##note:x[<<Isa 64:8>>] of all flesh: ##is##note:x[<<Jer 32:17; Matt 19:26>>] there any thing too hard for me?
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD; ##Behold##note:x[<<Jer 32:3>>], I will give this city into the hand of the Chaldeans, and into the hand of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon, and he shall take it:
@ And the Chaldeans, that fight against this city, shall come ##and set##note:x[<<Jer 21:10; 52:13>>] fire on this city, and burn it with the houses, ##upon##note:x[<<Jer 19:13>>] whose roofs they have offered incense unto Baal, and poured out drink offerings unto other gods, to provoke me to anger.
@ For the ##children##note:x[<<Jer 7:22–26>>] of Israel and the children of Judah have only done evil before me from their youth: for the children of Israel have only provoked me to anger with the work of their hands, saith the LORD.
@ For this city hath been to me __as__ a provocation of mine anger and of my fury from the day that they built it even unto this day; ##that I##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:27>>] should remove it from before my face,note:tn[__a provocation…__: Heb. for my anger]
@ Because of all the evil of the children of Israel and of the children of Judah, which ##they##note:x[<<Isa 1:4–6; Jer 2:26; Dan 9:8>>] have done to provoke me to anger, they, their kings, their princes, their priests, and their prophets, and the men of Judah, and the inhabitants of Jerusalem.
@ And they have ##turned##note:x[<<Jer 2:27; Ezek 8:16>>] unto me the ##back##note:tn[Heb. neck], and not the face: though I taught them, ##rising##note:x[<<Jer 7:13>>] up early and teaching __them__, yet they have not hearkened to receive instruction.
@ ##But they set their abominations in the house, which is called by my name, to defile it.##note:x[<<Jer 7:30; Ezek 8:5–16>>]
@ And ##they built##note:x[<<2 Chr 33:6; Jer 7:31>>] the high places of Baal, ##which##note:x[<<Jer 7:31>>] __are__ in the valley of the son of Hinnom, to cause their sons and their daughters to pass through __the fire__ unto Molech; which I commanded them not, neither came it into my mind, that they should do this abomination, to cause Judah to sin.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Promise of Restoration

[.parallel]
(<<Ezekiel 11:13–21>>)

@ ¶ And now therefore thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel, concerning this city, whereof ye say, ##It##note:x[<<Jer 32:3, 24>>] shall be delivered into the hand of the king of Babylon by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence;
@ Behold, ##I will gather##note:x[<<Jer 23:3; Ezek 34:12–14>>] them out of all countries, whither I have driven them in mine anger, and in my fury, and in great wrath; and I will bring them again unto this place, and ##I will cause##note:x[<<Jer 23:6>>] them to dwell safely:
@ ##And they shall be my people, and I will be their God:##note:x[<<Ps 144:15; Jer 24:7; Ezek 11:19–20>>]
@ And ##I##note:x[<<Ezek 11:19–20; John 17:21; Acts 4:32>>] will give them one heart, and one way, that they may fear me for ever, for the good of them, and of their children after them&#58;note:tn[__for ever__: Heb. all days]
@ And ##I will make##note:x[<<Jer 31:31–33; Heb 6:13–18>>] an everlasting covenant with them, that I will not turn away from them, to do them good; ##but I##note:x[<<Ezek 36:26>>] will put my fear in their hearts, that they shall not depart from me.note:tn[__from them__: Heb. from after them]
@ ##Yea, I##note:x[<<Isa 62:5; Zeph 3:17>>] will rejoice over them to do them good, ##and I##note:x[<<Jer 24:6>>] will plant them in this land ##assuredly##note:tn[Heb. in truth, or, stability] with my whole heart and with my whole soul.
@ For thus saith the LORD; ##Like##note:x[<<Josh 23:14–15; Jer 31:28>>] as I have brought all this great evil upon this people, so will I bring upon them all the good that I have promised them.
@ ##And fields shall be bought in this land, whereof ye say, __It is__ desolate without man or beast; it is given into the hand of the Chaldeans.##note:x[<<Ezek 37:11–14>>]
@ Men shall buy fields for money, and subscribe evidences, and seal __them__, and take witnesses ##in the land##note:x[<<Jer 17:26>>] of Benjamin, and in the places about Jerusalem, and in the cities of Judah, and in the cities of the mountains, and in the cities of the valley, and in the cities of the south: ##for I##note:x[<<Jer 33:7, 11>>] will cause their captivity to return, saith the LORD.

=== @33

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Excellence of the Restored Nation

@ Moreover the word of the LORD came unto Jeremiah the second time, while ##he##note:x[<<Jer 32:2–3; 37:21; 38:28>>] was yet shut up in the court of the prison, saying,
@ Thus saith ##the LORD##note:x[<<Exod 6:3; 15:3>>] ##the maker##note:x[<<Isa 37:26>>] thereof, the LORD that formed it, to establish it; the LORD __is__ his name;note:tn[__the LORD is__: or, JEHOVAH, etc]
@ ##Call##note:x[<<Isa 65:24; Jer 29:12>>] unto me, and I will answer thee, and ##shew##note:x[<<Eph 3:20>>] thee great and ##mighty##note:tn[or, hidden] things, which thou knowest not.
@ For thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel, concerning the houses of this city, and concerning the houses of the kings of Judah, which are ##thrown##note:x[<<Jer 32:24; Ezek 4:2; Hab 1:10>>] down by the mounts, and by the sword;
@ They ##come##note:x[<<Jer 21:4–7; 32:5>>] to fight with the Chaldeans, but __it is__ to fill them with the dead bodies of men, whom I have slain in mine anger and in my fury, and for all whose wickedness ##I have hid##note:x[<<Isa 8:17>>] my face from this city.
@ Behold, ##I will bring##note:x[<<Isa 30:26>>] it health and cure, and I will cure them, ##and will##note:x[<<Isa 30:26; John 10:10>>] reveal unto them the abundance of peace and truth.
@ And I ##will cause##note:x[<<Jer 30:3>>] the captivity of Judah and the captivity of Israel to return, ##and will##note:x[<<Isa 1:26; Amos 9:14–15>>] build them, as at the first.
@ ##And I will cleanse them from all their iniquity, whereby they have sinned against me; and I will pardon all their iniquities, whereby they have sinned, and whereby they have transgressed against me.##note:x[<<Ezek 36:25; Mic 7:18–19; Zech 13:1>>]

@ ¶ And it shall be to me ##a name##note:x[<<Ps 126:2–3; Zeph 3:17–20>>] of joy, a praise and an honour before all the nations of the earth, which shall hear all the good that I do unto them: and they shall ##fear##note:x[<<Isa 60:5>>] and tremble for all the goodness and for all the prosperity that I procure unto it.

@ Thus saith the LORD; Again there shall be heard in this place, ##which ye##note:x[<<Jer 32:36; Ezek 37:11>>] say __shall be__ desolate without man and without beast, __even__ in the cities of Judah, and in the streets of Jerusalem, that are desolate, without man, and without inhabitant, and without beast,
@ The voice of joy, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride, the voice of them that shall say, ##Praise the##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:34; Ps 106:1; 107:1>>] LORD of hosts: for the LORD __is__ good; for his mercy __endureth__ for ever: __and__ of them that shall bring the sacrifice of praise into the house of the LORD. For I will cause to return the captivity of the land, as at the first, saith the LORD.
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts; Again in this place, which is desolate without man and without beast, and ##in all##note:x[<<Isa 65:10>>] the cities thereof, shall be an habitation of shepherds causing __their__ flocks to lie down.
@ In the cities of the mountains, in the cities of the vale, and in the cities of the south, and in the land of Benjamin, and in the places about Jerusalem, and in the cities of Judah, ##shall##note:x[<<Lev 27:32>>] the flocks pass again under the hands of him that telleth __them__, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Covenant with David

@ ##Behold, the days come, saith the LORD, that I will perform that good thing which I have promised unto the house of Israel and to the house of Judah.##note:x[<<1 Chr 17:13–14; Jer 23:5; Hag 2:6–9>>]

@ ¶ In those days, and at that time, will I cause ##the Branch##note:x[<<Isa 4:2; 11:1–5; Jer 23:5–6>>] of righteousness to grow up unto David; and he shall execute judgment and righteousness in the land.
@ In those days ##shall Judah##note:x[<<Jer 23:6>>] be saved, and Jerusalem shall dwell safely: and this __is the name__ wherewith she shall be called, ##The LORD our righteousness##note:x[<<Jer 23:6; 1 Cor 1:30>>].note:tn[__The LORD…__: Heb. Jehovah-tsidkenu]

@ ¶ For thus saith the LORD; ##David shall never want##note:x[<<1 Kgs 2:4; Ps 89:29–37; Luke 1:32–33>>] a man to sit upon the throne of the house of Israel;note:tn[__David…__: Heb. There shall not be cut off from David]
@ Neither shall the priests the Levites want a man before me to offer burnt offerings, and to kindle meat offerings, and to do sacrifice continually.

@ ¶ And the word of the LORD came unto Jeremiah, saying,
@ ##Thus saith the LORD; If ye can break my covenant of the day, and my covenant of the night, and that there should not be day and night in their season;##note:x[<<Gen 8:22; Ps 89:37; Isa 54:9–10>>]
@ __Then__ ##may##note:x[<<2 Sam 23:5; 2 Chr 7:18; Ps 89:34>>] also my covenant be broken with David my servant, that he should not have a son to reign upon his throne; and with the Levites the priests, my ministers.
@ As ##the host##note:x[<<Gen 15:5; 22:17; Heb 11:12>>] of heaven cannot be numbered, neither the sand of the sea measured: so will I multiply the seed of David my servant, and the Levites that minister unto me.
@ Moreover the word of the LORD came to Jeremiah, saying,
@ Considerest thou not what this people have spoken, saying, The two families which the LORD hath chosen, he hath even cast them off? thus they have despised my people, that they should be no more a nation before them.
@ Thus saith the LORD; ##If my##note:x[<<Jer 33:20>>] covenant __be__ not with day and night, ##__and if__##note:x[<<Ps 74:16–17; Jer 31:35–36>>] I have not appointed the ordinances of heaven and earth;
@ Then ##will I##note:x[<<Jer 31:37>>] cast away the seed of Jacob, and David my servant, __so__ that I will not take __any__ of his seed __to be__ rulers over the seed of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob: for I will cause their captivity to return, ##and have##note:x[<<Isa 14:1; Hos 2:23>>] mercy on them.

=== @34

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Prophecy against Zedekiah

@ The word which came unto Jeremiah from the LORD, when Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, and all his army, and ##all the kingdoms##note:x[<<Jer 1:15; Dan 2:37–38>>] of the earth of his dominion, and all the people, fought against Jerusalem, and against all the cities thereof, saying,note:tn[__of his…__: Heb. the dominion of his hand]
@ Thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel; Go and speak to Zedekiah king of Judah, and tell him, Thus saith the LORD; ##Behold##note:x[<<Jer 21:10; 34:22>>], I will give this city into the hand of the king of Babylon, and he shall burn it with fire:
@ ##And thou##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:4–5; Jer 21:7; 32:4>>] shalt not escape out of his hand, but shalt surely be taken, and delivered into his hand; and thine eyes shall behold the eyes of the king of Babylon, and he shall speak with thee mouth to mouth, and thou shalt go to Babylon.note:tn[__he shall…__: Heb. his mouth shall speak to thy mouth]
@ Yet hear the word of the LORD, O Zedekiah king of Judah; Thus saith the LORD of thee, Thou shalt not die by the sword:
@ __But__ thou shalt die in peace: ##and with##note:x[<<2 Chr 16:14>>] the burnings of thy fathers, the former kings which were before thee, so shall they burn __odours__ for thee; ##and they##note:x[<<Jer 22:18>>] will lament thee, __saying__, Ah lord! for I have pronounced the word, saith the LORD.
@ Then Jeremiah the prophet spake all these words unto Zedekiah king of Judah in Jerusalem,
@ When the king of Babylon’s army fought against Jerusalem, and against all the cities of Judah that were left, against ##Lachish##note:x[<<Josh 10:3>>], and against Azekah: for these defenced cities remained of the cities of Judah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Freedom for Hebrew Slaves

@ ¶ __This is__ the word that came unto Jeremiah from the LORD, after that the king Zedekiah ##had##note:x[<<2 Kgs 11:17; 23:2–3>>] made a covenant with all the people which __were__ at Jerusalem, ##to proclaim##note:x[<<Lev 25:10>>] liberty unto them;
@ That every man should let his manservant, and every man his maidservant, __being__ an Hebrew or an Hebrewess, go free; that none should serve himself of them, __to wit__, of a Jew his brother.
@ Now when all the princes, and all the people, which had entered into the covenant, heard that every one should let his manservant, and every one his maidservant, go free, that none should serve themselves of them any more, then they obeyed, and let __them__ go.
@ ##But afterward they turned, and caused the servants and the handmaids, whom they had let go free, to return, and brought them into subjection for servants and for handmaids.##note:x[<<2 Pet 2:20–22>>]

@ ¶ Therefore the word of the LORD came to Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel; I made a covenant with your fathers ##in the##note:x[<<Josh 24:17; Judg 6:8>>] day that I brought them forth ##out of##note:x[<<Josh 24:17>>] the land of Egypt, out of the house of bondmen, saying,
@ ##At the##note:x[<<Deut 15:12>>] end of seven years let ye go every man his brother an Hebrew, which hath been sold unto thee; and when he hath served thee six years, thou shalt let him go free from thee: but your fathers hearkened not unto me, neither inclined their ear.note:tn[__hath been…__: or, hath sold himself]
@ And ye were ##now##note:tn[Heb. to day] turned, and had done right in my sight, in proclaiming liberty every man to his neighbour; and ##ye had##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:3; Jer 34:8>>] made a covenant before me in the house which is called by my name&#58;note:tn[__which…__: Heb. whereupon my name is called]
@ But ##ye turned##note:x[<<Jer 34:11; Ezek 3:20>>] and ##polluted##note:x[<<Lev 19:12>>] my name, and caused every man his servant, and every man his handmaid, whom ye had set at liberty at their pleasure, to return, and brought them into subjection, to be unto you for servants and for handmaids.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD; Ye have not hearkened unto me, in proclaiming liberty, every one to his brother, and every man to his neighbour: ##behold##note:x[<<Matt 7:2; Gal 6:7>>], I proclaim a liberty for you, saith the LORD, to the sword, to the pestilence, and to the famine; and ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 29:18>>] make you to be removed into all the kingdoms of the earth.note:tn[__to be…__: Heb. for a removing]
@ And I will give the men that ##have transgressed##note:x[<<Hos 6:7>>] my covenant, which have not performed the words of the covenant which they had made before me, ##when##note:x[<<Gen 15:10>>] they cut the calf in twain, and passed between the parts thereof,
@ The princes of Judah, and the princes of Jerusalem, the eunuchs, and the priests, and all the people of the land, which passed between the parts of the calf;
@ I will even give them into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of them that seek their life: ##and their##note:x[<<Jer 7:33; 16:4; 19:7>>] dead bodies shall be for meat unto the fowls of the heaven, and to the beasts of the earth.
@ And ##Zedekiah##note:x[<<Jer 39:6; 52:24–27>>] king of Judah and his princes will I give into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of them that seek their life, and into the hand of the king of Babylon’s army, which are gone up from you.
@ Behold, I will command, saith the LORD, and cause them to return to this city; and they ##shall fight##note:x[<<Jer 39:1–2, 8>>] against it, and take it, and burn it with fire: ##and I will##note:x[<<Jer 9:11>>] make the cities of Judah a desolation without an inhabitant.

=== @35

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Obedience of the Rechabites

@ The word which came unto Jeremiah from the LORD in the days of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah, saying,
@ Go unto ##the house##note:x[<<1 Chr 2:55>>] of the Rechabites, and speak unto them, and bring them into the house of the LORD, ##into one##note:x[<<1 Chr 9:26>>] of the chambers, and give them wine to drink.
@ Then I took Jaazaniah the son of Jeremiah, the son of Habaziniah, and his brethren, and all his sons, and the whole house of the Rechabites;
@ And I brought them into the house of the LORD, into the chamber of the sons of Hanan, the son of Igdaliah, ##a man##note:x[<<Deut 33:1; 2 Chr 8:14>>] of God, which __was__ by the chamber of the princes, which __was__ above the chamber of Maaseiah the son of Shallum, ##the keeper##note:x[<<2 Kgs 12:9>>] of the ##door##note:tn[Heb. threshold, or, vessel]:
@ And I set before the sons of the house of the Rechabites pots full of wine, and cups, and I said unto them, Drink ye wine.
@ But they said, We will drink no wine: for ##Jonadab##note:x[<<2 Kgs 10:15; 1 Chr 2:55>>] the son of Rechab our father commanded us, saying, ##Ye shall##note:x[<<Luke 1:15>>] drink no wine, __neither ye__, nor your sons for ever:
@ Neither shall ye build house, nor sow seed, nor plant vineyard, nor have __any:__ but all your days ye shall dwell in tents; that ye may live many days in the land where ye __be__ strangers.
@ ##Thus have we obeyed the voice of Jonadab the son of Rechab our father in all that he hath charged us, to drink no wine all our days, we, our wives, our sons, nor our daughters;##note:x[<<Col 3:20>>]
@ ##Nor to build houses for us to dwell in: neither have we vineyard, nor field, nor seed:##note:x[<<1 Tim 6:6>>]
@ But we have dwelt in tents, and have obeyed, and done according to all that Jonadab our father commanded us.
@ But it came to pass, when Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon came up into the land, that we said, ##Come##note:x[<<Jer 8:14>>], and let us go to Jerusalem for fear of the army of the Chaldeans, and for fear of the army of the Syrians: so we dwell at Jerusalem.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judah Rebuked

@ ¶ Then came the word of the LORD unto Jeremiah, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Go and tell the men of Judah and the inhabitants of Jerusalem, ##Will##note:x[<<Jer 5:3; 32:33>>] ye not receive instruction to hearken to my words? saith the LORD.
@ The words of Jonadab the son of Rechab, that he commanded his sons not to drink wine, are performed; for unto this day they drink none, but obey their father’s commandment: notwithstanding I have spoken unto you, ##rising##note:x[<<Jer 7:13; 11:7; 25:3–4>>] early and speaking; but ye hearkened not unto me.
@ I have sent also unto you all my servants the prophets, rising up early and sending __them__, saying, ##Return##note:x[<<Jer 18:11; 25:5; Ezek 18:30–32>>] ye now every man from his evil way, and amend your doings, and go not after other gods to serve them, and ye shall dwell in the land which I have given to you and to your fathers: but ye have not inclined your ear, nor hearkened unto me.
@ ##Because the sons of Jonadab the son of Rechab have performed the commandment of their father, which he commanded them; but this people hath not hearkened unto me:##note:x[<<Jer 35:14; Mal 1:6>>]
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD God of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, I will bring upon Judah and upon all the inhabitants of Jerusalem all the evil that I have pronounced against them: ##because##note:x[<<Isa 65:12; 66:4; Jer 7:13>>] I have spoken unto them, but they have not heard; and I have called unto them, but they have not answered.

@ ¶ And Jeremiah said unto the house of the Rechabites, Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Because##note:x[<<Exod 20:12; Deut 5:16; Eph 6:1–3>>] ye have obeyed the commandment of Jonadab your father, and kept all his precepts, and done according unto all that he hath commanded you:
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Jonadab##note:x[<<Jer 33:17>>] the son of Rechab shall not want a man to ##stand##note:x[<<Jer 15:19; Luke 21:36>>] before me for ever.note:tn[__Jonadab…__: Heb. There shall not a man be cut off from Jonadab the son of Rechab to stand, etc]

=== @36

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Scroll Read in the Temple

@ ##And it came to pass in the fourth year of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah, __that__ this word came unto Jeremiah from the LORD, saying,##note:x[<<Jer 25:1>>]
@ Take thee ##a roll##note:x[<<Jer 30:2; 36:6>>] of a book, and ##write##note:x[<<Jer 30:2>>] therein all the words that I have spoken unto thee against Israel, and against Judah, and against all the nations, from the day I spake unto thee, from the days of Josiah, even unto this day.
@ It ##may be##note:x[<<Jer 18:8>>] that the house of Judah will hear all the evil which I purpose to do unto them; that ##they may##note:x[<<Jer 18:8>>] return every man from his evil way; ##that I##note:x[<<Acts 3:19>>] may forgive their iniquity and their sin.
@ Then Jeremiah called ##Baruch##note:x[<<Jer 32:12>>] the son of Neriah: and Baruch ##wrote##note:x[<<Jer 36:32>>] from the mouth of Jeremiah all the words of the LORD, which he had spoken unto him, ##upon##note:x[<<Jer 36:32>>] a roll of a book.
@ ##And Jeremiah commanded Baruch, saying, I __am__ shut up; I cannot go into the house of the LORD:##note:x[<<Jer 32:2; 33:1>>]
@ Therefore go thou, ##and read##note:x[<<Jer 36:8>>] in the roll, which thou hast written from my mouth, the words of the LORD in the ears of the people in the LORD’s house upon the fasting day: and also thou shalt read them in the ears of all Judah that come out of their cities.
@ ##It may##note:x[<<Jer 36:3>>] be they will present their supplication before the LORD, and will return every one from his evil way: for great __is__ the anger and the fury that the LORD hath pronounced against this people.note:tn[__they…__: Heb. their supplication shall fail]
@ And Baruch the son of Neriah did according to all that Jeremiah the prophet commanded him, reading in the book the words of the LORD in the LORD’s house.

@ And it came to pass in the fifth year of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah, in the ninth month, __that__ ##they##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:3>>] proclaimed a fast before the LORD to all the people in Jerusalem, and to all the people that came from the cities of Judah unto Jerusalem.
@ Then read Baruch in the book the words of Jeremiah in the house of the LORD, in the chamber of ##Gemariah##note:x[<<Jer 36:11>>] the son of ##Shaphan##note:x[<<Jer 36:11>>] ##the scribe##note:x[<<Jer 52:25>>], in the higher court, at the ##entry##note:tn[or, door] of the new gate of the LORD’s house, in the ears of all the people.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Scroll Read in the Palace

@ ¶ When Michaiah the son of Gemariah, the son of Shaphan, had heard out of the book all the words of the LORD,
@ Then he went down into the king’s house, into the scribe’s chamber: and, lo, all the princes sat there, __even__ Elishama the scribe, and Delaiah the son of Shemaiah, and ##Elnathan##note:x[<<2 Kgs 22:12; Jer 26:22; 36:25>>] the son of Achbor, and Gemariah the son of Shaphan, and Zedekiah the son of Hananiah, and all the princes.
@ Then Michaiah declared unto them all the words that he had heard, when Baruch read the book in the ears of the people.
@ Therefore all the princes sent Jehudi the son of Nethaniah, the son of Shelemiah, the son of Cushi, unto Baruch, saying, Take in thine hand the roll wherein thou hast read in the ears of the people, and come. So Baruch the son of Neriah took the roll in his hand, and came unto them.
@ And they said unto him, Sit down now, and read it in our ears. So Baruch read __it__ in their ears.
@ Now it came to pass, when they had heard all the words, they were afraid both one and other, and said unto Baruch, We will surely tell the king of all these words.
@ And they asked Baruch, saying, Tell us now, How didst thou write all these words at his mouth?
@ Then Baruch answered them, ##He##note:x[<<Jer 36:4>>] pronounced all these words unto me with his mouth, and I wrote __them__ with ink in the book.
@ ##Then said the princes unto Baruch, Go, hide thee, thou and Jeremiah; and let no man know where ye be.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 17:3>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jehoiakim Burns the Scroll

@ ¶ ##And they went in to the king into the court, but they laid up the roll in the chamber of Elishama the scribe, and told all the words in the ears of the king.##note:x[<<Jer 36:12>>]
@ So the king sent Jehudi to fetch the roll: and he took it out of Elishama the scribe’s chamber. ##And Jehudi##note:x[<<2 Kgs 22:10>>] read it in the ears of the king, and in the ears of all the princes which stood beside the king.
@ ##Now the king sat in the winterhouse in the ninth month: and __there was a fire__ on the hearth burning before him.##note:x[<<Amos 3:15>>]
@ And it came to pass, __that__ when Jehudi had read three or four leaves, ##he cut##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:8>>] it with the penknife, and cast __it__ into the fire that __was__ on the hearth, until all the roll was consumed in the fire that __was__ on the hearth.
@ Yet ##they##note:x[<<Ps 36:1; Jer 36:16>>] were not afraid, nor rent their garments, __neither__ the king, nor any of his servants that heard all these words.
@ Nevertheless Elnathan and Delaiah and Gemariah had made intercession to the king that he would not burn the roll: but he would not hear them.
@ But the king commanded Jerahmeel the son of Hammelech, and Seraiah the son of Azriel, and Shelemiah the son of Abdeel, ##to take##note:x[<<1 Kgs 19:1–3, 10, 14>>] Baruch the scribe and Jeremiah the prophet: but the LORD hid them.note:tn[__of Hammelech__: or, of the king]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Rewrites the Scroll

@ ¶ Then the word of the LORD came to Jeremiah, after that the king had burned the roll, and the words which Baruch wrote at the mouth of Jeremiah, saying,
@ Take thee again another roll, and write in it all the former words that were in the first roll, which Jehoiakim the king of Judah hath burned.
@ And thou shalt say to Jehoiakim king of Judah, Thus saith the LORD; Thou hast burned this roll, saying, ##Why##note:x[<<Isa 30:10; Jer 26:9>>] hast thou written therein, saying, The king of Babylon shall certainly come and destroy this land, and shall cause to cease from thence man and beast?
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of Jehoiakim king of Judah; ##He shall##note:x[<<Jer 22:30>>] have none to sit upon the throne of David: and his dead body shall be cast out in the day to the heat, and in the night to the frost.
@ And I will ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] him and his seed and his servants for their iniquity; and I ##will bring##note:x[<<Prov 29:1>>] upon them, and upon the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and upon the men of Judah, all the evil that I have pronounced against them; but they hearkened not.

@ ¶ Then took Jeremiah another roll, and gave it to Baruch the scribe, the son of Neriah; ##who##note:x[<<Jer 36:4, 18>>] wrote therein from the mouth of Jeremiah all the words of the book which Jehoiakim king of Judah had burned in the fire: and there were added besides unto them many ##like##note:tn[Heb. as they] words.

=== @37

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Warns Zedekiah

@ note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:12>>]And king ##Zedekiah##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:17>>] the son of Josiah reigned instead of ##Coniah##note:x[<<Jer 22:24>>] the son of Jehoiakim, whom Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon made king in the land of Judah.
@ But neither he, nor his servants, nor the people of the land, did hearken unto the words of the LORD, which he spake by the prophet Jeremiah.note:tn[__by…__: Heb. by the hand of the prophet]
@ And Zedekiah the king sent Jehucal the son of Shelemiah and ##Zephaniah##note:x[<<Jer 21:1–2; 29:25>>] the son of Maaseiah the priest to the prophet Jeremiah, saying, ##Pray##note:x[<<Jer 21:1–2>>] now unto the LORD our God for us.
@ Now Jeremiah came in and went out among the people: ##for##note:x[<<Jer 37:15>>] they had not put him into prison.
@ note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:7; Ezek 17:15>>]Then Pharaoh’s army was come forth out of Egypt: and when the Chaldeans that besieged Jerusalem heard tidings of them, ##they##note:x[<<Jer 37:11>>] departed from Jerusalem.

@ ¶ Then came the word of the LORD unto the prophet Jeremiah, saying,
@ note:x[<<Lam 4:17>>]##Thus##note:x[<<Jer 21:2; 37:3>>] saith the LORD, the God of Israel; Thus shall ye say to the king of Judah, that sent you unto me to enquire of me; Behold, Pharaoh’s army, which is come forth to help you, shall return to Egypt into their own land.
@ And the Chaldeans shall come again, and fight against this city, and take it, and burn it with fire.
@ Thus saith the LORD; ##Deceive##note:x[<<Obad 1:3>>] not ##yourselves##note:tn[Heb. your souls], saying, The Chaldeans shall surely depart from us: for they shall not depart.
@ For though ye had smitten the whole army of the Chaldeans that fight against you, and there remained __but__ ##wounded##note:tn[Heb. thrust through] men among them, __yet__ should they rise up every man in his tent, and burn this city with fire.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Imprisoned

@ ¶ And it came to pass, that when the army of the Chaldeans was broken up from Jerusalem for fear of Pharaoh’s army,note:tn[__broken…__: Heb. made to ascend]
@ Then Jeremiah went forth out of Jerusalem to go into the land of Benjamin, to separate himself thence in the midst of the people.note:tn[__separate…__: or, to slip away from thence in the midst of the people]
@ And when he was ##in the##note:x[<<Jer 38:7; Zech 14:10>>] gate of Benjamin, a captain of the ward __was__ there, whose name __was__ Irijah, the son of Shelemiah, the son of Hananiah; and he took Jeremiah the prophet, saying, Thou fallest away to the Chaldeans.
@ Then said Jeremiah, __It is__ ##false##note:tn[Heb. falsehood, or, a lie]; I fall not away to the Chaldeans. But he hearkened not to him: so Irijah took Jeremiah, and brought him to the princes.
@ Wherefore the princes were wroth with Jeremiah, and smote him, and put him in prison ##in the##note:x[<<Jer 38:26>>] house of Jonathan the scribe: for they had made that the prison.

@ ¶ When Jeremiah was entered ##into the dungeon##note:x[<<Jer 38:6>>], and into the ##cabins##note:tn[or, cells], and Jeremiah had remained there many days;
@ Then Zedekiah the king sent, and took him out: and the king asked him secretly in his house, and said, Is there __any__ word from the LORD? And Jeremiah said, There is: for, said he, ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Jer 21:7>>] be delivered into the hand of the king of Babylon.
@ ##Moreover Jeremiah said unto king Zedekiah, What have I offended against thee, or against thy servants, or against this people, that ye have put me in prison?##note:x[<<John 10:32; Acts 25:8>>]
@ ##Where##note:x[<<Jer 2:28>>] __are__ now ##your##note:x[<<Jer 28:10–17>>] prophets which prophesied unto you, saying, The king of Babylon shall not come against you, nor against this land?
@ Therefore hear now, I pray thee, O my lord the king: let my supplication, I pray thee, be accepted before thee; that thou cause me not to return to the house of Jonathan the scribe, lest I die there.note:tn[__let…__: Heb. let my supplication fall]
@ Then Zedekiah the king commanded that they should commit Jeremiah ##into the##note:x[<<Jer 38:13, 28>>] court of the prison, and that they should give him daily a piece of bread out of the bakers’ street, until all the bread in the city were spent. ##Thus##note:x[<<Jer 38:13>>] Jeremiah remained in the court of the prison.

=== @38

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Cast into the Cistern

@ Then Shephatiah the son of Mattan, and Gedaliah the son of Pashur, and Jucal the son of Shelemiah, and Pashur the son of Malchiah, heard the words that Jeremiah had spoken unto all the people, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD, ##He##note:x[<<Jer 34:17>>] that remaineth in this city shall die by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence: but he that goeth forth to the Chaldeans shall live; for he ##shall have##note:x[<<Jer 39:18; 45:5>>] his life for a prey, and shall live.
@ note:x[<<Jer 21:10; 32:3–5>>]Thus saith the LORD, This city shall surely be given into the hand of the king of Babylon’s army, which shall take it.
@ Therefore ##the princes##note:x[<<Jer 26:11>>] said unto the king, We beseech thee, let this man be put to death: for ##thus##note:x[<<Acts 16:20>>] he weakeneth the hands of the men of war that remain in this city, and the hands of all the people, in speaking such words unto them: for this man seeketh not the ##welfare##note:tn[Heb. peace] of this people, but the hurt.
@ Then Zedekiah the king said, Behold, he __is__ in your hand: ##for##note:x[<<John 19:12–16>>] the king __is__ not __he that__ can do __any__ thing against you.
@ Then ##took##note:x[<<Jer 37:21>>] they Jeremiah, and cast him ##into##note:x[<<Jer 37:16>>] the dungeon of Malchiah the son of Hammelech, that __was__ in the court of the prison: and they let down Jeremiah with cords. ##And in##note:x[<<Jer 38:22>>] the dungeon __there was__ no water, but mire: so Jeremiah sunk in the mire.note:tn[__of Hammelech__: or, of the king]

@ ¶ Now when Ebed-melech the Ethiopian, one of the ##eunuchs##note:x[<<Jer 29:2>>] which was in ##the king##note:x[<<Jer 37:13; Amos 5:10>>]’s house, heard that they had put Jeremiah in the dungeon; the king then sitting in the gate of Benjamin;
@ Ebed-melech went forth out of the king’s house, and spake to the king, saying,
@ My lord the king, these men have done evil in all that they have done to Jeremiah the prophet, whom they have cast into the dungeon; and he ##is like to die##note:x[<<Jer 37:21>>] for hunger in the place where he is: for __there is__ no more bread in the city.note:tn[__he is like…__: Heb. he will die]
@ Then the king commanded Ebed-melech the Ethiopian, saying, Take from hence thirty men with thee, and take up Jeremiah the prophet out of the dungeon, before he die.note:tn[__with thee__: Heb. in thine hand]
@ So Ebed-melech took the men with him, and went into the house of the king under the treasury, and took thence old cast clouts and old rotten rags, and let them down by cords into the dungeon to Jeremiah.
@ And Ebed-melech the Ethiopian said unto Jeremiah, Put now __these__ old cast clouts and rotten rags under thine armholes under the cords. And Jeremiah did so.
@ So they drew up ##Jeremiah##note:x[<<Jer 37:21>>] with cords, and took him up out of the dungeon: and Jeremiah remained in the court of the prison.

@ ¶ Then Zedekiah the king ##sent##note:x[<<Jer 21:1–2; 37:17>>], and took Jeremiah the prophet unto him into the ##third##note:tn[or, principal] entry that __is__ in the house of the LORD: and the king said unto Jeremiah, ##I will##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:16>>] ask thee a thing; hide nothing from me.
@ ##Then Jeremiah said unto Zedekiah, If I declare __it__ unto thee, wilt thou not surely put me to death? and if I give thee counsel, wilt thou not hearken unto me?##note:x[<<Luke 22:67–68>>]
@ So Zedekiah the king ##sware##note:x[<<Jer 37:17; John 3:2>>] secretly unto Jeremiah, saying, __As__ the LORD liveth, that made us this soul, I will not put thee to death, neither will I give thee into the hand ##of these##note:x[<<Jer 38:1–6>>] men that seek thy life.
@ Then said Jeremiah unto Zedekiah, Thus saith the LORD, the God of hosts, the God of Israel; ##If thou##note:x[<<Jer 27:12, 17; 38:2>>] wilt assuredly go forth unto the king of Babylon’s princes, then thy soul shall live, and this city shall not be burned with fire; and thou shalt live, and thine house:
@ But if thou wilt not go forth to the king of Babylon’s princes, ##then##note:x[<<Jer 24:8–10; 32:3–5; 38:3>>] shall this city be given into the hand of the Chaldeans, and they shall burn it with fire, and thou shalt not escape out of their hand.
@ And Zedekiah the king said unto Jeremiah, ##I##note:x[<<Isa 51:12–13; 57:11; John 12:42>>] am afraid of the Jews that are fallen to the Chaldeans, lest they deliver me into their hand, and they mock me.
@ But Jeremiah said, They shall not deliver __thee__. ##Obey##note:x[<<Jer 26:13; Dan 4:27>>], I beseech thee, the voice of the LORD, which I speak unto thee: so it shall be well unto thee, ##and thy##note:x[<<Isa 55:3>>] soul shall live.
@ But if thou refuse to go forth, this __is__ the word that the LORD hath shewed me:
@ And, behold, all the women that are left in the king of Judah’s house __shall be__ brought forth to the king of Babylon’s princes, and those __women__ shall say, Thy friends have set thee on, and have prevailed against thee: thy feet are sunk in the mire, __and__ they are turned away back.note:tn[__Thy friends__: Heb. Men of thy peace]
@ So ##they shall##note:x[<<Jer 38:18; 39:6; 41:10>>] bring out all thy wives and thy children to the Chaldeans: and thou shalt not escape out of their hand, but shalt be taken by the hand of the king of Babylon: and thou shalt cause this city to be burned with fire.note:tn[__thou shalt cause…__: Heb. thou shalt burn, etc]

@ ¶ Then said Zedekiah unto Jeremiah, Let no man know of these words, and thou shalt not die.
@ But if the princes hear that I have talked with thee, and they come unto thee, and say unto thee, Declare unto us now what thou hast said unto the king, hide it not from us, and we will not put thee to death; also what the king said unto thee:
@ note:x[<<Jer 37:20>>]Then thou shalt say unto them, I presented my supplication before the king, that he would not cause me to return to Jonathan’s house, to die there.
@ Then came all the princes unto Jeremiah, and asked him: and he told them according to all these words that the king had commanded. So they left off speaking with him; for the matter was not perceived.note:tn[__they…__: Heb. they were silent from him]
@ ##So Jeremiah abode in the court of the prison until the day that Jerusalem was taken: and he was __there__ when Jerusalem was taken.##note:x[<<Jer 37:21; 39:14>>]

=== @39

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Fall of Jerusalem

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 25:1–12; 2 Chronicles 36:15–21>>)

@ In the ninth year of Zedekiah king of Judah, in the tenth month, came Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon and all his army against Jerusalem, and they besieged it.
@ __And__ in the eleventh year of Zedekiah, in the fourth month, the ninth __day__ of the month, the city was broken up.
@ And ##all the##note:x[<<Jer 21:4; 38:17>>] princes of the king of Babylon came in, and sat in the middle gate, __even__ Nergal-sharezer, Samgar-nebo, Sarsechim, Rab-saris, Nergal-sharezer, Rab-mag, with all the residue of the princes of the king of Babylon.

@ ¶ And it came to pass, __that__ when Zedekiah the king of Judah saw them, and all the men of war, then they fled, and went forth out of the city by night, by the way of the king’s garden, by the gate betwixt the two walls: and he went out the way of the plain.
@ But the Chaldeans’ army pursued after them, and overtook Zedekiah ##in the plains##note:x[<<Josh 5:10>>] of Jericho: and when they had taken him, they brought him up to Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon to ##Riblah##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:33>>] in the land of Hamath, where he gave judgment upon him.note:tn[__gave…__: Heb. spake with him judgments]
@ Then the king of Babylon ##slew the##note:x[<<Jer 52:10>>] sons of Zedekiah in Riblah before his eyes: also the king of Babylon slew all the nobles of Judah.
@ Moreover ##he put##note:x[<<Ezek 12:13>>] out Zedekiah’s eyes, and bound him with chains, to carry him to Babylon.note:tn[__with…__: Heb. with two brasen chains, or, fetters]

@ ¶ And the Chaldeans ##burned##note:x[<<Jer 21:10; 38:18>>] the king’s house, and the houses of the people, with fire, ##and brake##note:x[<<Neh 1:3>>] down the walls of Jerusalem.
@ Then ##Nebuzar-adan##note:x[<<Jer 40:1>>] the ##captain of the guard##note:x[<<Gen 37:36>>] carried away captive into Babylon the remnant of the people that remained in the city, and those that fell away, that fell to him, with the rest of the people that remained.note:tn[__captain…__: or, chief marshal: Heb. chief of the executioners, or, slaughtermen]
@ But Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard ##left of##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:12; Jer 40:7>>] the poor of the people, which had nothing, in the land of Judah, and gave them vineyards and fields at the same time.note:tn[__captain…__: or, chief marshal: Heb. chief of the executioners, or, slaughtermen]note:tn[__at the…__: Heb. in that day]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Delivered

@ ¶ Now Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon gave charge concerning Jeremiah ##to##note:tn[Heb. by the hand of] Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard, saying,note:tn[__captain…__: or, chief marshal: Heb. chief of the executioners, or, slaughtermen]
@ Take him, and look well to him, and ##do him##note:x[<<Prov 16:7>>] no harm; but do unto him even as he shall say unto thee.note:tn[__look…__: Heb. set thine eyes upon him]
@ So Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard sent, and Nebushasban, Rab-saris, and Nergal-sharezer, Rab-mag, and all the king of Babylon’s princes;note:tn[__captain…__: or, chief marshal: Heb. chief of the executioners, or, slaughtermen]
@ Even they sent, and ##took##note:x[<<Jer 38:28>>] Jeremiah out of the court of the prison, and committed him unto Gedaliah the son of Ahikam the son of Shaphan, that he should carry him home: so he dwelt among the people.

@ ¶ Now the word of the LORD came unto Jeremiah, while he was shut up in the court of the prison, saying,
@ Go and speak to Ebed-melech the Ethiopian, saying, Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##Behold##note:x[<<Dan 9:12>>], I will bring my words upon this city for evil, and not for good; and they shall be __accomplished__ in that day before thee.
@ But ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 41:1–2; 50:15; 91:14–15>>] deliver thee in that day, saith the LORD: and thou shalt not be given into the hand of the men of whom thou __art__ afraid.
@ For I will surely deliver thee, and thou shalt not fall by the sword, ##but##note:x[<<Jer 21:9; 38:2>>] thy life shall be for a prey unto thee: ##because##note:x[<<Ps 34:22>>] thou hast put thy trust in me, saith the LORD.

=== @40

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Remains in Judah

@ The word that came to Jeremiah from the LORD, after that Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard had let him go from ##Ramah##note:x[<<Josh 18:25; Jer 31:15>>], when he had taken him being ##bound##note:x[<<Acts 12:6–7>>] in ##chains##note:tn[or, manicles] among all that were carried away captive of Jerusalem and Judah, which were carried away captive unto Babylon.
@ And the captain of the guard took Jeremiah, and said unto him, ##The LORD##note:x[<<Deut 29:24–28; Jer 22:8–9>>] thy God hath pronounced this evil upon this place.
@ Now the LORD hath brought __it__, and done according as he hath said: ##because##note:x[<<Rom 2:5>>] ye have sinned against the LORD, and have not obeyed his voice, therefore this thing is come upon you.
@ And now, behold, I loose thee this day from the chains which __were__ upon thine hand. If it seem good unto thee to come with me into Babylon, come; and I will look well unto thee: but if it seem ill unto thee to come with me into Babylon, forbear: behold, all the land __is__ before thee: whither it seemeth good and convenient for thee to go, thither go.note:tn[__were…__: or, are upon thine hand]note:tn[__I will…__: Heb. I will set mine eye upon thee]
@ Now while he was not yet gone back, __he said__, ##Go back##note:x[<<Jer 39:14>>] also to Gedaliah the son of Ahikam the son of Shaphan, whom the king of Babylon hath made governor over the cities of Judah, and dwell with him among the people: or go wheresoever it seemeth convenient unto thee to go. So the captain of the guard gave him victuals and a reward, and let him go.
@ ##Then##note:x[<<Jer 39:14>>] went Jeremiah unto Gedaliah the son of Ahikam to ##Mizpah##note:x[<<Judg 20:1>>]; and dwelt with him among the people that were left in the land.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Gedaliah Governs in Judah

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 25:22–24>>)

@ ¶ Now when all the captains of the forces which __were__ in the fields, __even__ they and their men, heard that the king of Babylon had made Gedaliah the son of Ahikam governor in the land, and had committed unto him men, and women, and children, and of ##the poor##note:x[<<Jer 39:10>>] of the land, of them that were not carried away captive to Babylon;
@ Then they came to Gedaliah to Mizpah, even Ishmael the son of Nethaniah, and ##Johanan##note:x[<<Jer 42:1>>] and Jonathan the sons of Kareah, and Seraiah the son of Tanhumeth, and the sons of Ephai the Netophathite, and Jezaniah the son of a ##Maachathite##note:x[<<Deut 3:14>>], they and their men.
@ And Gedaliah the son of Ahikam the son of Shaphan sware unto them and to their men, saying, ##Fear##note:x[<<Jer 27:11>>] not to serve the Chaldeans: dwell in the land, and serve the king of Babylon, and it shall be well with you.
@ As for me, behold, I will dwell at Mizpah to ##serve##note:x[<<Deut 1:38; Jer 35:19>>] the Chaldeans, which will come unto us: but ye, gather ye wine, and ##summer##note:x[<<Jer 40:12>>] fruits, and oil, and put __them__ in your vessels, and dwell in your cities that ye have taken.note:tn[__to serve__: Heb. to stand before]
@ Likewise when all the Jews that __were__ in Moab, and among the Ammonites, and in Edom, and that __were__ in all the countries, heard that the king of Babylon had left a remnant of Judah, and that he had set over them Gedaliah the son of Ahikam the son of Shaphan;
@ Even all the Jews returned out of all places whither they were driven, and came to the land of Judah, to Gedaliah, unto Mizpah, and gathered wine and summer fruits very much.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Plot against Gedaliah

@ ¶ Moreover Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces that __were__ in the fields, came to Gedaliah to Mizpah,
@ And said unto him, Dost thou certainly know that Baalis the king of the ##Ammonites##note:x[<<Jer 25:21; 41:10>>] hath sent ##Ishmael##note:x[<<Jer 41:10>>] the son of Nethaniah to slay thee? But Gedaliah the son of Ahikam believed them not.note:tn[__to slay…__: Heb. to strike thee in soul?]
@ Then Johanan the son of Kareah spake to Gedaliah in Mizpah secretly, saying, Let me go, I pray thee, and I will slay Ishmael the son of Nethaniah, and no man shall know __it:__ wherefore should he slay thee, that all the Jews which are gathered unto thee should be scattered, and the remnant in Judah perish?
@ But Gedaliah the son of Ahikam said unto Johanan the son of Kareah, Thou shalt not do this thing: for thou speakest falsely of Ishmael.

=== @41

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Murder of Gedaliah

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 25:25–26>>)

@ Now it came to pass in the seventh month, __that__ ##Ishmael##note:x[<<Jer 40:6, 8>>] the son of Nethaniah the son of Elishama, of the seed royal, and the princes of the king, even ten men with him, came unto Gedaliah the son of Ahikam to Mizpah; and there ##they did##note:x[<<Ps 41:9>>] eat bread together in Mizpah.
@ Then arose Ishmael the son of Nethaniah, and the ten men that were with him, ##and smote##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:25>>] Gedaliah the son of Ahikam the son of Shaphan with the sword, and slew him, whom the king of Babylon had made governor over the land.
@ Ishmael also slew all the Jews that were with him, __even__ with Gedaliah, at Mizpah, and the Chaldeans that were found there, __and__ the men of war.
@ And it came to pass the second day after he had slain Gedaliah, and no man knew __it__,
@ That there came certain from Shechem, from ##Shiloh##note:x[<<Josh 18:1>>], and from ##Samaria##note:x[<<1 Kgs 16:24>>], __even__ fourscore men, having ##their beards##note:x[<<Deut 14:1>>] shaven, and their clothes rent, and having cut themselves, with offerings and incense in their hand, to bring __them__ to the house of the LORD.
@ And Ishmael the son of Nethaniah went forth from Mizpah to meet them, weeping all along as he went: and it came to pass, as he met them, he said unto them, Come to Gedaliah the son of Ahikam.note:tn[__weeping…__: Heb. in going and weeping]
@ And it was __so__, when they came into the midst of the city, that Ishmael the son of Nethaniah slew them, __and cast them__ into the midst of the pit, he, and the men that __were__ with him.
@ But ten men were found among them that said unto Ishmael, Slay us not: for we have treasures in the field, of wheat, and of barley, and of oil, and of honey. So he forbare, and slew them not among their brethren.
@ Now the pit wherein Ishmael had cast all the dead bodies of the men, whom he had slain because of Gedaliah, __was__ it which Asa the king had made for fear of Baasha king of Israel: __and__ Ishmael the son of Nethaniah filled it with __them that were__ slain.note:tn[__because…__: or, near Gedaliah: Heb. by the hand, or, by the side of Gedaliah]
@ Then Ishmael carried away captive all the residue of the people that __were__ in Mizpah, __even__ the king’s daughters, and all the people that remained in Mizpah, ##whom##note:x[<<Jer 40:7>>] Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard had committed to Gedaliah the son of Ahikam: and Ishmael the son of Nethaniah carried them away captive, and departed to go over ##to the##note:x[<<Jer 40:14>>] Ammonites.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Johanan Rescues the Captives

@ ¶ But when Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces that __were__ with him, heard of all the evil that Ishmael the son of Nethaniah had done,
@ Then they took all the men, and went to fight with Ishmael the son of Nethaniah, and found him by ##the great##note:x[<<2 Sam 2:13>>] waters that __are__ in Gibeon.
@ Now it came to pass, __that__ when all the people which __were__ with Ishmael saw Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces that __were__ with him, then they were glad.
@ So all the people that Ishmael had carried away captive from Mizpah cast about and returned, and went unto Johanan the son of Kareah.
@ But Ishmael the son of Nethaniah ##escaped##note:x[<<Job 21:30; Prov 28:17>>] from Johanan with eight men, and went to the Ammonites.
@ Then took Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces that __were__ with him, all the remnant of the people whom he had recovered from Ishmael the son of Nethaniah, from Mizpah, after __that__ he had slain Gedaliah the son of Ahikam, __even__ mighty men of war, and the women, and the children, and the eunuchs, whom he had brought again from Gibeon:
@ And they departed, and dwelt in the habitation of ##Chimham##note:x[<<2 Sam 19:37–38>>], which is by Beth-lehem, ##to go##note:x[<<Jer 42:14>>] to enter into Egypt,
@ Because of the Chaldeans: ##for they##note:x[<<Jer 42:16; Luke 12:4–5>>] were afraid of them, because Ishmael the son of Nethaniah had slain Gedaliah the son of Ahikam, ##whom##note:x[<<Jer 40:5>>] the king of Babylon made governor in the land.

=== @42

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning against Going to Egypt

@ Then ##all the##note:x[<<Jer 40:13; 41:11>>] captains of the forces, and Johanan the son of Kareah, and Jezaniah the son of Hoshaiah, and all the people ##from##note:x[<<Jer 6:13>>] the least even unto the greatest, came near,
@ And said unto Jeremiah the prophet, Let, we beseech thee, our supplication ##be accepted before thee##note:x[<<Jer 36:7>>], ##and pray##note:x[<<Jer 37:3>>] for us unto the LORD thy God, __even__ for all this remnant; (for we are ##left##note:x[<<Lev 26:22>>] __but__ a few of many, as thine eyes do behold us:)note:tn[__Let…__: or, Let our supplication fall before thee]
@ ##That the LORD thy God may shew us the way wherein we may walk, and the thing that we may do.##note:x[<<Ps 86:11; Prov 3:6; Jer 6:16>>]
@ Then Jeremiah the prophet said unto them, I have heard __you;__ behold, ##I will pray##note:x[<<Exod 8:29; 1 Sam 12:23>>] unto the LORD your God according to your words; and it shall come to pass, __that__ whatsoever thing the LORD shall answer you, I will declare __it__ unto you; ##I will keep##note:x[<<1 Sam 3:17–18>>] nothing back from you.
@ Then they said to Jeremiah, ##The LORD be##note:x[<<Gen 31:50; Judg 11:10; Mal 2:14>>] a true and faithful witness between us, if we do not even according to all things for the which the LORD thy God shall send thee to us.
@ Whether ##__it be__ good##note:x[<<Rom 8:7>>], or whether __it be__ evil, we will obey the voice of the LORD our God, to whom we send thee; ##that it##note:x[<<Deut 5:29; Jer 7:23>>] may be well with us, when we obey the voice of the LORD our God.

@ ¶ ##And it came to pass after ten days, that the word of the LORD came unto Jeremiah.##note:x[<<Ps 27:14>>]
@ Then called he ##Johanan##note:x[<<Jer 42:1>>] the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces which __were__ with him, and all the people from the least even to the greatest,
@ And said ##unto##note:x[<<2 Kgs 19:4, 6; Jer 42:2>>] them, Thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel, unto whom ye sent me to present your supplication before him;
@ If ye will still abide in this land, ##then##note:x[<<Jer 24:6; 31:28; Ezek 36:36>>] will I build you, and not pull __you__ down, and I will plant you, and not pluck __you__ up: for I repent me of the evil that I have done unto you.
@ Be not afraid of the king of Babylon, of whom ye are afraid; be not afraid of him, saith the LORD: ##for I##note:x[<<Isa 43:2; Jer 1:19; Rom 8:31>>] __am__ with you to save you, and to deliver you from his hand.
@ ##And I will shew mercies unto you, that he may have mercy upon you, and cause you to return to your own land.##note:x[<<Neh 1:11; Ps 106:45–46; Prov 16:7>>]

@ ¶ ##But if ye say, We will not dwell in this land, neither obey the voice of the LORD your God,##note:x[<<Jer 44:16>>]
@ Saying, No; but ##we will go##note:x[<<Jer 41:17>>] into the land of Egypt, where we shall see no war, ##nor hear##note:x[<<Jer 4:19>>] the sound of the trumpet, nor have hunger of bread; and there will we dwell:
@ And now therefore hear the word of the LORD, ye remnant of Judah; Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##If##note:x[<<Deut 17:16; Jer 44:12–14>>] ye wholly set your faces to enter into Egypt, and go to sojourn there;
@ Then it shall come to pass, ##__that__ the sword##note:x[<<Jer 44:27; Ezek 11:8>>], which ye feared, shall overtake you there in the land of Egypt, and the famine, whereof ye were afraid, shall follow close after you there in Egypt; and ##there ye##note:x[<<Jer 44:27>>] shall die.note:tn[__shall follow…__: Heb. shall cleave after you]
@ So shall it be with all the men that set their faces to go into Egypt to sojourn there; ##they shall##note:x[<<Jer 24:10; 42:22>>] die by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence: and ##none##note:x[<<Jer 44:28>>] of them shall remain or escape from the evil that I will bring upon them.note:tn[__So…__: Heb. So shall all the men be]
@ For thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; ##As mine##note:x[<<Jer 7:20; 39:1–9>>] anger and my fury hath been poured forth upon the inhabitants of Jerusalem; so shall my fury be poured forth upon you, when ye shall enter into Egypt: and ##ye shall be##note:x[<<Jer 18:16>>] an execration, and an astonishment, and a curse, and a reproach; and ye shall see this place no more.

@ ¶ The LORD hath said concerning you, O ye remnant of Judah; ##Go##note:x[<<Deut 17:16; Isa 30:1–7>>] ye not into Egypt: know certainly that I have admonished you this day.note:tn[__admonished…__: Heb. testified against you]
@ For ye dissembled in your hearts, when ye sent me unto the LORD your God, saying, Pray for us unto the LORD our God; and according unto all that the LORD our God shall say, so declare unto us, and we will do __it__.note:tn[__ye dissembled…__: or, ye have used deceit against your souls]
@ And __now__ ##I have##note:x[<<Ezek 2:7>>] this day declared __it__ to you; but ye have not obeyed the voice of the LORD your God, nor any __thing__ for the which he hath sent me unto you.
@ Now therefore ##know##note:x[<<Jer 42:17; Ezek 6:11>>] certainly that ye shall die by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence, ##in the##note:x[<<Hos 9:6>>] place whither ye desire to go __and__ to sojourn.note:tn[__to go…__: or, to go to sojourn]

=== @43

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah Taken to Egypt

@ And it came to pass, __that__ when Jeremiah ##had made##note:x[<<Jer 26:8>>] an end of speaking unto all the people all the words of the LORD their God, for which the LORD their God had sent him to them, __even__ all these words,
@ Then spake Azariah the son of Hoshaiah, and Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the proud men, saying unto Jeremiah, ##Thou speakest##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:13; Isa 7:9>>] falsely: the LORD our God hath not sent thee to say, Go not into Egypt to sojourn there:
@ But Baruch the son of Neriah setteth thee on against us, for to deliver us into the hand of the Chaldeans, that they might put us to death, and carry us away captives into Babylon.
@ So Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces, and all the people, ##obeyed##note:x[<<Jer 42:5–6>>] not the voice of the LORD, to dwell in the land of Judah.
@ But Johanan the son of Kareah, and all the captains of the forces, ##took##note:x[<<Jer 40:11–12>>] all the remnant of Judah, that were returned from all nations, whither they had been driven, to dwell in the land of Judah;
@ __Even__ men, and women, and children, and the king’s daughters, and ##every##note:x[<<Jer 39:10; 40:7>>] person that Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard had left with Gedaliah the son of Ahikam the son of Shaphan, and Jeremiah the prophet, and Baruch the son of Neriah.
@ note:x[<<Jer 44:1>>]So they came into the land of Egypt: for they obeyed not the voice of the LORD: thus came they __even__ to ##Tahpanhes##note:x[<<Jer 2:16>>].

@ ¶ Then came the word of the LORD unto Jeremiah in Tahpanhes, saying,
@ Take great stones in thine hand, and hide them in the clay in the brickkiln, which __is__ at the entry of Pharaoh’s house in Tahpanhes, in the sight of the men of Judah;
@ And say unto them, Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, ##I will send##note:x[<<Jer 1:15; Ezek 29:18–20>>] and take Nebuchadrezzar the king of Babylon, ##my servant##note:x[<<Isa 44:28>>], and will set his throne upon these stones that I have hid; and he shall spread his royal pavilion over them.
