@ And when he cometh, ##he shall smite##note:x[<<Ezek 29:19–20>>] the land of Egypt, __and deliver__ ##such __as are__ for death##note:x[<<Jer 15:2; 44:13>>] to death; and such __as are__ for captivity to captivity; and such __as are__ for the sword to the sword.
@ And I will kindle a fire ##in the##note:x[<<Isa 19:1; Ezek 30:13>>] houses of the gods of Egypt; and he shall burn them, and carry them away captives: and he shall array himself with the land of Egypt, as a shepherd ##putteth##note:x[<<Ps 109:18–19>>] on his garment; and he shall go forth from thence in peace.
@ He shall break also the ##images##note:tn[Heb. statues, or, standing images] of ##Beth-shemesh##note:tn[or, The house of the sun], that __is__ in the land of Egypt; and the houses of the gods of the Egyptians shall he burn with fire.

=== @44

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on the Jews in Egypt

@ note:x[<<Jer 46:14>>]The word that came to Jeremiah concerning all the Jews which dwell in the land of Egypt, which dwell at ##Migdol##note:x[<<Exod 14:2; Jer 46:14>>], and at Tahpanhes, and at Noph, and in the country of Pathros, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Ye have seen all the evil that I have brought upon Jerusalem, and upon all the cities of Judah; and, behold, this day they __are__ ##a desolation##note:x[<<Isa 6:11; Jer 9:11; 34:22>>], and no man dwelleth therein,
@ Because of their wickedness which they have committed to provoke me to anger, in that they went to burn incense, __and__ to serve other ##gods##note:x[<<Deut 13:6; 29:26; 32:17>>], whom they knew not, __neither__ they, ye, nor your fathers.
@ Howbeit ##I sent##note:x[<<Jer 7:13, 25; 26:5>>] unto you all my servants the prophets, rising early and sending __them__, saying, Oh, do not this abominable thing that I hate.
@ But they hearkened not, nor inclined their ear to turn from their wickedness, to burn no incense unto other gods.
@ Wherefore ##my fury##note:x[<<Jer 7:20>>] and mine anger was poured forth, and was kindled in the cities of Judah and in the streets of Jerusalem; and they are wasted __and__ desolate, as at this day.
@ Therefore now thus saith the LORD, the God of hosts, the God of Israel; Wherefore commit ye __this__ great evil ##against##note:x[<<Num 16:38; Hab 2:10>>] your souls, ##to cut##note:x[<<Jer 51:22>>] off from you man and woman, child and suckling, out of Judah, to leave you none to remain;note:tn[__out…__: Heb. out of the midst of Judah]
@ In that ##ye provoke##note:x[<<2 Kgs 17:15–17; Jer 25:6–7>>] me unto wrath with the works of your hands, burning incense unto other gods in the land of Egypt, whither ye be gone to dwell, that ye might cut yourselves off, and that ye might be ##a curse##note:x[<<Jer 42:18>>] and a reproach among all the nations of the earth?
@ Have ye forgotten ##the wickedness of your##note:x[<<Jer 7:17–18>>] fathers, and the wickedness of the kings of Judah, and the wickedness of their wives, and your own wickedness, and the wickedness of your wives, which they have committed in the land of Judah, and in the streets of Jerusalem?note:tn[__wickedness…__: Heb. wickednesses, or, punishments, etc]
@ They are not ##humbled##note:tn[Heb. contrite] __even__ unto this day, ##neither##note:x[<<Prov 28:14>>] have they feared, nor walked in my law, nor in my statutes, that I set before you and before your fathers.

@ ¶ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 21:10>>] set my face against you for evil, and to cut off all Judah.
@ And ##I will take##note:x[<<Jer 42:15–18, 22>>] the remnant of Judah, that have set their faces to go into the land of Egypt to sojourn there, and they shall all be consumed, __and__ fall in the land of Egypt; they shall __even__ be consumed by the sword __and__ by the famine: they shall die, from the least even unto the greatest, by the sword and by the famine: and they shall be an execration, __and__ an astonishment, and a curse, and a reproach.
@ For I will punish them that dwell in the land of Egypt, as I have punished Jerusalem, by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence:
@ So that none of the remnant of Judah, which are gone into the land of Egypt to sojourn there, shall escape or remain, that they should return into the land of Judah, to the ##which they##note:x[<<Jer 22:26–27>>] have a desire to return to dwell there: ##for none##note:x[<<Rom 9:27>>] shall return but such as shall escape.note:tn[__have…__: Heb. lift up their soul]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Stubbornness of the People

@ ¶ Then ##all the##note:x[<<Prov 11:21; Jer 5:1–5>>] men which knew that their wives had burned incense unto other gods, and all the women that stood by, a great multitude, even all the people that dwelt in the land of Egypt, in Pathros, answered Jeremiah, saying,
@ __As for__ the word that thou hast spoken unto us in the name of the LORD, ##we##note:x[<<Luke 19:27>>] will not hearken unto thee.
@ But we will certainly do whatsoever thing goeth forth out of our own mouth, to burn incense unto the ##queen of heaven##note:x[<<Jer 7:18>>], and to pour out drink offerings unto her, ##as we##note:x[<<Neh 9:34>>] have done, we, and ##our fathers##note:x[<<Neh 9:34>>], our kings, and our princes, in the cities of Judah, and in the streets of Jerusalem: for __then__ had we plenty of ##victuals##note:tn[Heb. bread], and were well, and saw no evil.note:tn[__queen…__: or, frame of heaven]
@ But since we left off to burn incense to the queen of heaven, and to pour out drink offerings unto her, ##we have##note:x[<<Num 11:5–6; Mal 3:13–15>>] wanted all __things__, and have been consumed by the sword and by the famine.
@ And when ##we burned##note:x[<<Jer 7:18; 44:15>>] incense to the queen of heaven, and poured out drink offerings unto her, did we make her cakes to worship her, and pour out drink offerings unto her, without our ##men##note:tn[or, husbands?]?

[discrete.pericope]
==== Calamity for the Jews

@ ¶ Then Jeremiah said unto all the people, to the men, and to the women, and to all the people which had given him __that__ answer, saying,
@ The incense that ye burned in the cities of Judah, ##and in##note:x[<<Jer 11:13; 44:9, 17>>] the streets of Jerusalem, ye, and your fathers, your kings, and your princes, and the people of the land, did not the LORD remember them, and came it __not__ into his mind?
@ So that the LORD could no longer bear, because of the evil of your doings, __and__ because of the abominations which ye have committed; therefore is ##your land##note:x[<<Jer 25:18>>] a desolation, and an astonishment, and a curse, without an inhabitant, as at this day.
@ Because ye have burned incense, and because ye have sinned against the LORD, and have not obeyed the voice of the LORD, nor walked in his law, nor in his statutes, nor in his testimonies; ##therefore##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:9; Dan 9:11–12>>] this evil is happened unto you, as at this day.
@ Moreover Jeremiah said unto all the people, and to all the women, Hear the word of the LORD, ##all Judah##note:x[<<Jer 43:7>>] that __are__ in the land of Egypt:
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, saying; Ye and your wives have both spoken with your mouths, and fulfilled with your hand, saying, We will surely perform our vows that we have vowed, to burn incense to the queen of heaven, and to pour out drink offerings unto her: ye will surely accomplish your vows, and surely perform your vows.
@ Therefore hear ye the word of the LORD, all Judah that dwell in the land of Egypt; Behold, ##I have sworn##note:x[<<Gen 22:16; Heb 6:13>>] by my great name, saith the LORD, ##that my name##note:x[<<Ps 50:16>>] shall no more be named in the mouth of any man of Judah in all the land of Egypt, saying, The Lord GOD liveth.
@ Behold, I ##will watch##note:x[<<Jer 31:28>>] over them for evil, and not for good: and all the men of Judah that __are__ in the land of Egypt shall be consumed by the sword and by the famine, until there be an end of them.
@ Yet ##a small##note:x[<<Isa 10:19; 27:12–13; Jer 44:14>>] number that escape the sword shall return out of the land of Egypt into the land of Judah, and all the remnant of Judah, that are gone into the land of Egypt to sojourn there, shall know whose words shall stand, mine, or theirs.note:tn[__mine…__: Heb. from me, or, from them]

@ ¶ And this __shall be__ ##a sign##note:x[<<Matt 24:15–16>>] unto you, saith the LORD, that I will punish you in this place, that ye may know that ##my words##note:x[<<Prov 19:21>>] shall surely stand against you for evil:
@ Thus saith the LORD; Behold, I will give Pharaoh-hophra king of Egypt into the hand of his enemies, and into the hand of them that seek his life; ##as I##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:4–7>>] gave Zedekiah king of Judah into the hand of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon, his enemy, and that sought his life.

=== @45

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Message to Baruch

@ The word that Jeremiah the prophet spake unto ##Baruch##note:x[<<Jer 32:12>>] the son of Neriah, ##when##note:x[<<Jer 36:1>>] he had written these words in a book at the mouth of Jeremiah, ##in the##note:x[<<Jer 36:1>>] fourth year of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD, the God of Israel, unto thee, O Baruch;
@ Thou didst say, Woe is me now! for the LORD hath added grief to my sorrow; I fainted in my sighing, and I find no rest.

@ ¶ Thus shalt thou say unto him, The LORD saith thus; Behold, ##__that__ which##note:x[<<Jer 1:10; 18:7–10; 31:28>>] I have built will I break down, and that which I have planted I will pluck up, even this whole land.
@ And ##seekest##note:x[<<1 Tim 6:6–9; Heb 13:5>>] thou great things for thyself? seek __them__ not: for, behold, I will bring evil upon all flesh, saith the LORD: but ##thy life##note:x[<<Jer 39:18>>] will I give unto thee for a prey in all places whither thou goest.

=== @46

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Egypt

@ The word of the LORD which came to Jeremiah the prophet ##against##note:x[<<Jer 1:10>>] the Gentiles;
@ ##Against Egypt##note:x[<<Jer 25:19; 46:14>>], against the army of ##Pharaoh-necho##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:29>>] king of Egypt, which was by the river Euphrates in Carchemish, which Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon smote in the fourth year of Jehoiakim the son of Josiah king of Judah.
@ ##Order ye the buckler and shield, and draw near to battle.##note:x[<<Isa 21:5>>]
@ Harness the horses; and get up, ye horsemen, and stand forth with __your__ helmets; ##furbish##note:x[<<Ezek 21:9–11>>] the spears, __and__ put on the brigandines.
@ Wherefore have I seen them dismayed __and__ turned away back? and their mighty ones are beaten down, and are fled apace, and look not back: __for__ ##fear##note:x[<<Jer 6:25; 49:29>>] __was__ round about, saith the LORD.note:tn[__beaten…__: Heb. broken in pieces]note:tn[__fled…__: Heb. fled a flight]
@ Let not the swift flee away, nor the mighty man escape; they shall ##stumble##note:x[<<Jer 46:12; Dan 11:19>>], and fall toward the north by the river Euphrates.
@ Who __is__ this __that__ cometh up ##as a flood##note:x[<<Jer 47:2>>], whose waters are moved as the rivers?
@ Egypt riseth up like a flood, and __his__ waters are moved like the rivers; and he saith, I will go up, __and__ will cover the earth; I will destroy the city and the inhabitants thereof.
@ note:x[<<Ezek 27:10>>]note:x[<<Isa 66:19; Ezek 27:10>>]Come up, ye horses; and rage, ye chariots; and let the mighty men come forth; the Ethiopians and the Libyans, that handle the shield; and the Lydians, that handle __and__ bend the bow.note:tn[__the Ethiopians__: Heb. Cush]note:tn[__the Libyans__: Heb. Put]
@ For this __is__ ##the day##note:x[<<Joel 1:15>>] of the Lord GOD of hosts, a day of vengeance, that he may avenge him of his adversaries: and ##the sword##note:x[<<Deut 32:42>>] shall devour, and it shall be satiate and made drunk with their blood: for the Lord GOD of hosts hath a sacrifice in ##the north##note:x[<<Jer 46:2>>] country by the river Euphrates.
@ Go up into ##Gilead##note:x[<<Jer 8:22>>], and take balm, O virgin, the daughter of Egypt: ##in vain##note:x[<<Mic 1:9>>] shalt thou use many medicines; __for__ thou shalt not be cured.note:tn[__thou shalt…__: Heb. no cure shall be unto thee]

@ The nations have ##heard##note:x[<<Nah 3:8–10>>] of thy shame, and thy cry hath filled the land: for the mighty man hath stumbled against the mighty, __and__ they are fallen both together.

@ ¶ The word that the LORD spake to Jeremiah the prophet, how Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon should come __and__ smite the land of Egypt.
@ Declare ye in Egypt, and publish in ##Migdol##note:x[<<Jer 44:1>>], and publish in Noph and in Tahpanhes: say ye, Stand fast, and prepare thee; for ##the sword##note:x[<<Jer 46:10>>] shall devour round about thee.
@ Why are ##thy##note:x[<<Isa 66:15–16>>] valiant __men__ swept away? they stood not, because ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 68:2>>] did drive them.
@ He made many to fall, yea, ##one##note:x[<<Lev 26:36–37>>] fell upon another: and they said, Arise, and let us go again to our own people, and to the land of our nativity, from the oppressing sword.note:tn[__made…__: Heb. multiplied the faller]
@ They did cry there, ##Pharaoh##note:x[<<Isa 19:11–16>>] king of Egypt __is but__ a noise; he hath passed the time appointed.
@ __As__ I live, ##saith##note:x[<<Jer 48:15>>] the King, whose name __is__ the LORD of hosts, Surely as ##Tabor##note:x[<<Josh 19:22; Judg 4:6>>] __is__ among the mountains, and as Carmel by the sea, __so__ shall he come.
@ O ##thou##note:x[<<Jer 48:18>>] daughter dwelling in Egypt, ##furnish thyself to go into captivity##note:x[<<Isa 20:4>>]: for Noph shall be waste and desolate without an inhabitant.note:tn[__furnish…__: Heb. make thee instruments of captivity]
@ Egypt __is like__ ##a very##note:x[<<Hos 10:11>>] fair heifer, __but__ destruction cometh; ##it cometh##note:x[<<Jer 47:2>>] out of the north.
@ Also ##her hired##note:x[<<2 Kgs 7:6>>] men __are__ in the midst of her like fatted bullocks; for they also are turned back, __and__ are fled away together: ##they did##note:x[<<Jer 46:5>>] not stand, because ##the day##note:x[<<Obad 1:13>>] of their calamity was come upon them, __and__ the time of their visitation.note:tn[__fatted…__: Heb. bullocks of the stall]
@ The voice thereof shall go like a serpent; for they shall march with an army, and come against her with axes, as hewers of wood.
@ They shall cut down her forest, saith the LORD, though it cannot be searched; ##because##note:x[<<Judg 6:5; 7:12>>] they are more than the grasshoppers, and __are__ innumerable.
@ The daughter of Egypt shall be confounded; ##she shall##note:x[<<Jer 1:15>>] be delivered into the hand of the people of the north.
@ The LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, saith; Behold, I will punish the ##multitude##note:tn[or, nourisher: Heb. Amon] of No, and Pharaoh, and Egypt, ##with their##note:x[<<Zeph 2:11>>] gods, and their kings; even Pharaoh, and __all__ them that trust in him:
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 44:30; Ezek 32:11>>] deliver them into the hand of those that seek their lives, and into the hand of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon, and into the hand of his servants: and afterward it shall be inhabited, as in the days of old, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ But ##fear##note:x[<<Isa 43:1, 5; Jer 30:10–11>>] not thou, O my servant Jacob, and be not dismayed, O Israel: for, behold, I will save thee from afar off, and thy seed from the land of their captivity; and Jacob shall return, and be in rest and at ease, and none shall make __him__ afraid.
@ Fear thou not, O Jacob my servant, saith the LORD: for I __am__ with thee; for I will make a full end of all the nations whither I have driven thee: but I will not make a full end of thee, but ##correct##note:x[<<Jer 10:24; 1 Cor 11:32>>] thee in measure; yet ##will I##note:x[<<Rev 3:19>>] not leave thee wholly unpunished.note:tn[__not leave…__: or, not utterly cut thee off]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on the Philistines

[.parallel]
(<<Zephaniah 2:4–7>>)

@ The word of the LORD that came to Jeremiah the prophet against the Philistines, before that Pharaoh smote note:x[<<Gen 10:19; Jer 25:20>>]##Gaza##note:tn[Heb. Azzah].
@ Thus saith the LORD; Behold, waters rise up out of the north, and shall be an overflowing flood, and shall overflow the land, and all that is therein; the city, and them that dwell therein: then the men shall cry, and all the inhabitants of the land shall howl.note:tn[__all that…__: Heb. the fulness thereof]
@ At the noise of the stamping of the hoofs of his strong __horses__, at the rushing of his chariots, __and at__ the rumbling of his wheels, the fathers shall not look back to __their__ children for feebleness of hands;
@ note:x[<<Amos 9:7>>]Because of the day that cometh to spoil all the Philistines, __and__ to cut off from Tyrus and Zidon ##every##note:x[<<Isa 20:6>>] helper that remaineth: for the LORD will spoil the Philistines, ##the remnant##note:x[<<Amos 9:7>>] of the country of Caphtor.note:tn[__the country__: Heb. the isle]
@ ##Baldness##note:x[<<Jer 48:37>>] is come upon Gaza; Ashkelon is cut off __with__ ##the remnant##note:x[<<Jer 25:20>>] of their valley: ##how##note:x[<<Jer 48:37>>] long wilt thou cut thyself?
@ O ##thou sword##note:x[<<Jer 12:12; Ezek 21:3–5>>] of the LORD, how long __will it be__ ere thou be quiet? put up thyself into thy scabbard, rest, and be still.note:tn[__put…__: Heb. gather thyself]
@ How can it be quiet, seeing ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ezek 14:17>>] hath given it a charge against Ashkelon, and against the sea shore? there ##hath he##note:x[<<Mic 6:9>>] appointed it.note:tn[__How…__: Heb. How canst thou]

=== @48

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Moab

[.parallel]
(<<Isaiah 15:1–9>>)

@ Against Moab thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Woe unto ##Nebo##note:x[<<Num 32:3, 37–38; Jer 48:22–23>>]! for it is spoiled: Kiriathaim is confounded __and__ taken: ##Misgab##note:tn[or, the high place] is confounded and dismayed.
@ __There shall be__ no more praise of Moab: in Heshbon they have devised evil against it; come, and let us cut it off from __being__ a nation. Also thou shalt be cut down, O Madmen; the sword shall pursue thee.note:tn[__be cut…__: or, be brought to silence]note:tn[__pursue…__: Heb. go after thee]
@ A voice of crying __shall be__ from ##Horonaim##note:x[<<Isa 15:5; Jer 48:34>>], spoiling and great destruction.
@ Moab is destroyed; her little ones have caused a cry to be heard.
@ For in the going up of ##Luhith##note:x[<<Isa 15:5>>] continual weeping shall go up; for in the going down of Horonaim the enemies have heard a cry of destruction.note:tn[__continual…__: Heb. weeping with weeping]
@ Flee, save your lives, and ##be like##note:x[<<Jer 17:6>>] the heath in the wilderness.note:tn[__the heath__: or, a naked tree]

@ ¶ For ##because##note:x[<<Ps 52:7; Jer 9:23>>] thou hast trusted in thy works and in thy treasures, thou shalt also be taken: and ##Chemosh##note:x[<<Num 21:29>>] shall go forth into captivity __with__ his priests and his princes together.
@ And the spoiler shall come upon every city, and no city shall escape: the valley also shall perish, and the plain shall be destroyed, as the LORD hath spoken.
@ Give wings unto Moab, that it may flee and get away: for the cities thereof shall be desolate, without any to dwell therein.
@ ##Cursed##note:x[<<Judg 5:23; 1 Sam 15:3; 1 Kgs 20:42>>] __be__ he that doeth the work of the LORD ##deceitfully##note:tn[or, negligently], and cursed __be__ he that keepeth back his sword from blood.

@ ¶ note:x[<<Jer 48:29>>]Moab ##hath been##note:x[<<Zech 1:15>>] at ease from his youth, and ##he hath##note:x[<<Zeph 1:12>>] settled on his lees, and hath not been emptied from vessel to vessel, neither hath he gone into captivity: therefore his taste ##remained##note:tn[Heb. stood] in him, and his scent is not changed.
@ Therefore, behold, the days come, saith the LORD, that I will send unto him wanderers, that shall cause him to wander, and shall ##empty##note:x[<<Nah 2:2>>] his vessels, and break their bottles.
@ And Moab shall be ##ashamed##note:x[<<Isa 45:16>>] of Chemosh, as the house of Israel was ashamed of Beth-el their confidence.

@ ¶ How say ye, ##We##note:x[<<Ps 33:16>>] __are__ mighty and strong men for the war?
@ Moab is spoiled, and ##gone##note:x[<<Jer 50:27>>] up __out of__ her cities, and his chosen young men are gone down to the slaughter, ##saith##note:x[<<Jer 46:18; 51:57>>] the King, whose name __is__ the LORD of hosts.note:tn[__his…__: Heb. the choice of]
@ The calamity of Moab __is__ ##near##note:x[<<Isa 13:22>>] to come, and his affliction hasteth fast.
@ All ye that are about him, bemoan him; and all ye that know his name, say, ##How##note:x[<<Isa 9:4>>] is the strong staff broken, __and__ the beautiful rod!
@ Thou ##daughter##note:x[<<Isa 47:1>>] that dost inhabit ##Dibon##note:x[<<Num 21:30; Isa 15:2>>], come down from __thy__ glory, and sit in thirst; for the spoiler of Moab shall come upon thee, __and__ he shall destroy thy strong holds.
@ O ##inhabitant##note:tn[Heb. inhabitress] of ##Aroer##note:x[<<Deut 2:36>>], stand by the way, and espy; ##ask##note:x[<<1 Sam 4:16>>] him that fleeth, and her that escapeth, __and__ say, What is done?
@ Moab is confounded; for it is broken down: howl and cry; tell ye it in Arnon, that Moab is spoiled,
@ And judgment is come upon the plain country; upon Holon, and upon note:x[<<Isa 15:4>>]##Jahazah##note:x[<<Josh 13:18>>], and upon Mephaath,
@ And upon Dibon, and upon Nebo, and upon Beth-diblathaim,
@ note:x[<<Josh 13:17>>]And upon Kiriathaim, and upon Beth-gamul, and upon Beth-meon,
@ And upon ##Kerioth##note:x[<<Amos 2:2>>], and upon Bozrah, and upon all the cities of the land of Moab, far or near.
@ The ##horn##note:x[<<Ps 75:10>>] of Moab is cut off, ##and his##note:x[<<Ps 10:15>>] arm is broken, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ Make ##ye him##note:x[<<Isa 29:9>>] drunken: ##for he##note:x[<<Jer 48:42>>] magnified __himself__ against the LORD: Moab also shall ##wallow##note:x[<<Isa 19:14>>] in his vomit, and he also shall be in derision.
@ For ##was not##note:x[<<Mic 7:8–10>>] Israel a derision unto thee? ##was he found##note:x[<<Jer 2:26>>] among thieves? for since thou spakest of him, thou skippedst for joy.note:tn[__skippedst…__: or, movedst thyself]
@ O ye that dwell in Moab, ##leave##note:x[<<Judg 6:2>>] the cities, and dwell in the rock, and be ##like##note:x[<<Ps 55:6–7; Song 2:14>>] the dove __that__ maketh her nest in the sides of the hole’s mouth.
@ We have ##heard##note:x[<<Isa 16:6>>] the pride of Moab, (he is exceeding proud) his loftiness, and his arrogancy, and his pride, and the haughtiness of his heart.
@ I know his wrath, saith the LORD; but __it shall__ not __be__ so; his lies shall not so effect __it__.note:tn[__his lies…__: or, those on whom he stayeth (Heb. his bars) do not right]
@ Therefore ##will I howl##note:x[<<Isa 15:5>>] for Moab, and I will cry out for all Moab; __mine heart__ shall mourn for the men of ##Kir-heres##note:x[<<2 Kgs 3:25>>].
@ note:x[<<Isa 16:8–9>>]O vine of Sibmah, I will weep for thee with the weeping of ##Jazer##note:x[<<Num 21:32>>]: thy plants are gone over the sea, they reach __even__ to the sea of Jazer: the spoiler is fallen upon thy summer fruits and upon thy vintage.
@ And joy and gladness is taken from the plentiful field, and from the land of Moab; and I have caused wine to fail from the winepresses: none shall tread with shouting; __their__ shouting __shall be__ no shouting.
@ From ##the cry##note:x[<<Isa 15:4–6>>] of Heshbon __even__ unto Elealeh, __and even__ unto Jahaz, have they uttered their voice, from Zoar __even__ unto Horonaim, __as__ an heifer of three years old: for the waters also of ##Nimrim##note:x[<<Num 32:3>>] shall be ##desolate##note:tn[Heb. desolations].
@ Moreover I will cause to cease in Moab, saith the LORD, ##him that offereth##note:x[<<Isa 15:2; 16:12>>] in the high places, and him that burneth incense to his gods.
@ Therefore ##mine heart##note:x[<<Isa 16:11>>] shall sound for Moab like pipes, and mine heart shall sound like pipes for the men of Kir-heres: because ##the riches##note:x[<<Isa 15:7>>] __that__ he hath gotten are perished.
@ For ##every head##note:x[<<Isa 15:2–3; Jer 41:5; 47:5>>] __shall be__ bald, and every beard ##clipped##note:tn[Heb. diminished]: upon all the hands __shall be__ cuttings, and upon the loins sackcloth.
@ __There shall be__ lamentation generally upon all the housetops of Moab, and in the streets thereof: for I have ##broken##note:x[<<Jer 22:28>>] Moab like a vessel wherein __is__ no pleasure, saith the LORD.
@ They shall howl, __saying__, How is it broken down! how hath Moab turned the ##back##note:tn[Heb. neck] with shame! so shall Moab be a derision and a dismaying to all them about him.
@ For thus saith the LORD; Behold, ##he shall##note:x[<<Deut 28:49>>] fly as an eagle, and shall ##spread##note:x[<<Isa 8:8; Jer 49:22>>] his wings over Moab.
@ ##Kerioth##note:tn[or, The cities] is taken, and the strong holds are surprised, and the mighty men’s hearts in Moab at that day shall be ##as the heart##note:x[<<Isa 13:8; 21:3>>] of a woman in her pangs.
@ And Moab shall be destroyed ##from##note:x[<<Jer 48:2>>] __being__ a people, because he hath magnified __himself__ against the LORD.
@ ##Fear, and the pit, and the snare, __shall be__ upon thee, O inhabitant of Moab, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Isa 24:17–18; Lam 3:47>>]
@ He ##that fleeth##note:x[<<1 Kgs 19:17>>] from the fear shall fall into the pit; and he that getteth up out of the pit shall be taken in the snare: for I will bring upon it, __even__ upon Moab, ##the year##note:x[<<Jer 11:23>>] of their visitation, saith the LORD.
@ They that fled stood under the shadow of Heshbon because of the force: but ##a fire##note:x[<<Num 21:28>>] shall come forth out of Heshbon, and a flame from the midst of Sihon, and shall ##devour##note:x[<<Num 24:17>>] the corner of Moab, and the crown of the head of the tumultuous ones.note:tn[__tumultuous…__: Heb. children of noise]
@ ##Woe##note:x[<<Num 21:29>>] be unto thee, O Moab! the people of Chemosh perisheth: for thy sons are taken ##captives, and##note:tn[Heb. in captivity, etc] thy daughters captives.

@ ¶ ##Yet will I bring##note:x[<<Jer 49:6, 39>>] again the captivity of Moab in the latter days, saith the LORD. Thus far __is__ the judgment of Moab.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on the Ammonites

@ ##Concerning##note:tn[or, Against] the ##Ammonites##note:x[<<Deut 23:3; Ezek 25:2–10; Amos 1:13–15>>], thus saith the LORD; Hath Israel no sons? hath he no heir? why __then__ doth their king inherit Gad, and his people dwell in his cities?note:tn[__their king__: or, Melcom]
@ Therefore, behold, the days come, saith the LORD, ##that I will##note:x[<<Jer 4:19>>] cause an alarm of war to be heard in ##Rabbah##note:x[<<Deut 3:11; Ezek 21:20>>] of the Ammonites; and it shall be a desolate heap, and her daughters shall be burned with fire: then shall Israel be heir unto them that were his heirs, saith the LORD.
@ ##Howl##note:x[<<Isa 15:2>>], O Heshbon, for Ai is spoiled: cry, ye daughters of Rabbah, ##gird##note:x[<<Jer 48:37>>] you with sackcloth; lament, and run to and fro by the hedges; for their king ##shall go##note:x[<<Jer 48:7>>] into captivity, __and__ his priests and his princes together.note:tn[__their king__: or, Melcom]
@ Wherefore ##gloriest##note:x[<<Jer 9:23>>] thou in the valleys, thy flowing valley, O backsliding daughter? that ##trusted##note:x[<<1 Tim 6:17>>] in her treasures, __saying__, ##Who##note:x[<<Jer 21:13>>] shall come unto me?note:tn[__thy…__: or, thy valley floweth away]
@ Behold, I will bring a fear upon thee, saith the Lord GOD of hosts, from all those that be about thee; and ye shall be driven out every man right forth; and none shall gather up him that wandereth.
@ ##And afterward I will bring again the captivity of the children of Ammon, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Jer 48:47; 49:39>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Edom

[.parallel]
(<<Obadiah 1:1–14>>)

@ ¶ Concerning ##Edom##note:x[<<Gen 25:30; Jer 25:21; Amos 1:11–12>>], thus saith the LORD of hosts; __Is__ wisdom no more in Teman? is counsel perished from the prudent? is their wisdom vanished?
@ Flee ye, turn back, dwell deep, O inhabitants of ##Dedan##note:x[<<Jer 25:23>>]; ##for##note:x[<<Jer 46:21>>] I will bring the calamity of Esau upon him, the time __that__ I will visit him.note:tn[__turn…__: or, they are turned back]
@ If grapegatherers come to thee, would they not leave __some__ gleaning grapes? if thieves by night, they will destroy till they have enough.note:tn[__till…__: Heb. their sufficiency]
@ But I have made Esau bare, I have uncovered his secret places, and he shall not be able to hide himself: his seed is spoiled, and his brethren, and his neighbours, and ##he __is__ not##note:x[<<Isa 17:14>>].
@ Leave ##thy fatherless##note:x[<<Ps 68:5; Hos 14:3; Jas 1:27>>] children, I will preserve __them__ alive; and let thy widows trust in me.
@ For thus saith the LORD; Behold, ##they whose##note:x[<<Jer 25:28–29>>] judgment __was__ not to drink of the cup have assuredly drunken; and __art__ thou he __that__ shall altogether go unpunished? thou shalt not go unpunished, but thou shalt surely drink __of it__.
@ For ##I have##note:x[<<Gen 22:16>>] sworn by myself, saith the LORD, that ##Bozrah##note:x[<<Gen 36:33; Isa 34:6>>] shall become a desolation, a reproach, a waste, and a curse; and all the cities thereof shall be perpetual wastes.
@ I have heard a rumour from the LORD, and an ambassador is sent unto the heathen, __saying__, Gather ye together, and come against her, and rise up to the battle.
@ For, lo, I will make thee small among the heathen, __and__ despised among men.
@ Thy ##terribleness##note:x[<<Isa 49:25>>] hath deceived thee, __and__ the pride of thine heart, O thou that dwellest in the clefts of the rock, that holdest the height of the hill: ##though##note:x[<<Job 39:27; Amos 9:2>>] thou shouldest make thy nest as high as the eagle, I will bring thee down from thence, saith the LORD.
@ Also ##Edom##note:x[<<Jer 49:13; Ezek 35:7>>] shall be a desolation: ##every##note:x[<<Jer 50:13>>] one that goeth by it shall be astonished, and shall hiss at all the plagues thereof.
@ As ##in the##note:x[<<Deut 29:23>>] overthrow of Sodom and Gomorrah and the neighbour __cities__ thereof, saith the LORD, ##no man##note:x[<<Jer 49:33>>] shall abide there, neither shall a son of man dwell in it.
@ Behold, he shall come up like a lion from ##the swelling##note:x[<<Jer 12:5>>] of Jordan against the habitation of the strong: but I will suddenly make him run away from her: and who __is__ a chosen __man, that__ I may appoint over her? for who __is__ like me? and who will appoint me the time? and who __is__ ##that shepherd##note:x[<<Jer 30:21>>] that will stand before me?note:tn[__appoint me…__: or, convent me in judgment?]
@ Therefore hear ##the counsel##note:x[<<Jer 50:45>>] of the LORD, that he hath taken against Edom; and his purposes, that he hath purposed against the inhabitants of Teman: ##Surely##note:x[<<Jer 50:45>>] the least of the flock shall draw them out: surely he shall ##make##note:x[<<Mal 1:3–4>>] their habitations desolate with them.
@ The ##earth##note:x[<<Jer 50:46>>] is moved at the noise of their fall, at the cry the noise thereof was heard in the Red sea.note:tn[__Red sea__: Heb. Weedy sea]
@ Behold, ##he shall##note:x[<<Jer 48:40–41; Hos 8:1>>] come up and fly as the eagle, and spread his wings over Bozrah: and at that day shall ##the heart of the##note:x[<<Isa 13:8>>] mighty men of Edom be as the heart of a woman in her pangs.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Damascus

[.parallel]
(<<Isaiah 17:1–14>>)

@ ¶ Concerning ##Damascus##note:x[<<Gen 14:15>>]. ##Hamath##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:34; Isa 10:9>>] is confounded, and Arpad: for they have heard evil tidings: they are ##fainthearted##note:tn[Heb. melted]; __there is__ sorrow on the sea; it cannot be quiet.note:tn[__on…__: or, as on the sea]
@ Damascus is waxed feeble, __and__ turneth herself to flee, and fear hath seized on __her:__ anguish and sorrows have taken her, as a woman in travail.
@ ##How is the city of praise not left, the city of my joy!##note:x[<<Jer 33:9>>]
@ ##Therefore her young men shall fall in her streets, and all the men of war shall be cut off in that day, saith the LORD of hosts.##note:x[<<Jer 50:30>>]
@ And I will kindle a fire in the wall of Damascus, and it shall consume the palaces of Ben-hadad.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Kedar and Hazor

@ ¶ Concerning ##Kedar##note:x[<<Gen 25:13>>], and concerning the kingdoms of Hazor, which Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon shall smite, thus saith the LORD; Arise ye, go up to Kedar, and ##spoil##note:x[<<Judg 6:3; Isa 11:14>>] the men of the east.
@ Their tents and their flocks shall they take away: they shall take to themselves their curtains, and all their vessels, and their camels; and they shall cry unto them, ##Fear##note:x[<<Jer 6:25; 46:5>>] __is__ on every side.

@ ¶ Flee, get you far off, dwell deep, O ye inhabitants of Hazor, saith the LORD; for Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon hath taken counsel against you, and hath conceived a purpose against you.note:tn[__get…__: Heb. flit greatly]
@ Arise, get you up unto the ##wealthy##note:tn[or, that is at ease] nation, ##that##note:x[<<Ezek 38:11>>] dwelleth without care, saith the LORD, which have neither gates nor bars, __which__ dwell alone.
@ And their camels shall be a booty, and the multitude of their cattle a spoil: and I will scatter into all winds them __that are__ ##in the utmost corners##note:x[<<Jer 9:26>>]; and I will bring their calamity from all sides thereof, saith the LORD.note:tn[__in…__: Heb. cut off into corners, or, that have the corners of their hair polled]
@ And Hazor shall be ##a dwelling##note:x[<<Jer 9:11; 10:22; 51:37>>] for dragons, __and__ a desolation for ever: there shall no man abide there, nor __any__ son of man dwell in it.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Elam

@ ¶ The word of the LORD that came to Jeremiah the prophet against ##Elam##note:x[<<Gen 10:22; 14:1; Jer 25:25>>] in the beginning of the reign of Zedekiah king of Judah, saying,
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts; Behold, I will ##break##note:x[<<Isa 22:6>>] the bow of Elam, the chief of their might.
@ And upon Elam will I bring the four winds from the four quarters of heaven, and will ##scatter##note:x[<<Jer 49:32>>] them toward all those winds; and there shall be no nation whither the outcasts of Elam shall not come.
@ For I will cause Elam to be dismayed before their enemies, and before them that seek their life: and I will bring evil upon them, __even__ my fierce anger, saith the LORD; and ##I will send the sword##note:x[<<Jer 9:16>>] after them, till I have consumed them:
@ And I will set my throne in Elam, and will destroy from thence the king and the princes, saith the LORD.

@ ¶ But it shall come to pass ##in the##note:x[<<Jer 48:47>>] latter days, __that__ I will bring again the captivity of Elam, saith the LORD.

=== @50

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Prophecy against Babylon

@ The word that the LORD spake ##against Babylon##note:x[<<Jer 25:26–27>>] __and__ against the land of the Chaldeans by Jeremiah the prophet.note:tn[__by…__: Heb. by the hand of Jeremiah]
@ Declare ye among the nations, and publish, and set up a standard; publish, __and__ conceal not: say, ##Babylon##note:x[<<Isa 21:9>>] is taken, ##Bel##note:x[<<Isa 46:1; Jer 51:44>>] is confounded, Merodach is broken in pieces; her idols are confounded, her images are broken in pieces.note:tn[__set up__: Heb. lift up]
@ For ##out of the##note:x[<<Jer 51:11>>] north there cometh up a nation against her, which shall make her land desolate, and none shall dwell therein: they shall remove, they shall depart, ##both##note:x[<<Zeph 1:3>>] man and beast.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Hope for Israel and Judah

@ ¶ In those days, and in that time, saith the LORD, ##the children of Israel##note:x[<<Jer 31:31; Hos 1:11>>] shall come, they and the children of Judah together, going and weeping: they shall go, and ##seek the##note:x[<<Hos 3:5>>] LORD their God.
@ They shall ##ask##note:x[<<Isa 35:8>>] the way to Zion with their faces thitherward, __saying__, Come, and let us join ourselves to the LORD ##in a##note:x[<<Jer 32:40>>] perpetual covenant ##__that__ shall##note:x[<<Jer 32:40>>] not be forgotten.
@ My ##people##note:x[<<Isa 53:6; Matt 9:36; 10:6>>] hath been lost sheep: their shepherds have caused them to go astray, they have turned them away __on__ the mountains: they have gone from mountain to hill, they have forgotten their ##restingplace##note:tn[Heb. place to lie down in].
@ All that found them have devoured them: and their adversaries said, ##We offend##note:x[<<Jer 2:3; 40:2–3>>] not, because they have sinned against the LORD, the habitation of justice, even the LORD, ##the hope##note:x[<<Jer 14:8>>] of their fathers.
@ Remove ##out of the midst##note:x[<<Isa 48:20; Jer 51:6; Rev 18:4>>] of Babylon, and go forth out of the land of the Chaldeans, and be as the he goats before the flocks.

@ ¶ For, lo, I will raise and cause to come up against Babylon an assembly of great nations from the north country: and they shall set themselves in array against her; from thence she shall be taken: their arrows __shall be__ as of a mighty expert man; none shall return in vain.note:tn[__expert…__: or, destroyer]
@ And Chaldea shall be a spoil: all that spoil her shall be satisfied, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Babylon’s Fall Is Certain

@ Because ye were glad, because ye rejoiced, O ye destroyers of mine heritage, because ye are grown ##fat##note:tn[Heb. big, or, corpulent] as the heifer at grass, and bellow as bulls;note:tn[__bellow…__: or, neigh as steeds]
@ Your mother shall be sore confounded; she that bare you shall be ashamed: behold, the hindermost of the nations __shall be__ ##a wilderness##note:x[<<Jer 51:43>>], a dry land, and a desert.
@ Because of the wrath of the LORD it shall not be inhabited, but it shall be wholly desolate: ##every##note:x[<<Jer 18:16; 49:17>>] one that goeth by Babylon shall be astonished, and hiss at all her plagues.
@ Put yourselves in array against Babylon round about: all ye that ##bend##note:x[<<Jer 50:29>>] the bow, shoot at her, spare no arrows: ##for she##note:x[<<Jer 50:29; Hab 2:17>>] hath sinned against the LORD.
@ Shout against her round about: she hath given her hand: ##her foundations##note:x[<<Jer 51:58>>] are fallen, her walls are thrown down: ##for it##note:x[<<Jer 46:10; 51:6>>] __is__ the vengeance of the LORD: take vengeance upon her; as she hath done, do unto her.
@ Cut off the sower from Babylon, and him that handleth the ##sickle##note:tn[or, scythe] in the time of harvest: for fear of the oppressing sword ##they shall turn every one##note:x[<<Isa 13:14; Jer 46:16; 51:9>>] to his people, and they shall flee every one to his own land.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Redemption for God’s People

@ ¶ Israel __is__ a scattered sheep; ##the lions##note:x[<<Jer 2:15>>] have driven __him__ away: first the king of Assyria hath devoured him; and last this Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon hath broken his bones.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; Behold, I will punish the king of Babylon and his land, as I have punished the king of Assyria.
@ And I will bring Israel again to his habitation, and he shall feed on Carmel and Bashan, and his soul shall be satisfied upon mount Ephraim and Gilead.
@ In those days, and in that time, saith the LORD, ##the iniquity##note:x[<<Jer 31:34; Mic 7:19>>] of Israel shall be sought for, and __there shall be__ none; and the sins of Judah, and they shall not be found: for ##I will pardon##note:x[<<Mic 7:19>>] them whom I reserve.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Destruction of Babylon

@ ¶ Go up against the land of ##Merathaim##note:x[<<Ezek 23:23>>], __even__ against it, and against the inhabitants of ##Pekod##note:tn[or, Visitation]: waste and utterly destroy after them, saith the LORD, and do according to all that I have commanded thee.note:tn[__of Merathaim__: or, of the rebels]
@ ##A sound of battle __is__ in the land, and of great destruction.##note:x[<<Jer 4:19–21>>]
@ ##How is the hammer of the whole earth cut asunder and broken! how is Babylon become a desolation among the nations!##note:x[<<Jer 51:20–24>>]
@ I have laid a snare for thee, and thou art also taken, O Babylon, ##and thou wast##note:x[<<Dan 5:30–31>>] not aware: thou art found, and also caught, ##because##note:x[<<Job 9:4>>] thou hast striven against the LORD.
@ The LORD hath ##opened##note:x[<<Jer 51:20>>] his armoury, and hath brought forth the weapons of his indignation: for ##this##note:x[<<Jer 51:25, 55>>] __is__ the work of the Lord GOD of hosts in the land of the Chaldeans.
@ Come against her from the utmost border, open her storehouses: cast her up as heaps, and ##destroy##note:x[<<Isa 14:23>>] her utterly: let nothing of her be left.note:tn[__from…__: Heb. from the end]note:tn[__cast…__: or, tread her]
@ Slay all her ##bullocks##note:x[<<Isa 34:7; Jer 46:21>>]; let them go down to the slaughter: woe unto them! for their day is come, the time of their visitation.
@ The ##voice##note:x[<<Isa 48:20>>] of them that flee and escape out of the land of Babylon, ##to declare##note:x[<<Jer 50:15>>] in Zion the vengeance of the LORD our God, the ##vengeance of his##note:x[<<Lam 1:10>>] temple.
@ Call together ##the archers##note:x[<<Jer 50:14>>] against Babylon: all ye that bend the bow, camp against it round about; let none thereof escape: ##recompense##note:x[<<Jer 51:56>>] her according to her work; according to all that she hath done, do unto her: ##for she hath##note:x[<<Isa 47:10>>] been proud against the LORD, against the Holy One of Israel.
@ Therefore shall ##her young##note:x[<<Jer 49:26>>] men fall in the streets, and all her men of war shall be cut off in that day, saith the LORD.
@ Behold, ##I __am__##note:x[<<Jer 21:13>>] against thee, ##__O thou__##note:x[<<Jer 50:29>>] most proud, saith the Lord GOD of hosts: for thy day is come, the time __that__ I will visit thee.note:tn[__most…__: Heb. pride]
@ And ##the most proud##note:x[<<Isa 10:12–15>>] shall stumble and fall, and none shall raise him up: and I will ##kindle##note:x[<<Jer 21:14; 49:27>>] a fire in his cities, and it shall devour all round about him.note:tn[__the…__: Heb. pride]

@ ¶ Thus saith the LORD of hosts; The children of Israel and the children of Judah __were__ oppressed together: ##and all##note:x[<<Isa 14:17>>] that took them captives held them fast; ##they refused##note:x[<<Exod 9:2–3; Isa 14:17>>] to let them go.
@ Their ##Redeemer##note:x[<<Isa 41:14; 47:4; 54:5>>] __is__ strong; the LORD of hosts __is__ his name: he shall throughly plead their cause, that he may give rest to the land, and disquiet the inhabitants of Babylon.

@ ¶ A ##sword##note:x[<<Jer 47:6>>] __is__ upon the Chaldeans, saith the LORD, and upon the inhabitants of Babylon, and upon her princes, and upon her wise __men__.
@ A sword __is__ upon the ##liars##note:tn[or, chief stays: Heb. bars]; and they shall dote: a sword __is__ upon ##her mighty##note:x[<<Jer 49:22>>] men; and they shall be dismayed.
@ A sword __is__ upon ##their horses##note:x[<<Jer 51:21>>], and upon their chariots, and upon all the mingled people that __are__ in the midst of her; and they shall become ##as women##note:x[<<Jer 51:30; Nah 3:13>>]: a sword __is__ upon her treasures; and they shall be robbed.
@ ##A drought##note:x[<<Isa 44:27; Rev 16:12>>] __is__ upon her waters; and they shall be dried up: for it __is__ ##the land##note:x[<<Jer 50:2>>] of graven images, and they are mad upon __their__ idols.
@ ##Therefore the wild beasts of the desert with the wild beasts of the islands shall dwell __there__, and the owls shall dwell therein: and it shall be no more inhabited for ever; neither shall it be dwelt in from generation to generation.##note:x[<<Isa 13:20–22; Rev 18:2>>]
@ ##As God overthrew Sodom and Gomorrah and the neighbour __cities__ thereof, saith the LORD; __so__ shall no man abide there, neither shall any son of man dwell therein.##note:x[<<Gen 19:24–25>>]
@ ##Behold, a people shall come from the north, and a great nation, and many kings shall be raised up from the coasts of the earth.##note:x[<<Jer 51:27–28>>]
@ They shall hold the bow and the lance: they __are__ cruel, and will not shew mercy: ##their voice##note:x[<<Isa 5:30>>] shall roar like the sea, and they shall ride upon horses, __every one__ put in array, like a man to the battle, against thee, O daughter of Babylon.
@ The king of Babylon hath heard the report of them, and his hands waxed feeble: anguish took hold of him, __and__ pangs as of a woman in travail.
@ Behold, he shall come up ##like a lion##note:x[<<Jer 49:19–21>>] from the swelling of Jordan unto the habitation of the strong: but I will make them suddenly run away from her: and who __is__ a chosen __man, that__ I may appoint over her? for who __is__ like me? and who will appoint me the time? and ##who __is__ that##note:x[<<Jer 49:19>>] shepherd that will stand before me?note:tn[__appoint me…__: or, convent me to plead?]
@ Therefore hear ye the counsel of the LORD, that he hath taken against Babylon; and his purposes, that he hath purposed against the land of the Chaldeans: Surely ##the least##note:x[<<Jer 49:20>>] of the flock shall draw them out: surely he shall make __their__ habitation desolate with them.
@ ##At the noise of the taking of Babylon the earth is moved, and the cry is heard among the nations.##note:x[<<Jer 49:21>>]

=== @51

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Babylon

@ Thus saith the LORD; Behold, I will raise up against Babylon, and against them that dwell in the ##midst##note:tn[Heb. heart] of them that rise up against me, a destroying wind;
@ And will send unto Babylon ##fanners##note:x[<<Isa 41:16; Jer 15:7; Matt 3:12>>], that shall fan her, and shall empty her land: for in the day of trouble they shall be against her round about.
@ Against __him that__ bendeth ##let the##note:x[<<Jer 50:14>>] archer bend his bow, and against __him that__ lifteth himself up in his ##brigandine##note:x[<<Jer 46:4>>]: and spare ye not her young men; destroy ye utterly all her host.
@ Thus the slain shall fall in the land of the Chaldeans, and __they that are__ ##thrust##note:x[<<Isa 13:15; Jer 49:26; 50:30>>] through in her streets.
@ For ##Israel##note:x[<<Rom 11:1–2>>] __hath__ not __been__ forsaken, nor Judah of his God, of the LORD of hosts; though their land was filled with sin against the Holy One of Israel.
@ ##Flee##note:x[<<Jer 50:8, 28>>] out of the midst of Babylon, and deliver every man his soul: be not cut off in her iniquity; ##for this##note:x[<<Jer 50:8>>] __is__ the time of the LORD’s vengeance; he will render unto her a recompence.
@ Babylon __hath been__ ##a golden##note:x[<<Rev 17:4>>] cup in the LORD’s hand, that made all the earth drunken: ##the nations##note:x[<<Rev 14:8; 17:2>>] have drunken of her wine; therefore the nations are mad.
@ Babylon is ##suddenly##note:x[<<Isa 21:9; Rev 14:8>>] fallen and destroyed: howl for her; ##take balm##note:x[<<Jer 46:11>>] for her pain, if so be she may be healed.
@ We would have healed Babylon, but she is not healed: ##forsake##note:x[<<Isa 13:14; Jer 50:16>>] her, and let us go every one into his own country: for ##her judgment##note:x[<<Rev 18:5>>] reacheth unto heaven, and is lifted up __even__ to the skies.
@ The LORD hath ##brought##note:x[<<Ps 37:6>>] forth our righteousness: come, and ##let us##note:x[<<Isa 51:11; Jer 50:28>>] declare in Zion the work of the LORD our God.
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Jer 46:4>>] ##bright##note:tn[Heb. pure] the arrows; gather the shields: the LORD hath raised up the spirit of the kings of the Medes: for ##his device##note:x[<<Jer 50:45>>] __is__ against Babylon, to destroy it; because it __is__ the vengeance of the LORD, the vengeance of his temple.
@ Set up ##the standard##note:x[<<Isa 13:2>>] upon the walls of Babylon, make the watch strong, set up the watchmen, prepare the ##ambushes##note:tn[Heb. liers in wait]: for ##the LORD hath both##note:x[<<Jer 51:11>>] devised and done that which he spake against the inhabitants of Babylon.
@ O thou that ##dwellest##note:x[<<Rev 17:1, 15>>] upon many waters, ##abundant##note:x[<<Isa 45:3>>] in treasures, thine end is come, __and__ the measure of thy covetousness.
@ The LORD of hosts hath sworn by himself, __saying__, Surely I will fill thee with men, as with caterpillers; and they shall lift up a shout against thee.note:tn[__by himself__: Heb. by his soul]note:tn[__lift up__: Heb. utter]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Praise to the God of Jacob

[.parallel]
(<<Isaiah 25:1–12>>)

@ He ##hath made##note:x[<<Jer 10:12–16; 32:17; Rev 4:11>>] the earth by his power, he hath established the world by his wisdom, and hath stretched out the heaven by his understanding.
@ When he uttereth __his__ voice, ##__there is__##note:x[<<Ps 135:7>>] a ##multitude##note:tn[or, noise] of waters in the heavens; ##and he causeth##note:x[<<Ps 135:7>>] the vapours to ascend from the ends of the earth: he maketh lightnings with rain, and ##bringeth##note:x[<<Ps 135:7>>] forth the wind out of his treasures.
@ ##Every##note:x[<<Jer 10:14>>] man is brutish by __his__ knowledge; every founder is confounded by the graven image: ##for his##note:x[<<Jer 10:14; Hab 2:18–19>>] molten image __is__ falsehood, and __there is__ no breath in them.note:tn[__is brutish…__: or, is more brutish than to know]
@ They __are__ vanity, the work of errors: in the time of their visitation they shall perish.
@ The ##portion##note:x[<<Ps 73:26; Jer 10:16>>] of Jacob __is__ not like them; for he __is__ the former of all things: and __Israel is__ the rod of his inheritance: ##the LORD##note:x[<<Jer 10:16>>] of hosts __is__ his name.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Babylon’s Punishment

@ Thou ##__art__##note:x[<<Isa 37:26; 41:15–16; Mic 4:13>>] my battle axe __and__ weapons of war: for ##with thee##note:tn[or, in thee, or, by thee] will I break in pieces the nations, and with thee will I destroy kingdoms;
@ ##And with thee will I break in pieces the horse and his rider; and with thee will I break in pieces the chariot and his rider;##note:x[<<Exod 15:1; Mic 5:10; Zech 10:5>>]
@ ##With thee also will I break in pieces man and woman; and with thee will I break in pieces old and young; and with thee will I break in pieces the young man and the maid;##note:x[<<1 Sam 15:3; 2 Chr 36:17; Ezek 9:6>>]
@ I will also break in pieces with thee the shepherd and his flock; and with thee will I break in pieces the husbandman and his yoke of oxen; and with thee will I break in pieces captains and rulers.
@ ##And I will render unto Babylon and to all the inhabitants of Chaldea all their evil that they have done in Zion in your sight, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Jer 50:15>>]
@ Behold, I __am__ against thee, ##O destroying##note:x[<<Zech 4:7>>] mountain, saith the LORD, ##which destroyest##note:x[<<Rev 8:8>>] all the earth: and I will stretch out mine hand upon thee, and roll thee down from the rocks, ##and will##note:x[<<Rev 8:8>>] make thee a burnt mountain.
@ And they ##shall not##note:x[<<Isa 13:19–22>>] take of thee a stone for a corner, nor a stone for foundations; but thou shalt be desolate for ever, saith the LORD.note:tn[__desolate…__: Heb. everlasting desolations]
@ note:x[<<1 Chr 1:6>>]Set ye up a standard in the land, blow the trumpet among the nations, prepare the nations against her, call together against her the kingdoms of ##Ararat##note:x[<<Gen 8:4>>], Minni, and ##Ashchenaz##note:x[<<Gen 10:3>>]; appoint a captain against her; cause the horses to come up as the rough caterpillers.
@ Prepare against her the nations with ##the kings##note:x[<<Jer 51:11>>] of the Medes, the captains thereof, and all the rulers thereof, and all the land of his dominion.
@ And ##the land##note:x[<<Jer 8:16>>] shall tremble and sorrow: for ##every##note:x[<<Jer 51:11–12, 43>>] purpose of the LORD shall be performed against Babylon, to make the land of Babylon a desolation without an inhabitant.
@ ##The mighty##note:x[<<Jer 50:36–37; Nah 3:13>>] men of Babylon have forborn to fight, they have remained in __their__ holds: their might hath failed; they became as women: they have burned her dwellingplaces; ##her bars##note:x[<<Nah 3:13>>] are broken.
@ One ##post##note:x[<<2 Sam 18:19–31; 2 Chr 30:6>>] shall run to meet another, and one messenger to meet another, to shew the king of Babylon that his city is taken at __one__ end,
@ And that the passages are stopped, and the reeds they have burned with fire, and the men of war are affrighted.
@ For thus saith the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel; The daughter of Babylon ##__is__ like##note:x[<<Isa 21:10>>] a threshingfloor, __it is__ time to thresh her: yet a little while, and ##the time##note:x[<<Hos 6:11; Joel 3:13>>] of her harvest shall come.note:tn[__it is…__: or, in the time that he thresheth her]
@ Nebuchadrezzar ##the king##note:x[<<Jer 50:17>>] of Babylon hath devoured me, he hath crushed me, ##he hath made##note:x[<<Isa 24:1–3>>] me an empty vessel, he hath ##swallowed##note:x[<<Jer 51:44>>] me up like a dragon, he hath filled his belly with my delicates, he hath cast me out.
@ The violence done to me and to my ##flesh##note:tn[or, remainder] __be__ upon Babylon, shall the ##inhabitant##note:tn[Heb. inhabitress] of Zion say; and my blood upon the inhabitants of Chaldea, shall Jerusalem say.note:tn[__The violence…__: Heb. My violence]
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD; Behold, ##I will plead##note:x[<<Ps 140:12>>] thy cause, and ##take##note:x[<<Rom 12:19>>] vengeance for thee; ##and I will##note:x[<<Jer 50:38>>] dry up her sea, and make her springs dry.
@ And Babylon shall ##become##note:x[<<Rev 18:2>>] heaps, a dwellingplace for dragons, an astonishment, and an hissing, without an inhabitant.
@ They shall roar together like lions: they shall ##yell##note:tn[or, shake themselves] as lions’ whelps.
@ In ##their heat##note:x[<<Jer 25:27>>] I will make their feasts, and I will make them drunken, that they may rejoice, ##and sleep##note:x[<<Jer 51:57>>] a perpetual sleep, and not wake, saith the LORD.
@ I will bring them down like lambs to the slaughter, like rams with he goats.
@ How is ##Sheshach##note:x[<<Jer 25:26>>] taken! and how is ##the praise##note:x[<<Isa 13:19>>] of the whole earth surprised! how is Babylon become an astonishment among the nations!
@ ##The sea is come up upon Babylon: she is covered with the multitude of the waves thereof.##note:x[<<Isa 8:7–8>>]
@ Her cities are a desolation, a dry land, and a wilderness, ##a land##note:x[<<Isa 13:20; Jer 2:6>>] wherein no man dwelleth, neither doth __any__ son of man pass thereby.
@ And ##I will punish##note:x[<<Jer 50:2>>] Bel in Babylon, and ##I will bring##note:x[<<Jer 51:34>>] forth out of his mouth that which he hath swallowed up: and the nations shall not flow together any more unto him: yea, ##the wall##note:x[<<Jer 51:58>>] of Babylon shall fall.
@ My people, ##go##note:x[<<Isa 48:20; Jer 51:6; Rev 18:4>>] ye out of the midst of her, and deliver ye every man his soul from the fierce anger of the LORD.
@ And note:x[<<2 Kgs 19:7>>]##lest##note:tn[or, let not] your heart faint, and ye fear for the rumour that shall be heard in the land; ##a rumour shall##note:x[<<Isa 13:3–5; 21:2–3>>] both come __one__ year, and after that in __another__ year __shall come__ a rumour, and violence in the land, ruler against ruler.
@ Therefore, behold, the days come, that ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 46:1–2; Jer 50:2; 51:52>>] do judgment upon the graven images of Babylon: and her whole land shall be confounded, and all her slain shall fall in the midst of her.note:tn[__do…__: Heb. visit upon]
@ Then ##the heaven##note:x[<<Isa 44:23; Rev 18:20>>] and the earth, and all that __is__ therein, shall sing for Babylon: for ##the spoilers##note:x[<<Jer 50:3>>] shall come unto her from the north, saith the LORD.
@ As Babylon ##__hath__##note:x[<<Jer 50:29; 51:24>>] __caused__ the slain of Israel to fall, so at Babylon shall fall the slain of all the earth.note:tn[__As…__: or, Both Babylon is to fall, O ye slain of Israel, and with Babylon, etc]note:tn[__the earth__: or, the country]
@ Ye that have ##escaped##note:x[<<Jer 44:28; 51:45>>] the sword, go away, stand not still: remember the LORD afar off, and let ##Jerusalem##note:x[<<Ps 122:6>>] come into your mind.
@ We are confounded, because we have heard reproach: shame hath covered our faces: ##for strangers##note:x[<<Lam 1:10>>] are come into the sanctuaries of the LORD’s house.
@ Wherefore, behold, the days come, saith the LORD, ##that I##note:x[<<Jer 51:47>>] will do judgment upon her graven images: and through all her land the wounded shall groan.
@ Though Babylon should ##mount##note:x[<<Gen 11:4; Jer 49:16>>] up to heaven, and though she should fortify the height of her strength, __yet__ from me shall spoilers come unto her, saith the LORD.
@ ##A sound of a cry __cometh__ from Babylon, and great destruction from the land of the Chaldeans:##note:x[<<Jer 50:22>>]
@ Because the LORD hath spoiled Babylon, and destroyed out of her the great voice; when her waves do roar like great waters, a noise of their voice is uttered:
@ Because ##the spoiler##note:x[<<Jer 51:48; Hab 2:8>>] is come upon her, __even__ upon Babylon, and her mighty men are taken, every one of their bows is broken: for ##the LORD##note:x[<<Jer 51:6>>] God of recompences shall surely requite.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 25:27>>] make drunk her princes, and her wise __men__, her captains, and her rulers, and her mighty men: and they shall sleep a perpetual sleep, and not wake, saith ##the King##note:x[<<Jer 46:18; 48:15>>], whose name __is__ the LORD of hosts.
@ Thus saith the LORD of hosts; ##The broad walls of Babylon##note:x[<<Jer 50:15; 51:44>>] shall be utterly ##broken##note:tn[or, made naked], and her high gates shall be burned with fire; and ##the people##note:x[<<Hab 2:13>>] shall labour in vain, and the folk in the fire, and they shall be weary.note:tn[__The broad…__: or, The walls of broad Babylon]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jeremiah’s Message to Seraiah

@ ¶ The word which Jeremiah the prophet commanded Seraiah the son of ##Neriah##note:x[<<Jer 32:12; 36:4>>], the son of Maaseiah, when he went ##with##note:tn[or, on the behalf of] Zedekiah the king of Judah into Babylon in the fourth year of his reign. And __this__ Seraiah __was__ a quiet prince.note:tn[__quiet…__: or, prince of Menucha, or, chief chamberlain]
@ So Jeremiah wrote in a book all the evil that should come upon Babylon, __even__ all these words that are written against Babylon.
@ And Jeremiah said to Seraiah, When thou comest to Babylon, and shalt see, and shalt read all these words;
@ Then shalt thou say, O LORD, thou hast spoken against this place, ##to cut##note:x[<<Jer 50:3, 13>>] it off, that none shall remain in it, neither man nor beast, but that it shall be ##desolate##note:tn[Heb. desolations] for ever.
@ And it shall be, when thou hast made an end of reading this book, __that__ ##thou shalt bind##note:x[<<Jer 19:10–11; Rev 18:21>>] a stone to it, and cast it into the midst of Euphrates:
@ And thou shalt say, Thus shall Babylon sink, and shall not rise from the evil that I will bring upon her: and ##they shall##note:x[<<Jer 51:58>>] be weary. ##Thus far##note:x[<<Job 31:40>>] __are__ the words of Jeremiah.

=== @52

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Fall of Jerusalem Recounted

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 74:1–23; Psalms 79:1–13; 2 Kings 24:18–20; 2 Chronicles 36:11–14>>)

@ Zedekiah __was__ ##one##note:x[<<2 Kgs 24:18>>] and twenty years old when he began to reign, and he reigned eleven years in Jerusalem. And his mother’s name __was__ Hamutal the daughter of Jeremiah of ##Libnah##note:x[<<Josh 10:29>>].note:tn[__began…__: Heb. reigned]
@ And he did __that which was__ evil in the eyes of the LORD, according to all that Jehoiakim had done.
@ For through the anger of the LORD it came to pass in Jerusalem and Judah, till he had cast them out from his presence, that ##Zedekiah##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:13>>] rebelled against the king of Babylon.

@ ¶ And it came to pass in ##the ninth year##note:x[<<Jer 39:1; Ezek 24:1–2>>] of his reign, ##in the tenth month##note:x[<<Zech 8:19>>], in the tenth __day__ of the month, __that__ Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon came, he and all his army, against Jerusalem, and pitched against it, and built forts against it round about.
@ So the city was besieged unto the eleventh year of king Zedekiah.
@ And in ##the fourth##note:x[<<Jer 39:2>>] month, in the ninth __day__ of the month, ##the famine##note:x[<<Isa 3:1>>] was sore in the city, so that there was no bread for the people of the land.
@ Then the city was broken up, and all the men of war fled, and went forth out of the city by night by the way of the gate between the two walls, which __was__ by the king’s garden; (now the Chaldeans __were__ by the city round about:) and they went by the way of the plain.

@ ¶ ##But the army of the Chaldeans pursued after the king, and overtook Zedekiah in the plains of Jericho; and all his army was scattered from him.##note:x[<<Jer 34:21>>]
@ Then they took the king, and carried him up unto the king of Babylon to ##Riblah##note:x[<<Jer 39:5>>] in the land of ##Hamath##note:x[<<Num 13:21>>]; where he gave judgment upon him.
@ And the king of Babylon ##slew##note:x[<<Jer 22:30>>] the sons of Zedekiah before his eyes: he slew also all the princes of Judah in Riblah.
@ Then he ##put out the eyes of Zedekiah##note:x[<<Ezek 12:13>>]; and the king of Babylon bound him in ##chains##note:tn[or, fetters], and carried him to Babylon, and put him in ##prison##note:tn[Heb. house of the wards] till the day of his death.note:tn[__put out__: Heb. blinded]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Temple Destroyed

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 25:8–17>>)

@ ¶ Now in the ##fifth##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:8; Zech 8:19>>] month, in the tenth __day__ of the month, which __was__ ##the nineteenth##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:8>>] year of Nebuchadrezzar king of Babylon, came Nebuzar-adan, captain of the guard, __which__ ##served##note:tn[Heb. stood before] the king of Babylon, into Jerusalem,note:tn[__captain…__: or, chief marshal: Heb. chief of the executioners, or, slaughtermen]
@ And ##burned##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:19; Ps 79:1; Mic 3:12>>] the house of the LORD, and the king’s house; and all the houses of Jerusalem, and all the houses of the great __men__, burned he with fire:
@ And all the army of the Chaldeans, that __were__ with the captain of the guard, ##brake##note:x[<<Neh 1:3>>] down all the walls of Jerusalem round about.
@ Then Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard carried away captive __certain__ of the poor of the people, and the residue of the people that remained in the city, and those that fell away, that fell to the king of Babylon, and the rest of the multitude.
@ But Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard left __certain__ of the poor of the land for vinedressers and for husbandmen.
@ Also the ##pillars##note:x[<<Jer 27:19–22>>] of brass that __were__ in the house of the LORD, and the bases, and the brasen sea that __was__ in the house of the LORD, the Chaldeans brake, and carried all the brass of them to Babylon.
@ The ##caldrons##note:x[<<Exod 27:3>>] also, and note:x[<<Num 4:14; 1 Kgs 7:45>>]the ##shovels##note:tn[or, instruments to remove the ashes], and the snuffers, and the ##bowls##note:tn[or, basons], and the spoons, and all the vessels of brass wherewith they ministered, took they away.
@ And the basons, and the ##firepans##note:tn[or, censers], and the bowls, and the caldrons, and the candlesticks, and the spoons, and the cups; __that__ which __was__ of gold __in__ gold, and __that__ which __was__ of silver __in__ silver, took the captain of the guard away.
@ The two pillars, one sea, and twelve brasen bulls that __were__ under the bases, which king Solomon had made in the house of the LORD: the brass of all these vessels was ##without##note:x[<<1 Kgs 7:47>>] weight.note:tn[__the brass__: Heb. their brass]
@ And ##__concerning__##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:17>>] the pillars, the height of one pillar __was__ eighteen cubits; and a ##fillet##note:tn[Heb. thread] of twelve cubits did compass it; and the thickness thereof __was__ four fingers: __it was__ hollow.
@ And a chapiter of brass __was__ upon it; and the height of one chapiter __was__ five cubits, with network and pomegranates upon the chapiters round about, all __of__ brass. The second pillar also and the pomegranates __were__ like unto these.
@ And there were ninety and six pomegranates on a side; __and__ ##all the##note:x[<<1 Kgs 7:20>>] pomegranates upon the network __were__ an hundred round about.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Captives Carried to Babylon

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 25:18–21>>)

@ ¶ And ##the captain##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:18>>] of the guard took ##Seraiah##note:x[<<1 Chr 6:14>>] the chief priest, and ##Zephaniah##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:18>>] the second priest, and the three keepers of the ##door##note:tn[Heb. threshold]:
@ note:x[<<Esth 1:14>>]He took also out of the city ##an eunuch##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:19>>], which had the charge of the men of war; and seven men of them that were near the king’s person, which were found in the city; and the principal scribe of the host, who mustered the people of the land; and threescore men of the people of the land, that were found in the midst of the city.note:tn[__were near…__: Heb. saw the face of the king]note:tn[__principal…__: or, scribe of the captain of the host]
@ So Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard took them, and brought them to the king of Babylon to Riblah.
@ And the king of Babylon smote them, and put them to death in Riblah in the land of Hamath. Thus Judah was carried away captive out of his own land.
@ This __is__ the people whom Nebuchadrezzar carried away captive: in the seventh year three thousand Jews and three and twenty:
@ In the eighteenth year of Nebuchadrezzar he carried away captive from Jerusalem eight hundred thirty and two ##persons##note:tn[Heb. souls]:
@ In the three and twentieth year of Nebuchadrezzar Nebuzar-adan the captain of the guard carried away captive of the Jews seven hundred forty and five persons: all the persons __were__ four thousand and six hundred.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Jehoiachin Released from Prison

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 25:27–30>>)

@ ¶ And ##it came##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:27–30>>] to pass in the seven and thirtieth year of the captivity of Jehoiachin king of Judah, in the twelfth month, in the five and twentieth __day__ of the month, __that__ Evil-merodach ##king of Babylon##note:x[<<Prov 21:1>>] in the __first__ year of his reign ##lifted up##note:x[<<Ps 3:3>>] the head of Jehoiachin king of Judah, and brought him forth out of prison,
@ And spake kindly unto him, and set his throne above the throne of the kings that __were__ with him in Babylon,note:tn[__kindly…__: Heb. good things with him]
@ And changed his prison garments: and ##he did##note:x[<<2 Sam 9:7>>] continually eat bread before him all the days of his life.
@ And __for__ his diet, ##there was##note:x[<<2 Sam 9:10; Matt 6:11>>] a continual diet given him of the king of Babylon, ##every day a portion##note:x[<<Luke 11:3>>] until the day of his death, all the days of his life.note:tn[__every…__: Heb. the matter of the day in his day]
